================================================================
PATH: app/composables/useLocalRepository.ts
================================================================

import { getAppDatabase, type LocalDailyEntryRecord, type LocalHabitLogRecord, type SyncMetaRecord } from '~/composables/useAppDatabase';
import { computeSmartBackupMerge } from '~/core/backup-merger';
import {
	buildCreatedHabitWithLifecycle,
	buildUpdatedHabitWithLifecycle,
	isDailyEntryEmpty,
	resolveHabitRuleSnapshot,
	serializeHabitSpecSnapshot
} from '~/core/habit-engine';
import { getTodayIso, isoToJalaliParts, isoToJalaliSlash, resolveCompletedRangeDates } from '~/domain/calendar';
import { buildDemoSeedDataset, DEMO_ENTRY_TAG, isDemoRecordId } from '~/domain/demo-seed';
import { parseTagsJson } from '~/domain/tags';
import type {
	BookEntity,
	BookStatus,
	CalendarEventEntity,
	CreateBookInput,
	CreateCalendarEventInput,
	CreateHabitInput,
	CreateMediaInput,
	DatabaseBackupPayload,
	HabitEntity,
	LifecycleEventEntity,
	LocalWorkspaceSnapshot,
	MediaEntity,
	MediaStatus,
	MutateHabitStructureInput,
	NoteEntity,
	SaveCadencePlanBatch,
	SaveDailyEntryInput,
	SyncOutboxMutation,
	UpdateBookInput,
	UpdateMediaInput,
	UpsertNoteInput
} from '~/domain/schemas';

const mutationStagedListeners = new Set<() => void>();

export function onLocalMutationStaged(listener: () => void): () => void {
	mutationStagedListeners.add(listener);
	return () => {
		mutationStagedListeners.delete(listener);
	};
}

/**
 * Optimistic local operations repository using Dexie.js.
 * Every write operation writes instantly to indexed Dexie tables
 * and stages an atomic mutation record into `syncOutbox`.
 */
export function useLocalRepository() {
	const db = useAppDatabase();

	async function stageMutation(
		mutation: SyncOutboxMutation,
		applyLocal: () => Promise<void>
	): Promise<void> {
		await db.transaction('rw', [
			db.syncOutbox,
			db.habits,
			db.dailyEntries,
			db.habitLogs,
			db.cadencePlans,
			db.books,
			db.media,
			db.notes,
			db.calendarEvents,
			db.lifecycleEvents,
			db.reflectionTags
		], async () => {
			await applyLocal();
			await db.syncOutbox.put(mutation);
		});

		for (const listener of mutationStagedListeners) {
			listener();
		}
	}

	const daily = {
		async saveDailyEntry(input: SaveDailyEntryInput): Promise<void> {
			const nowIso = input.updatedAtIso || new Date().toISOString();
			const outboxId = `daily:${input.dateIso}`;

			const isClearState = isDailyEntryEmpty(input);

			const mutation: SyncOutboxMutation = {
				id: outboxId,
				kind: 'SAVE_DAILY_ENTRY',
				queuedAtIso: nowIso,
				payload: {
					...input,
					updatedAtIso: nowIso
				}
			};

			await stageMutation(mutation, async () => {
				if (isClearState) {
					await db.dailyEntries.delete(input.dateIso);
					await db.habitLogs.where('dateIso').equals(input.dateIso).delete();
					return;
				}

				const entryRecord: LocalDailyEntryRecord = {
					dateIso: input.dateIso,
					dayStatus: input.dayStatus,
					reflectionNote: input.reflectionNote,
					journalText: input.journalText?.trim() || null,
					tags: [...input.tags],
					updatedAtIso: nowIso
				};
				await db.dailyEntries.put(entryRecord);

				const allHabits = await db.habits.toArray();
				const habitMap = new Map(allHabits.map((h) => [h.id, h]));

				for (const log of input.logs) {
					const habit = habitMap.get(log.habitId);
					const logId = `log:${input.dateIso}:${log.habitId}`;

					if (
						habit?.trackingMode === 'tiered' &&
						(log.achievedTier === null || log.achievedTier === undefined)
					) {
						await db.habitLogs.delete(logId);
						continue;
					}

					const logRecord: LocalHabitLogRecord = {
						id: logId,
						dateIso: input.dateIso,
						habitId: log.habitId,
						habitTitleSnapshot: log.habitTitleSnapshot || habit?.title || null,
						achievedTier: log.achievedTier,
						achievedRuleSnapshot:
							log.achievedRuleSnapshot ||
							(habit && log.achievedTier !== null ? resolveHabitRuleSnapshot(habit, log.achievedTier) : null),
						habitSnapshotJson: habit ? serializeHabitSpecSnapshot(habit) : null,
						completedIntervals: log.completedIntervals ?? null,
						actualMinutesPerInterval: log.actualMinutesPerInterval ?? null,
						totalActualMinutes: log.totalActualMinutes ?? null,
						plannedIntervalsSnapshot: log.plannedIntervalsSnapshot ?? null,
						plannedMinutesSnapshot: log.plannedMinutesSnapshot ?? null,
						updatedAtIso: log.updatedAtIso || nowIso
					};
					await db.habitLogs.put(logRecord);
				}
			});
		},

		async saveCadencePlans(input: SaveCadencePlanBatch): Promise<void> {
			const nowIso = new Date().toISOString();
			const mutation: SyncOutboxMutation = {
				id: `cadence:${input.targetDateIso}`,
				kind: 'SAVE_CADENCE_PLAN',
				queuedAtIso: nowIso,
				payload: input
			};

			await stageMutation(mutation, async () => {
				for (const item of input.items) {
					await db.cadencePlans.put({
						id: `plan:${input.targetDateIso}:${item.habitId}`,
						dateIso: input.targetDateIso,
						habitId: item.habitId,
						plannedIntervals: item.plannedIntervals,
						plannedMinutesPerInterval: item.plannedMinutesPerInterval,
						cadenceWindowMinutes: item.cadenceWindowMinutes,
						updatedAtIso: nowIso
					});

					const habit = await db.habits.get(item.habitId);
					if (
						habit &&
						(habit.defaultIntervals !== item.plannedIntervals ||
							habit.defaultMinutesPerInterval !== item.plannedMinutesPerInterval ||
							habit.cadenceWindowMinutes !== item.cadenceWindowMinutes)
					) {
						await db.habits.update(habit.id, {
							defaultIntervals: item.plannedIntervals,
							defaultMinutesPerInterval: item.plannedMinutesPerInterval,
							cadenceWindowMinutes: item.cadenceWindowMinutes,
							updatedAtIso: nowIso
						});

						const lifecycleEvent: LifecycleEventEntity = {
							id: crypto.randomUUID(),
							habitId: habit.id,
							habitTitleSnapshot: habit.title,
							eventType: 'CADENCE_UPDATED',
							effectiveDateIso: input.targetDateIso,
							summary: `تنظیم برای روز بعد: در هر ${item.cadenceWindowMinutes} دقیقه، ${item.plannedMinutesPerInterval} دقیقه (${item.plannedIntervals} بازه)`,
							reason: input.reason || 'تنظیم از شب قبل',
							previousStateJson: JSON.stringify({
								intervals: habit.defaultIntervals,
								minutes: habit.defaultMinutesPerInterval,
								window: habit.cadenceWindowMinutes
							}),
							newStateJson: JSON.stringify({
								intervals: item.plannedIntervals,
								minutes: item.plannedMinutesPerInterval,
								window: item.cadenceWindowMinutes
							}),
							createdAtIso: nowIso
						};
						await db.lifecycleEvents.put(lifecycleEvent);
						await db.syncOutbox.put({
							id: `lifecycle:${lifecycleEvent.id}`,
							kind: 'ADD_LIFECYCLE_EVENT',
							queuedAtIso: nowIso,
							payload: lifecycleEvent
						});
					}
				}
			});
		}
	};

	const habits = {
		async create(input: CreateHabitInput): Promise<HabitEntity> {
			const todayIso = getTodayIso();
			const nowIso = new Date().toISOString();
			const existingHabits = await db.habits.toArray();
			const maxOrder = existingHabits.reduce((max, h) => Math.max(max, h.sortOrder), 0);

			const { habit: newHabit, lifecycleEvent } = buildCreatedHabitWithLifecycle({
				input,
				maxSortOrder: maxOrder,
				todayIso,
				nowIso
			});

			await stageMutation(
				{
					id: `habit:${newHabit.id}`,
					kind: 'UPSERT_HABIT',
					queuedAtIso: nowIso,
					payload: newHabit
				},
				async () => {
					await db.habits.put(newHabit);
					await db.lifecycleEvents.put(lifecycleEvent);
					await db.syncOutbox.put({
						id: `lifecycle:${lifecycleEvent.id}`,
						kind: 'ADD_LIFECYCLE_EVENT',
						queuedAtIso: nowIso,
						payload: lifecycleEvent
					});
				}
			);

			return newHabit;
		},

		async update(input: MutateHabitStructureInput): Promise<void> {
			const current = await db.habits.get(input.habitId);
			if (!current) throw new Error('طرح مورد نظر یافت نشد');

			const todayIso = getTodayIso();
			const nowIso = new Date().toISOString();
			const parent = input.parentId ? await db.habits.get(input.parentId) : undefined;

			const { updatedHabit, lifecycleEvent } = buildUpdatedHabitWithLifecycle({
				current,
				input,
				parentTitle: parent?.title ?? null,
				todayIso,
				nowIso
			});

			await stageMutation(
				{
					id: `habit:${input.habitId}`,
					kind: 'UPSERT_HABIT',
					queuedAtIso: nowIso,
					payload: updatedHabit
				},
				async () => {
					await db.habits.put(updatedHabit);
					await db.lifecycleEvents.put(lifecycleEvent);
					await db.syncOutbox.put({
						id: `lifecycle:${lifecycleEvent.id}`,
						kind: 'ADD_LIFECYCLE_EVENT',
						queuedAtIso: nowIso,
						payload: lifecycleEvent
					});
				}
			);
		}
	};

	function buildTrackedItemMeta(params: {
		id?: string;
		title: string;
		startedJalali?: string;
		finishedJalali?: string;
		isCompleted: boolean;
		rating: number | null;
		notes?: string;
		existingCreatedAtIso?: string;
	}) {
		const { startedAtIso, finishedAtIso } = resolveCompletedRangeDates({
			startedJalali: params.startedJalali,
			finishedJalali: params.finishedJalali,
			isCompleted: params.isCompleted
		});
		const nowIso = new Date().toISOString();
		return {
			nowIso,
			base: {
				id: params.id || crypto.randomUUID(),
				title: params.title.trim(),
				startedAtIso,
				finishedAtIso,
				rating: params.rating,
				notes: params.notes?.trim() || null,
				createdAtIso: params.existingCreatedAtIso || nowIso,
				updatedAtIso: nowIso
			}
		};
	}

	const books = {
		async create(input: CreateBookInput): Promise<BookEntity> {
			const { nowIso, base } = buildTrackedItemMeta({
				title: input.title,
				startedJalali: input.startedJalali,
				finishedJalali: input.finishedJalali,
				isCompleted: input.status === 'completed',
				rating: input.rating,
				notes: input.notes
			});
			const newBook: BookEntity = {
				...base,
				author: input.author?.trim() || null,
				status: input.status
			};

			await stageMutation(
				{
					id: `book:${newBook.id}`,
					kind: 'UPSERT_BOOK',
					queuedAtIso: nowIso,
					payload: newBook
				},
				async () => {
					await db.books.put(newBook);
				}
			);

			return newBook;
		},

		async update(input: UpdateBookInput): Promise<void> {
			const existing = await db.books.get(input.bookId);
			const { nowIso, base } = buildTrackedItemMeta({
				id: input.bookId,
				title: input.title,
				startedJalali: input.startedJalali,
				finishedJalali: input.finishedJalali,
				isCompleted: input.status === 'completed',
				rating: input.rating,
				notes: input.notes,
				existingCreatedAtIso: existing?.createdAtIso
			});
			const updated: BookEntity = {
				...base,
				author: input.author?.trim() || null,
				status: input.status
			};

			await stageMutation(
				{
					id: `book:${input.bookId}`,
					kind: 'UPSERT_BOOK',
					queuedAtIso: nowIso,
					payload: updated
				},
				async () => {
					await db.books.put(updated);
				}
			);
		},

		async updateStatus(bookId: string, status: BookStatus): Promise<void> {
			const book = await db.books.get(bookId);
			if (!book) return;

			const nowIso = new Date().toISOString();
			const finishedAtIso = status === 'completed' ? getTodayIso() : null;
			const updated: BookEntity = {
				...book,
				status,
				finishedAtIso,
				updatedAtIso: nowIso
			};

			await stageMutation(
				{
					id: `book:${bookId}`,
					kind: 'UPSERT_BOOK',
					queuedAtIso: nowIso,
					payload: updated
				},
				async () => {
					await db.books.put(updated);
				}
			);
		},

		async delete(bookId: string): Promise<void> {
			const nowIso = new Date().toISOString();
			await stageMutation(
				{
					id: `book:${bookId}`,
					kind: 'DELETE_BOOK',
					queuedAtIso: nowIso,
					payload: { bookId }
				},
				async () => {
					await db.books.delete(bookId);
				}
			);
		}
	};

	const notes = {

	const calendar = {
		async create(input: CreateCalendarEventInput): Promise<CalendarEventEntity> {
			const parts = isoToJalaliParts(input.dateIso);
			const isAnnual = Boolean(input.isAnnual);
			const nowIso = new Date().toISOString();

			const newEvent: CalendarEventEntity = {
				id: crypto.randomUUID(),
				title: input.title.trim(),
				eventType: input.eventType,
				isAnnual,
				dateIso: isAnnual ? null : input.dateIso,
				jalaliMonth: parts.jm,
				jalaliDay: parts.jd,
				isCompleted: false,
				notes: input.notes?.trim() || null,
				createdAtIso: nowIso,
				updatedAtIso: nowIso
			};

			await stageMutation(
				{
					id: `event:${newEvent.id}`,
					kind: 'UPSERT_CALENDAR_EVENT',
					queuedAtIso: nowIso,
					payload: newEvent
				},
				async () => {
					await db.calendarEvents.put(newEvent);
				}
			);

			return newEvent;
		},

		async toggleComplete(eventId: string, isCompleted: boolean): Promise<void> {
			const ev = await db.calendarEvents.get(eventId);
			if (!ev) return;

			const nowIso = new Date().toISOString();

				...ev,
				isCompleted,
				updatedAtIso: nowIso
			};

			await stageMutation(
				{
					id: `event:${eventId}`,
					kind: 'UPSERT_CALENDAR_EVENT',
					queuedAtIso: nowIso,
					payload: updated
				},
				async () => {
					await db.calendarEvents.put(updated);
				}
			);
		},

		async delete(eventId: string): Promise<void> {

			await stageMutation(
				{
					id: `event:${eventId}`,

					await db.calendarEvents.delete(eventId);
				}
			);
		}
	};

	async function getSyncMeta(): Promise<SyncMetaRecord> {
		const fallback: SyncMetaRecord = {
			key: 'meta:sync',
			clientId: typeof localStorage !== 'undefined' ? (localStorage.getItem('newlife_client_id') ?? '') : '',
			knownServerVersionIso: null,
			lastSyncedAtIso: null
		};

		if (!fallback.clientId && typeof localStorage !== 'undefined') {
			fallback.clientId = crypto.randomUUID();
			try {
				localStorage.setItem('newlife_client_id', fallback.clientId);
			} catch {
				// ignore quota errors
			}
		}

		const meta = await db.syncMeta.get('meta:sync');
		return meta || fallback;
	}

	async function saveSyncMeta(meta: SyncMetaRecord): Promise<void> {
		await db.syncMeta.put(meta);
	}

	async function getPendingMutations(): Promise<SyncOutboxMutation[]> {
		return db.syncOutbox.orderBy('queuedAtIso').toArray();
	}

	async function removeAppliedMutations(applied: Array<{ id: string; queuedAtIso: string }>): Promise<void> {
		await db.transaction('rw', db.syncOutbox, async () => {
			for (const item of applied) {
				const existing = await db.syncOutbox.get(item.id);
				if (existing && existing.queuedAtIso <= item.queuedAtIso) {
					await db.syncOutbox.delete(item.id);
				}
			}
		});
	}

	async function applyServerWorkspace(ws: LocalWorkspaceSnapshot): Promise<void> {
		await db.transaction('rw', [
			db.habits,
			db.dailyEntries,
			db.habitLogs,
			db.cadencePlans,
			db.books,
			db.media,
			db.notes,
			db.calendarEvents,
			db.lifecycleEvents,
			db.reflectionTags,
			db.syncOutbox
		], async () => {
			const pending = await db.syncOutbox.toArray();
			const pendingIds = new Set(pending.map((m) => m.id));
			const pendingDailyDates = new Set(
				pending
					.filter((m) => m.kind === 'SAVE_DAILY_ENTRY')
					.map((m) => m.payload.dateIso)
			);

			// Reconcile remote deletions for collections where items can be hard-deleted
			const serverBookIds = new Set(ws.books.map((b) => b.id));
			const localBookIds = await db.books.toCollection().primaryKeys();
			const booksToDelete = localBookIds.filter(
				(id) => !serverBookIds.has(id) && !pendingIds.has(`book:${id}`)
			);
			if (booksToDelete.length > 0) await db.books.bulkDelete(booksToDelete);

			const serverMediaIds = new Set(ws.media.map((m) => m.id));
			const localMediaIds = await db.media.toCollection().primaryKeys();
			const mediaToDelete = localMediaIds.filter(
				(id) => !serverMediaIds.has(id) && !pendingIds.has(`media:${id}`)
			);
			if (mediaToDelete.length > 0) await db.media.bulkDelete(mediaToDelete);

			const serverNoteIds = new Set(ws.notes.map((n) => n.id));
			const localNoteIds = await db.notes.toCollection().primaryKeys();
			const notesToDelete = localNoteIds.filter(
				(id) => !serverNoteIds.has(id) && !pendingIds.has(`note:${id}`)
			);
			if (notesToDelete.length > 0) await db.notes.bulkDelete(notesToDelete);

			const serverEventIds = new Set(ws.calendarEvents.map((ev) => ev.id));
			const localEventIds = await db.calendarEvents.toCollection().primaryKeys();
			const eventsToDelete = localEventIds.filter(
				(id) => !serverEventIds.has(id) && !pendingIds.has(`event:${id}`)
			);
			if (eventsToDelete.length > 0) await db.calendarEvents.bulkDelete(eventsToDelete);

			// Upsert non-pending server entities
			await db.habits.bulkPut(ws.habits.filter((h) => !pendingIds.has(`habit:${h.id}`)));
			await db.cadencePlans.bulkPut(
				ws.cadencePlans.filter((c) => !pendingIds.has(`cadence:${c.dateIso}`))
			);
			await db.books.bulkPut(ws.books.filter((b) => !pendingIds.has(`book:${b.id}`)));
			await db.media.bulkPut(ws.media.filter((m) => !pendingIds.has(`media:${m.id}`)));
			await db.notes.bulkPut(ws.notes.filter((n) => !pendingIds.has(`note:${n.id}`)));
			await db.calendarEvents.bulkPut(
				ws.calendarEvents.filter((ev) => !pendingIds.has(`event:${ev.id}`))
			);
			await db.lifecycleEvents.bulkPut(ws.lifecycleEvents);

			if (ws.availableTags && ws.availableTags.length > 0) {
				const tagRecords = ws.availableTags.map((label, idx) => ({
					id: `tag-${idx + 1}`,
					label,
					sortOrder: idx + 1
				}));
				await db.reflectionTags.bulkPut(tagRecords);
			}

			// Reconcile cleared daily entries and logs
			const serverEntryDates = new Set(ws.entries.map((e) => e.dateIso));
			const localEntryDates = await db.dailyEntries.toCollection().primaryKeys();
			const entriesToDelete = localEntryDates.filter(
				(dateIso) => !serverEntryDates.has(dateIso) && !pendingDailyDates.has(dateIso)
			);
			if (entriesToDelete.length > 0) {
				await db.dailyEntries.bulkDelete(entriesToDelete);
			}

			const entriesToPut: LocalDailyEntryRecord[] = [];
			for (const e of ws.entries) {
				if (!pendingDailyDates.has(e.dateIso)) {
					entriesToPut.push({
						dateIso: e.dateIso,
						dayStatus: e.dayStatus,
						reflectionNote: e.reflectionNote,
						journalText: e.journalText ?? null,
						tags: [...e.tags],
						updatedAtIso: e.updatedAtIso
					});
				}
			}
			if (entriesToPut.length > 0) {
				await db.dailyEntries.bulkPut(entriesToPut);
			}

			const serverLogIds = new Set(ws.logs.map((l) => l.id || `log:${l.dateIso}:${l.habitId}`));
			const allLocalLogs = await db.habitLogs.toArray();
			const logsToDelete = allLocalLogs
				.filter((l) => !serverLogIds.has(l.id) && !pendingDailyDates.has(l.dateIso))
				.map((l) => l.id);
			if (logsToDelete.length > 0) {
				await db.habitLogs.bulkDelete(logsToDelete);
			}

			const logsToPut: LocalHabitLogRecord[] = [];
			for (const l of ws.logs) {
				if (!pendingDailyDates.has(l.dateIso)) {
					logsToPut.push({
						id: l.id || `log:${l.dateIso}:${l.habitId}`,
						dateIso: l.dateIso,
						habitId: l.habitId,
						habitTitleSnapshot: l.habitTitleSnapshot ?? null,
						achievedTier: l.achievedTier,
						achievedRuleSnapshot: l.achievedRuleSnapshot ?? null,
						habitSnapshotJson: l.habitSnapshotJson ?? null,
						completedIntervals: l.completedIntervals ?? null,
						actualMinutesPerInterval: l.actualMinutesPerInterval ?? null,
						totalActualMinutes: l.totalActualMinutes ?? null,
						plannedIntervalsSnapshot: l.plannedIntervalsSnapshot ?? null,
						plannedMinutesSnapshot: l.plannedMinutesSnapshot ?? null,
						updatedAtIso: l.updatedAtIso || new Date().toISOString()
					});
				}
			}
			if (logsToPut.length > 0) {
				await db.habitLogs.bulkPut(logsToPut);
			}
		});
	}

	async function clearLocalData(): Promise<void> {
		await db.transaction('rw', [
			db.habits,
			db.dailyEntries,
			db.habitLogs,
			db.cadencePlans,
			db.books,
			db.media,
			db.notes,
			db.calendarEvents,
			db.lifecycleEvents,
			db.reflectionTags,
			db.syncOutbox,
			db.syncMeta
		], async () => {
			await db.habits.clear();
			await db.dailyEntries.clear();
			await db.habitLogs.clear();
			await db.cadencePlans.clear();
			await db.books.clear();
			await db.media.clear();
			await db.notes.clear();
			await db.calendarEvents.clear();
			await db.lifecycleEvents.clear();
			await db.reflectionTags.clear();
			await db.syncOutbox.clear();
			await db.syncMeta.clear();
		});
	}

	async function exportBackupPayload(): Promise<DatabaseBackupPayload> {
		const todayIso = getTodayIso();
		const nowIso = new Date().toISOString();

		const [
			allHabits,
			allEntries,
			allLogs,
			allCadence,
			allBooks,
			allMedia,
			allNotes,
			allEvents,
			allLifecycle,
			allTags
		] = await Promise.all([
			db.habits.toArray(),
			db.dailyEntries.toArray(),
			db.habitLogs.toArray(),
			db.cadencePlans.toArray(),
			db.books.toArray(),
			db.media.toArray(),
			db.notes.toArray(),
			db.calendarEvents.toArray(),
			db.lifecycleEvents.toArray(),
			db.reflectionTags.toArray()
		]);

		return {
			version: 1,
			appName: 'new-life',
			exportedAtIso: nowIso,
			jalaliDate: isoToJalaliSlash(todayIso),
			data: {
				habits: allHabits,
				dailyEntries: allEntries.map((e) => ({
					...e,
					id: `entry:${e.dateIso}`,
					tagsJson: JSON.stringify(e.tags)
				})),
				habitLogs: allLogs.map((l) => ({
					...l,
					habitTitleSnapshot: l.habitTitleSnapshot ?? null,
					achievedRuleSnapshot: l.achievedRuleSnapshot ?? null,
					habitSnapshotJson: l.habitSnapshotJson ?? null,
					totalActualMinutes: l.totalActualMinutes ?? null,
					completedIntervals: l.completedIntervals ?? null,
					actualMinutesPerInterval: l.actualMinutesPerInterval ?? null,
					plannedIntervalsSnapshot: l.plannedIntervalsSnapshot ?? null,
					plannedMinutesSnapshot: l.plannedMinutesSnapshot ?? null,
					updatedAtIso: l.updatedAtIso || nowIso
				})),
				cadencePlans: allCadence,
				books: allBooks,
				media: allMedia,
				notes: allNotes,
				calendarEvents: allEvents,
				lifecycleEvents: allLifecycle,
				reflectionTags: allTags.map((t) => ({
					id: t.id,
					label: t.label,
					sortOrder: t.sortOrder
				}))
			}
		};
	}

	async function restoreBackupPayload(
		payload: DatabaseBackupPayload
	): Promise<{ addedOrUpdatedCount: number; skippedDuplicatesCount: number }> {
		const nowIso = new Date().toISOString();
		const currentSnapshot = await exportBackupPayload();
		const merged = computeSmartBackupMerge(currentSnapshot.data, payload.data);

		await db.transaction('rw', [
			db.habits,
			db.dailyEntries,
			db.habitLogs,
			db.cadencePlans,
			db.books,
			db.media,
			db.notes,
			db.calendarEvents,
			db.lifecycleEvents,
			db.reflectionTags
		], async () => {
			if (merged.habitsToUpsert.length > 0) {
				await db.habits.bulkPut(merged.habitsToUpsert);
			}
			if (merged.dailyEntriesToUpsert.length > 0) {
				await db.dailyEntries.bulkPut(
					merged.dailyEntriesToUpsert.map((e) => ({
						dateIso: e.dateIso,
						dayStatus: e.dayStatus,
						reflectionNote: e.reflectionNote,
						journalText: e.journalText ?? null,
						tags: parseTagsJson(e.tagsJson),
						updatedAtIso: e.updatedAtIso
					}))
				);
			}
			if (merged.habitLogsToUpsert.length > 0) {
				await db.habitLogs.bulkPut(
					merged.habitLogsToUpsert.map((l) => ({
						...l,
						updatedAtIso: l.updatedAtIso || nowIso
					}))
				);
			}
			if (merged.cadencePlansToUpsert.length > 0) {
				await db.cadencePlans.bulkPut(merged.cadencePlansToUpsert);
			}
			if (merged.booksToUpsert.length > 0) {
				await db.books.bulkPut(merged.booksToUpsert);
			}
			if (merged.mediaToUpsert.length > 0) {
				await db.media.bulkPut(merged.mediaToUpsert);
			}
			if (merged.notesToUpsert.length > 0) {
				await db.notes.bulkPut(merged.notesToUpsert);
			}
			if (merged.calendarEventsToUpsert.length > 0) {
				await db.calendarEvents.bulkPut(merged.calendarEventsToUpsert);
			}
			if (merged.lifecycleEventsToUpsert.length > 0) {
				await db.lifecycleEvents.bulkPut(merged.lifecycleEventsToUpsert);
			}
			if (merged.reflectionTagsToUpsert.length > 0) {
				await db.reflectionTags.bulkPut(merged.reflectionTagsToUpsert);
			}
		});

		return {
			addedOrUpdatedCount: merged.addedOrUpdatedCount,
			skippedDuplicatesCount: merged.skippedDuplicatesCount
		};
	}

	async function injectDemoSeed(): Promise<void> {
		const dataset = buildDemoSeedDataset();
		const { data } = dataset;

		await db.transaction('rw', [
			db.habits,
			db.dailyEntries,
			db.habitLogs,
			db.cadencePlans,
			db.books,
			db.media,
			db.notes,
			db.calendarEvents,
			db.lifecycleEvents
		], async () => {
			await db.habits.bulkPut(data.habits);
			await db.cadencePlans.bulkPut(data.cadencePlans);
			await db.books.bulkPut(data.books);
			await db.media.bulkPut(data.media);
			await db.notes.bulkPut(data.notes);
			await db.calendarEvents.bulkPut(data.calendarEvents);
			await db.lifecycleEvents.bulkPut(data.lifecycleEvents);

			for (const e of data.dailyEntries) {
				const existing = await db.dailyEntries.get(e.dateIso);
				if (!existing || existing.tags.includes(DEMO_ENTRY_TAG)) {
					await db.dailyEntries.put({
						dateIso: e.dateIso,
						dayStatus: e.dayStatus,
						reflectionNote: e.reflectionNote,
						journalText: e.journalText ?? null,
						tags: parseTagsJson(e.tagsJson),
						updatedAtIso: e.updatedAtIso
					});
				}
			}

			await db.habitLogs.bulkPut(
				data.habitLogs.map((l) => ({
					...l,
					updatedAtIso: l.updatedAtIso || dataset.exportedAtIso
				}))
			);
		});
	}

	async function removeDemoSeed(): Promise<void> {
		await db.transaction('rw', [
			db.habits,
			db.dailyEntries,
			db.habitLogs,
			db.cadencePlans,
			db.books,
			db.media,
			db.notes,
			db.calendarEvents,
			db.lifecycleEvents,
			db.syncOutbox
		], async () => {
			const demoHabitIds = (await db.habits.toArray())
				.filter((h) => isDemoRecordId(h.id))
				.map((h) => h.id);
			if (demoHabitIds.length > 0) await db.habits.bulkDelete(demoHabitIds);

			const demoLogIds = (await db.habitLogs.toArray())
				.filter((l) => isDemoRecordId(l.id) || isDemoRecordId(l.habitId))
				.map((l) => l.id);
			if (demoLogIds.length > 0) await db.habitLogs.bulkDelete(demoLogIds);

			const demoEntryDates = (await db.dailyEntries.toArray())
				.filter((e) => e.tags.includes(DEMO_ENTRY_TAG))
				.map((e) => e.dateIso);
			if (demoEntryDates.length > 0) await db.dailyEntries.bulkDelete(demoEntryDates);

			const demoPlanIds = (await db.cadencePlans.toArray())
				.filter((p) => isDemoRecordId(p.id) || isDemoRecordId(p.habitId))
				.map((p) => p.id);
			if (demoPlanIds.length > 0) await db.cadencePlans.bulkDelete(demoPlanIds);

			const demoBookIds = (await db.books.toArray())
				.filter((b) => isDemoRecordId(b.id))
				.map((b) => b.id);
			if (demoBookIds.length > 0) await db.books.bulkDelete(demoBookIds);

			const demoMediaIds = (await db.media.toArray())
				.filter((m) => isDemoRecordId(m.id))
				.map((m) => m.id);
			if (demoMediaIds.length > 0) await db.media.bulkDelete(demoMediaIds);

			const demoNoteIds = (await db.notes.toArray())
				.filter((n) => isDemoRecordId(n.id))
				.map((n) => n.id);
			if (demoNoteIds.length > 0) await db.notes.bulkDelete(demoNoteIds);

			const demoEventIds = (await db.calendarEvents.toArray())
				.filter((ev) => isDemoRecordId(ev.id))
				.map((ev) => ev.id);
			if (demoEventIds.length > 0) await db.calendarEvents.bulkDelete(demoEventIds);

			const demoLifecycleIds = (await db.lifecycleEvents.toArray())
				.filter((lc) => isDemoRecordId(lc.id) || isDemoRecordId(lc.habitId))
				.map((lc) => lc.id);
			if (demoLifecycleIds.length > 0) await db.lifecycleEvents.bulkDelete(demoLifecycleIds);

			const demoOutboxIds = (await db.syncOutbox.toArray())
				.filter((m) => m.id.includes('demo:'))
				.map((m) => m.id);
			if (demoOutboxIds.length > 0) await db.syncOutbox.bulkDelete(demoOutboxIds);
		});
	}

	return {
		daily,
		habits,
		books,
		media,
		notes,
		calendar,
		getSyncMeta,
		saveSyncMeta,
		getPendingMutations,
		removeAppliedMutations,
		applyServerWorkspace,
		exportBackupPayload,
		restoreBackupPayload,
		injectDemoSeed,
		removeDemoSeed,
		clearLocalData
	};
}


----------------------------------------------------------------

================================================================
PATH: app/composables/useSyncEngine.ts
================================================================

import { ref } from 'vue';
import { onLocalMutationStaged, useLocalRepository } from '~/composables/useLocalRepository';
import type { ServerSyncResponse } from '~/domain/schemas';

export type SyncStatusVisual = 'synced' | 'local_pending' | 'syncing' | 'offline' | 'error';

const status = ref<SyncStatusVisual>('synced');
const pendingCount = ref(0);
const isOnline = ref(true);
const lastSyncedAtIso = ref<string | null>(null);
const knownServerVersionIso = ref<string | null>(null);
const lastErrorMessage = ref<string | null>(null);

let isInitialized = false;
let isSyncInFlight = false;
let hasQueuedSyncDuringFlight = false;
let debounceTimer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null = null;
let retryTimer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null = null;
let periodicPollTimer: ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | null = null;
let retryAttempt = 0;

export function useSyncEngine() {
	const localRepo = useLocalRepository();

	async function refreshPendingCount(): Promise<number> {
		if (!import.meta.client) return 0;
		const list = await localRepo.getPendingMutations();
		pendingCount.value = list.length;
		return list.length;
	}

	async function syncNow(options?: { checkRemoteUpdates?: boolean }): Promise<boolean> {
		if (!import.meta.client) return false;

		if (!navigator.onLine) {
			isOnline.value = false;
			status.value = 'offline';
			return false;
		}

		if (isSyncInFlight) {
			hasQueuedSyncDuringFlight = true;
			return false;
		}
		isSyncInFlight = true;
		hasQueuedSyncDuringFlight = false;

		try {
			const meta = await localRepo.getSyncMeta();
			knownServerVersionIso.value = meta.knownServerVersionIso;
			lastSyncedAtIso.value = meta.lastSyncedAtIso;

			const mutations = await localRepo.getPendingMutations();
			pendingCount.value = mutations.length;

			if (mutations.length === 0 && !options?.checkRemoteUpdates) {
				status.value = 'synced';
				lastErrorMessage.value = null;
				isSyncInFlight = false;
				return true;
			}

			status.value = mutations.length > 0 ? 'syncing' : 'synced';

			const data = await $fetch<ServerSyncResponse>('/api/sync', {
				method: 'POST',
				body: {
					clientId: meta.clientId || 'nuxt-client',
					knownServerVersionIso: knownServerVersionIso.value,
					mutations
				}
			});

			if (!data.ok) {
				throw new Error(data.error || 'خطا در پردازش بسته همگام‌سازی');
			}

			const appliedSet = new Set(data.appliedMutationIds);
			const appliedSnapshots = mutations
				.filter((m) => appliedSet.has(m.id))
				.map((m) => ({ id: m.id, queuedAtIso: m.queuedAtIso }));

			await localRepo.removeAppliedMutations(appliedSnapshots);

			if (data.fullWorkspace) {
				await localRepo.applyServerWorkspace(data.fullWorkspace);
			}

			knownServerVersionIso.value = data.serverVersionIso;
			lastSyncedAtIso.value = data.serverTimeIso;
			lastErrorMessage.value = null;
			retryAttempt = 0;

			await localRepo.saveSyncMeta({
				key: 'meta:sync',
				clientId: meta.clientId || 'nuxt-client',
				knownServerVersionIso: knownServerVersionIso.value,
				lastSyncedAtIso: lastSyncedAtIso.value
			});

			const remaining = await refreshPendingCount();
			status.value = remaining > 0 ? 'local_pending' : 'synced';
			isSyncInFlight = false;

			if (remaining > 0 || hasQueuedSyncDuringFlight) {
				hasQueuedSyncDuringFlight = false;
				setTimeout(() => {
					void syncNow({ checkRemoteUpdates: false });
				}, 350);
			}
			return true;
		} catch (err) {
			isSyncInFlight = false;
			status.value = navigator.onLine ? 'error' : 'offline';
			lastErrorMessage.value = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'خطای ارتباط با سرور';
			scheduleRetry();
			return false;
		}
	}

	function scheduleRetry() {
		if (!isOnline.value) return;
		if (retryTimer) clearTimeout(retryTimer);

		retryAttempt = Math.min(retryAttempt + 1, 5);
		const delayMs = Math.min(30000, 2000 * Math.pow(2, retryAttempt - 1));

		retryTimer = setTimeout(() => {
			retryTimer = null;
			if (isOnline.value && !isSyncInFlight) {
				void syncNow({ checkRemoteUpdates: false });
			}
		}, delayMs);
	}

	async function notifyLocalMutationStaged(): Promise<void> {
		if (!import.meta.client) return;
		await refreshPendingCount();

		if (!navigator.onLine) {
			isOnline.value = false;
			status.value = 'offline';
			return;
		}

		if (status.value !== 'syncing') {
			status.value = 'local_pending';
		}

		if (debounceTimer) clearTimeout(debounceTimer);
		debounceTimer = setTimeout(() => {
			debounceTimer = null;
			void syncNow({ checkRemoteUpdates: false });
		}, 350);
	}

	function init() {
		if (!import.meta.client || isInitialized) return;
		isInitialized = true;
		isOnline.value = navigator.onLine;

		onLocalMutationStaged(() => {
			void notifyLocalMutationStaged();
		});

		window.addEventListener('online', () => {
			isOnline.value = true;
			retryAttempt = 0;
			if (retryTimer) clearTimeout(retryTimer);
			void syncNow({ checkRemoteUpdates: true });
		});

		window.addEventListener('offline', () => {
			isOnline.value = false;
			status.value = 'offline';
			if (retryTimer) clearTimeout(retryTimer);
		});

		document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', () => {
			if (document.visibilityState === 'visible' && isOnline.value) {
				retryAttempt = 0;
				void syncNow({ checkRemoteUpdates: true });
			}
		});

		window.addEventListener('focus', () => {
			if (isOnline.value) {
				void syncNow({ checkRemoteUpdates: true });
			}
		});

		window.addEventListener('pagehide', () => {
			if (debounceTimer) {
				clearTimeout(debounceTimer);
				debounceTimer = null;
			}
			if (isOnline.value && pendingCount.value > 0 && !isSyncInFlight) {
				void syncNow({ checkRemoteUpdates: false });
			}
		});

		periodicPollTimer = setInterval(() => {
			if (document.visibilityState === 'visible' && isOnline.value && !isSyncInFlight) {
				void syncNow({ checkRemoteUpdates: true });
			}
		}, 3 * 60 * 1000);

		void refreshPendingCount().then(() => {
			if (isOnline.value) {
				void syncNow({ checkRemoteUpdates: true });
			}
		});
	}

	function reset() {
		if (debounceTimer) {
			clearTimeout(debounceTimer);
			debounceTimer = null;
		}
		if (retryTimer) {
			clearTimeout(retryTimer);
			retryTimer = null;
		}
		isSyncInFlight = false;
		hasQueuedSyncDuringFlight = false;
		retryAttempt = 0;
		pendingCount.value = 0;
		knownServerVersionIso.value = null;
		lastSyncedAtIso.value = null;
		lastErrorMessage.value = null;
		status.value = 'synced';
	}

	return {
		status,
		pendingCount,
		isOnline,
		lastSyncedAtIso,
		knownServerVersionIso,
		lastErrorMessage,
		init,
		reset,
		syncNow,
		notifyLocalMutationStaged,
		refreshPendingCount
	};
}


----------------------------------------------------------------

================================================================
PATH: app/composables/useDexieLiveQuery.ts
================================================================

import { liveQuery, type Observable, type Subscription } from 'dexie';
import { ref, shallowRef, watch, onScopeDispose, type Ref, type ShallowRef, type WatchSource } from 'vue';

export type DexieLiveQueryRef<T> = ShallowRef<T> & {
	isLoading: Ref<boolean>;
};

/**
 * Connects a Dexie liveQuery to a reactive Vue 3 ShallowRef with an attached `isLoading` state.
 * Automatically tracks both IndexedDB table modifications and optional Vue reactive dependencies,
 * cleans up subscriptions on unmount, and handles SSR isolation safely.
 */
export function useDexieLiveQuery<T>(
	querier: () => Promise<T> | T,
	initialValue: T,
	deps?: WatchSource | WatchSource[]
): DexieLiveQueryRef<T> {
	const isLoading = ref<boolean>(Boolean(import.meta.client));
	const state = Object.assign(shallowRef<T>(initialValue), {
		isLoading
	}) as DexieLiveQueryRef<T>;

	if (!import.meta.client) {
		return state;
	}

	let subscription: Subscription | null = null;

	function cleanup() {
		if (subscription) {
			subscription.unsubscribe();
			subscription = null;
		}
	}

	function subscribeQuery() {
		cleanup();
		isLoading.value = true;
		try {
			const observable: Observable<T> = liveQuery(querier);
			subscription = observable.subscribe({
				next: (val) => {
					state.value = val;
					isLoading.value = false;
				},
				error: (err) => {
					console.error('[Dexie liveQuery] Subscription error:', err);
					isLoading.value = false;
				}
			});
		} catch (err) {
			console.error('[Dexie liveQuery] Initialization error:', err);
			isLoading.value = false;
		}
	}

	if (deps) {
		watch(deps, () => {
			subscribeQuery();
		}, { immediate: true });
	} else {
		subscribeQuery();
	}

	onScopeDispose(() => {
		cleanup();
	});

	return state;
}

================================================================
PATH: app/composables/useDailyTracker.ts
================================================================

import { ref, computed, watch, type Ref } from 'vue';
import { useAppDatabase } from '~/composables/useAppDatabase';
import { useDexieLiveQuery } from '~/composables/useDexieLiveQuery';
import { useLocalRepository } from '~/composables/useLocalRepository';
import {
	buildHabitTree,
	computeCompositeParentDisplay,
	computeDailyCompletionStats,
	evaluateDayStatusColor,
	getApplicableHabitsForDate,
	getMaxSupportedTier,
	isHabitNodeCompleted,
	resolveEffectiveCadenceMinutes,
	resolveHabitRuleSnapshot
} from '~/core/habit-engine';
import { shiftIsoDate } from '~/domain/calendar';
import { DEFAULT_REFLECTION_TAGS, TIER_SCORES } from '~/domain/constants';
import type {
	DayStatusColor,
	HabitEntity,
	HabitLogItemInput,
	SaveDailyEntryInput
} from '~/domain/schemas';

export interface CadenceDraftItem {
	habitId: string;
	plannedIntervals: number;
	plannedMinutesPerInterval: number;
	cadenceWindowMinutes: number;
}

export function useDailyTracker(dateIso: Ref<string>) {
	const db = useAppDatabase();
	const localRepo = useLocalRepository();

	const tomorrowIso = computed(() => shiftIsoDate(dateIso.value, 1));

	// Dexie Live Queries
	const habits = useDexieLiveQuery(() => db.habits.orderBy('sortOrder').toArray(), []);
	const entry = useDexieLiveQuery(() => db.dailyEntries.get(dateIso.value), undefined, dateIso);
	const logs = useDexieLiveQuery(() => db.habitLogs.where('dateIso').equals(dateIso.value).toArray(), [], dateIso);
	const allEntries = useDexieLiveQuery(() => db.dailyEntries.orderBy('dateIso').reverse().limit(35).toArray(), []);
	const tagsRecords = useDexieLiveQuery(() => db.reflectionTags.orderBy('sortOrder').toArray(), []);
	const dayCadencePlans = useDexieLiveQuery(() => db.cadencePlans.where('dateIso').equals(dateIso.value).toArray(), [], dateIso);
	const tomorrowCadencePlans = useDexieLiveQuery(() => db.cadencePlans.where('dateIso').equals(tomorrowIso.value).toArray(), [], tomorrowIso);
	const calendarEvents = useDexieLiveQuery(() => db.calendarEvents.toArray(), []);

	// Local mutable state
	const reflectionNote = ref('');
	const journalText = ref('');
	const selectedTags = ref<string[]>([]);
	const manualDayStatus = ref<DayStatusColor | null>(null);
	const logsByHabitId = ref<Record<string, HabitLogItemInput>>({});
	const tomorrowPlansByHabitId = ref<Record<string, CadenceDraftItem>>({});
	const saveState = ref<'idle' | 'saving' | 'saved' | 'error'>('idle');
	const isDirty = ref(false);

	let autoSaveTimer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null = null;

	const availableTags = computed(() => {
		if (tagsRecords.value.length > 0) {
			return tagsRecords.value.map((t) => t.label);
		}
		return [...DEFAULT_REFLECTION_TAGS];
	});

	// Synchronize when date or database queries change
	watch(
		[
			dateIso,
			entry,
			logs,
			habits,
			dayCadencePlans,
			tomorrowCadencePlans,
			entry.isLoading,
			logs.isLoading,
			dayCadencePlans.isLoading,
			tomorrowCadencePlans.isLoading
		],
		() => {
			if (
				entry.isLoading.value ||
				logs.isLoading.value ||
				dayCadencePlans.isLoading.value ||
				tomorrowCadencePlans.isLoading.value
			) {
				return;
			}
			reflectionNote.value = entry.value?.reflectionNote ?? '';
			journalText.value = entry.value?.journalText ?? '';
			selectedTags.value = entry.value?.tags ? [...entry.value.tags] : [];
			manualDayStatus.value = entry.value?.dayStatus ?? null;

			const dayPlanMap = new Map(dayCadencePlans.value.map((p) => [p.habitId, p]));
			const existingLogMap = new Map(logs.value.map((l) => [l.habitId, l]));
			const nextLogs: Record<string, HabitLogItemInput> = {};

			for (const habit of habits.value) {
				const existing = existingLogMap.get(habit.id);
				const dayPlan = dayPlanMap.get(habit.id);
				const plannedIntervals = dayPlan?.plannedIntervals ?? null;
				const plannedMinutes = dayPlan?.plannedMinutesPerInterval ?? null;

				if (existing) {
					nextLogs[habit.id] = {
						habitId: habit.id,
						habitTitleSnapshot: existing.habitTitleSnapshot ?? habit.title,
						achievedTier: existing.achievedTier,
						achievedRuleSnapshot:
							existing.achievedRuleSnapshot ?? resolveHabitRuleSnapshot(habit, existing.achievedTier),
						completedIntervals: existing.completedIntervals ?? null,
						actualMinutesPerInterval: existing.actualMinutesPerInterval,
						totalActualMinutes: existing.totalActualMinutes,
						plannedIntervalsSnapshot: existing.plannedIntervalsSnapshot ?? plannedIntervals,
						plannedMinutesSnapshot: existing.plannedMinutesSnapshot ?? plannedMinutes,
						updatedAtIso: existing.updatedAtIso ?? null
					};
				} else {
					const effectivePlannedIntervals =
						habit.trackingMode === 'interval_cadence'
							? (plannedIntervals ?? habit.defaultIntervals)
							: plannedIntervals;
					const effectivePlannedMinutes =
						habit.trackingMode === 'interval_cadence'
							? (plannedMinutes ?? habit.defaultMinutesPerInterval)
							: plannedMinutes;

					nextLogs[habit.id] = {
						habitId: habit.id,
						habitTitleSnapshot: habit.title,
						achievedTier: null,
						achievedRuleSnapshot: null,
						completedIntervals: null,
						actualMinutesPerInterval: habit.trackingMode === 'interval_cadence' ? 0 : null,
						totalActualMinutes: habit.trackingMode === 'interval_cadence' ? 0 : null,
						plannedIntervalsSnapshot: effectivePlannedIntervals,
						plannedMinutesSnapshot: effectivePlannedMinutes,
						updatedAtIso: null
					};
				}
			}
			logsByHabitId.value = nextLogs;

			const tomorrowPlanMap = new Map(tomorrowCadencePlans.value.map((p) => [p.habitId, p]));
			const nextTomorrow: Record<string, CadenceDraftItem> = {};
			for (const habit of habits.value.filter((h) => h.trackingMode === 'interval_cadence')) {
				const tPlan = tomorrowPlanMap.get(habit.id);
				const todayPlan = dayPlanMap.get(habit.id);
				nextTomorrow[habit.id] = {
					habitId: habit.id,
					plannedIntervals: 1,
					plannedMinutesPerInterval: tPlan?.plannedMinutesPerInterval ?? todayPlan?.plannedMinutesPerInterval ?? 0,
					cadenceWindowMinutes: 60
				};
			}
			tomorrowPlansByHabitId.value = nextTomorrow;
			isDirty.value = false;
			saveState.value = entry.value ? 'saved' : 'idle';
		},
		{ immediate: true }
	);

	const loggedHabitIds = computed<ReadonlySet<string>>(() => {
		const set = new Set<string>();
		for (const log of Object.values(logsByHabitId.value)) {
			const hasTier = log.achievedTier !== null && log.achievedTier !== undefined;
			const hasMinutes = Boolean(entry.value) && resolveEffectiveCadenceMinutes(log) > 0;
			if (hasTier || hasMinutes) {
				set.add(log.habitId);
			}
		}
		return set;
	});

	const applicableHabits = computed(() =>
		getApplicableHabitsForDate(habits.value, {
			dateIso: dateIso.value,
			hasExistingEntry: Boolean(entry.value),
			loggedHabitIds: loggedHabitIds.value
		})
	);

	const tree = computed(() =>
		buildHabitTree(habits.value, {
			dateIso: dateIso.value,
			hasExistingEntry: Boolean(entry.value),
			loggedHabitIds: loggedHabitIds.value
		})
	);

	const computedDayStatus = computed<DayStatusColor>(() => {
		if (manualDayStatus.value) return manualDayStatus.value;
		return evaluateDayStatusColor(applicableHabits.value, Object.values(logsByHabitId.value));
	});

	const completionStats = computed(() =>
		computeDailyCompletionStats({
			applicableHabits: applicableHabits.value,
			loggedHabitIds: loggedHabitIds.value,
			logsByHabitId: logsByHabitId.value,
			hasExistingEntry: Boolean(entry.value),
			isDirty: isDirty.value
		})
	);

	function buildDailyInputObject(): SaveDailyEntryInput {
		return {
			dateIso: dateIso.value,
			dayStatus: computedDayStatus.value,
			reflectionNote: reflectionNote.value,
			journalText: journalText.value.trim() || null,
			tags: [...selectedTags.value],
			updatedAtIso: new Date().toISOString(),
			logs: Object.values(logsByHabitId.value)
		};
	}

	function scheduleAutoSave(delayMs = 0) {
		isDirty.value = true;
		saveState.value = 'saving';

		if (autoSaveTimer) clearTimeout(autoSaveTimer);

		if (delayMs <= 0) {
			void flushPendingSave();
		} else {
			autoSaveTimer = setTimeout(() => {
				autoSaveTimer = null;
				void flushPendingSave();
			}, delayMs);
		}
	}

	async function flushPendingSave(): Promise<void> {
		if (autoSaveTimer) {
			clearTimeout(autoSaveTimer);
			autoSaveTimer = null;
		}
		if (!dateIso.value) return;

		saveState.value = 'saving';
		try {
			const payload = buildDailyInputObject();
			await localRepo.daily.saveDailyEntry(payload);
			isDirty.value = false;
			saveState.value = 'saved';
		} catch {
			saveState.value = 'error';
		}
	}

	function markAllOptimalFiveSecond() {
		const nowIso = new Date().toISOString();
		const updated = { ...logsByHabitId.value };
		for (const habit of applicableHabits.value) {
			if (habit.status !== 'active') continue;
			const current = updated[habit.id];
			if (!current) continue;

			if (habit.trackingMode === 'tiered') {
				const maxTier = getMaxSupportedTier(habit);
				updated[habit.id] = {
					...current,
					habitTitleSnapshot: habit.title,
					achievedTier: maxTier,
					achievedRuleSnapshot: resolveHabitRuleSnapshot(habit, maxTier),
					updatedAtIso: nowIso
				};
			} else {
				const plannedMinutes =
					current.plannedMinutesSnapshot && current.plannedMinutesSnapshot > 0
						? current.plannedMinutesSnapshot
						: current.actualMinutesPerInterval && current.actualMinutesPerInterval > 0
							? current.actualMinutesPerInterval
							: habit.defaultMinutesPerInterval > 0
								? habit.defaultMinutesPerInterval
								: 10;
				const intervals = current.completedIntervals ?? null;
				updated[habit.id] = {
					...current,
					completedIntervals: intervals,
					actualMinutesPerInterval: plannedMinutes,
					totalActualMinutes: intervals !== null ? Number((intervals * plannedMinutes).toFixed(1)) : plannedMinutes,
					achievedTier: TIER_SCORES.GOLD,
					updatedAtIso: nowIso
				};
			}
		}
		logsByHabitId.value = updated;
		manualDayStatus.value = 'optimal';
		scheduleAutoSave(0);
	}

	function setTierScore(habitId: string, tier: number) {
		const current = logsByHabitId.value[habitId];
		if (!current) return;
		const nextTier = current.achievedTier === tier ? null : tier;
		setTierExplicit(habitId, nextTier);
	}

	function setTierExplicit(habitId: string, tier: number | null) {
		const current = logsByHabitId.value[habitId];
		if (!current) return;
		const nowIso = new Date().toISOString();
		const habit = habits.value.find((h) => h.id === habitId);

		logsByHabitId.value[habitId] = {
			...current,
			habitTitleSnapshot: habit?.title ?? current.habitTitleSnapshot ?? null,
			achievedTier: tier,
			achievedRuleSnapshot: habit && tier !== null ? resolveHabitRuleSnapshot(habit, tier) : null,
			updatedAtIso: nowIso
		};
		manualDayStatus.value = null;
		scheduleAutoSave(0);
	}

	function setParentGroupAllTier(parentId: string, tier: number) {
		const children = habits.value.filter((h) => h.parentId === parentId && h.status === 'active');
		const allAlreadySelected =
			children.length > 0 && children.every((child) => logsByHabitId.value[child.id]?.achievedTier === tier);
		const targetTier = allAlreadySelected ? null : tier;

		for (const child of children) {
			const maxChildTier = getMaxSupportedTier(child);
			const effectiveTier =
				targetTier === null
					? null
					: targetTier === TIER_SCORES.FAILED || targetTier === TIER_SCORES.SKIPPED
						? targetTier
						: Math.min(targetTier, maxChildTier);
			setTierExplicit(child.id, effectiveTier);
		}
		setTierExplicit(parentId, targetTier);
	}

	function clearDay() {
		const nowIso = new Date().toISOString();
		const updated = { ...logsByHabitId.value };
		for (const habit of habits.value) {
			const current = updated[habit.id];
			if (!current) continue;
			updated[habit.id] = {
				...current,
				achievedTier: null,
				achievedRuleSnapshot: null,
				completedIntervals: null,
				actualMinutesPerInterval: habit.trackingMode === 'interval_cadence' ? 0 : null,
				totalActualMinutes: habit.trackingMode === 'interval_cadence' ? 0 : null,
				updatedAtIso: nowIso
			};
		}
		logsByHabitId.value = updated;
		reflectionNote.value = '';
		journalText.value = '';
		selectedTags.value = [];
		manualDayStatus.value = null;
		scheduleAutoSave(0);
	}

	function updateCadenceActual(habitId: string, minutesPerInterval: number) {
		const current = logsByHabitId.value[habitId];
		if (!current) return;
		const nowIso = new Date().toISOString();
		const safeMinutes = Math.max(0, Number.isNaN(minutesPerInterval) ? 0 : minutesPerInterval);

		logsByHabitId.value[habitId] = {
			...current,
			completedIntervals: null,
			actualMinutesPerInterval: safeMinutes,
			totalActualMinutes: safeMinutes,
			updatedAtIso: nowIso
		};
		manualDayStatus.value = null;
		scheduleAutoSave(0);
	}

	function toggleTag(tag: string) {
		if (selectedTags.value.includes(tag)) {
			selectedTags.value = selectedTags.value.filter((t) => t !== tag);
		} else {
			selectedTags.value = [...selectedTags.value, tag];
		}
		scheduleAutoSave(0);
	}

	function setTomorrowPlanMinutes(habitId: string, minutes: number) {
		const current = tomorrowPlansByHabitId.value[habitId];
		if (!current) return;
		tomorrowPlansByHabitId.value = {
			...tomorrowPlansByHabitId.value,
			[habitId]: {
				...current,
				plannedMinutesPerInterval: Math.max(0, Number.isNaN(minutes) ? 0 : minutes)
			}
		};
	}

	function copyTodayActualsToTomorrowPlan() {
		const next = { ...tomorrowPlansByHabitId.value };
		for (const habit of habits.value.filter((h) => h.trackingMode === 'interval_cadence')) {
			const log = logsByHabitId.value[habit.id];
			if (log) {
				next[habit.id] = {
					habitId: habit.id,
					plannedIntervals: 1,
					plannedMinutesPerInterval: log.actualMinutesPerInterval ?? 0,
					cadenceWindowMinutes: 60
				};
			}
		}
		tomorrowPlansByHabitId.value = next;
	}

	async function saveTomorrowCadencePlans(): Promise<void> {
		const items = Object.values(tomorrowPlansByHabitId.value);
		await localRepo.daily.saveCadencePlans({
			targetDateIso: tomorrowIso.value,
			items
		});
	}

	function isNodeCompleted(habitId: string): boolean {
		return isHabitNodeCompleted({
			habitId,
			habits: habits.value,
			logsByHabitId: logsByHabitId.value,
			hasExistingEntry: Boolean(entry.value),
			isDirty: isDirty.value
		});
	}

	function getCompositeNotation(parentId: string): string {
		const children = habits.value
			.filter((h) => h.parentId === parentId && h.status === 'active')
			.sort((a, b) => a.sortOrder - b.sortOrder);
		return computeCompositeParentDisplay(children, logsByHabitId.value);
	}

	return {
		habits,
		entry,
		allEntries,
		calendarEvents,
		tree,
		applicableHabits,
		availableTags,
		reflectionNote,
		journalText,
		selectedTags,
		manualDayStatus,
		logsByHabitId,
		tomorrowPlansByHabitId,
		tomorrowIso,
		saveState,
		isDirty,
		computedDayStatus,
		completionStats,
		markAllOptimalFiveSecond,
		setTierScore,
		setTierExplicit,
		setParentGroupAllTier,
		clearDay,
		updateCadenceActual,
		toggleTag,
		scheduleAutoSave,
		flushPendingSave,
		setTomorrowPlanMinutes,
		copyTodayActualsToTomorrowPlan,
		saveTomorrowCadencePlans,
		isNodeCompleted,
		getCompositeNotation
	};
}


----------------------------------------------------------------

================================================================
PATH: app/composables/useTheme.ts
================================================================

import { ref } from 'vue';

function getInitialDarkState(): boolean {
	if (!import.meta.client) return true;
	try {
		const stored = localStorage.getItem('newlife_theme');
		if (stored === 'light') return false;
		if (stored === 'dark') return true;
	} catch {
		// ignore storage access errors
	}
	return true;
}

const isDark = ref(getInitialDarkState());

export function useTheme() {
	function applyToDom() {
		if (!import.meta.client) return;
		const root = document.documentElement;
		root.classList.toggle('dark', isDark.value);
		root.style.colorScheme = isDark.value ? 'dark' : 'light';
	}

	function init() {
		if (!import.meta.client) return;
		isDark.value = getInitialDarkState();
		applyToDom();
	}

	function setTheme(dark: boolean) {
		if (isDark.value === dark) {
			applyToDom();
			return;
		}
		if (!import.meta.client) {
			isDark.value = dark;
			return;
		}

		const applyChange = () => {
			isDark.value = dark;
			try {
				localStorage.setItem('newlife_theme', isDark.value ? 'dark' : 'light');
			} catch {
				// ignore quota errors
			}
			applyToDom();
		};

		const docWithTransition = document as Document & {
			startViewTransition?: (callback: () => void) => void;
		};

		if (typeof docWithTransition.startViewTransition === 'function') {
			docWithTransition.startViewTransition(() => {
				applyChange();
			});
		} else {
			applyChange();
		}
	}

	function toggle() {
		setTheme(!isDark.value);
	}

	return {
		isDark,
		init,
		setTheme,
		toggle
	};
}

================================================================
PATH: app/composables/useAppDatabase.ts
================================================================

import Dexie, { type Table } from 'dexie';
import type {
	BookEntity,
	CadencePlanEntity,
	CalendarEventEntity,
	HabitEntity,
	LifecycleEventEntity,
	LocalAuthRecord,
	LocalDailyEntryRecord,
	LocalHabitLogRecord,
	LocalReflectionTagRecord,
	MediaEntity,
	NoteEntity,
	SyncMetaRecord,
	SyncOutboxMutation
} from '~/domain/schemas';

export type {
	LocalAuthRecord,
	LocalDailyEntryRecord,
	LocalHabitLogRecord,
	LocalReflectionTagRecord,
	SyncMetaRecord
};

export class PersonalOSDatabase extends Dexie {
	habits!: Table<HabitEntity, string>;
	dailyEntries!: Table<LocalDailyEntryRecord, string>;
	habitLogs!: Table<LocalHabitLogRecord, string>;
	cadencePlans!: Table<CadencePlanEntity, string>;
	books!: Table<BookEntity, string>;
	media!: Table<MediaEntity, string>;
	notes!: Table<NoteEntity, string>;
	calendarEvents!: Table<CalendarEventEntity, string>;
	lifecycleEvents!: Table<LifecycleEventEntity, string>;
	reflectionTags!: Table<LocalReflectionTagRecord, string>;
	syncOutbox!: Table<SyncOutboxMutation, string>;
	syncMeta!: Table<SyncMetaRecord, string>;
	localAuth!: Table<LocalAuthRecord, string>;

	constructor() {
		super('newlife_dexie_db');

		this.version(1).stores({
			habits: 'id, parentId, status, sortOrder, trackingMode, timeSlot, updatedAtIso',
			dailyEntries: 'dateIso, dayStatus, updatedAtIso',
			habitLogs: 'id, dateIso, habitId, [dateIso+habitId], updatedAtIso',
			cadencePlans: 'id, dateIso, habitId, [dateIso+habitId], updatedAtIso',
			books: 'id, status, startedAtIso, finishedAtIso, createdAtIso, updatedAtIso',
			media: 'id, category, status, finishedAtIso, createdAtIso, updatedAtIso',
			notes: 'id, category, isPinned, updatedAtIso',
			calendarEvents: 'id, dateIso, [jalaliMonth+jalaliDay], isCompleted, updatedAtIso',
			lifecycleEvents: 'id, habitId, effectiveDateIso, createdAtIso',
			reflectionTags: 'id, &label, sortOrder',
			syncOutbox: 'id, queuedAtIso, kind',
			syncMeta: 'key',
			localAuth: 'key'
		});
	}
}

let dbInstance: PersonalOSDatabase | null = null;

export function getAppDatabase(): PersonalOSDatabase | null {
	if (!import.meta.client) {
		return null;
	}
	if (!dbInstance) {
		dbInstance = new PersonalOSDatabase();
	}
	return dbInstance;
}

export function useAppDatabase(): PersonalOSDatabase {
	const db = getAppDatabase();
	if (!db) {
		throw new Error('Database is only accessible on client-side environments.');
	}
	return db;
}

@theme inline {
	--font-*: initial;
	--font-sans: 'Vazirmatn', system-ui, -apple-system, sans-serif;
	--font-mono: 'Vazirmatn', monospace;

	--color-*: initial;

	--color-surface: var(--bg-surface);
	--color-elevated: var(--bg-subtle);
	--color-muted: var(--bg-interactive);
	--color-overlay: var(--bg-overlay);

================================================================
PATH: app/components/ui/CustomSelect.vue
================================================================

<script setup lang="ts">
import { ref, computed, onMounted, onUnmounted } from 'vue';
import { Check, ChevronDown } from 'lucide-vue-next';

export interface SelectOption {
	value: string | number;
	label: string;
}

interface Props {
	id?: string;
	name?: string;
	modelValue: string | number;
	options: ReadonlyArray<SelectOption>;
	disabled?: boolean;
	triggerClass?: string;
	placeholder?: string;
}

const props = withDefaults(defineProps<Props>(), {
	id: undefined,
	name: undefined,
	disabled: false,
	triggerClass: '',
	placeholder: 'انتخاب کنید'
});

const emit = defineEmits<{
	(e: 'update:modelValue', value: string | number): void;
	(e: 'select', value: string | number): void;
}>();

const isOpen = ref(false);
const containerEl = ref<HTMLDivElement | null>(null);

const selectedOption = computed(
	() => props.options.find((opt) => String(opt.value) === String(props.modelValue)) ?? props.options[0]
);

function toggleDropdown() {
	if (props.disabled) return;
	isOpen.value = !isOpen.value;
}

function selectOption(opt: SelectOption) {
	emit('update:modelValue', opt.value);
	emit('select', opt.value);
	isOpen.value = false;
}

function handleWindowClick(e: MouseEvent) {
	if (!isOpen.value || !containerEl.value) return;
	if (!containerEl.value.contains(e.target as Node)) {
		isOpen.value = false;
	}
}

function handleKeydown(e: KeyboardEvent) {
	if (props.disabled) return;
	if (e.key === 'Escape' && isOpen.value) {
		e.preventDefault();
		isOpen.value = false;
		return;
	}
	if (!isOpen.value && (e.key === 'Enter' || e.key === ' ' || e.key === 'ArrowDown')) {
		e.preventDefault();
		isOpen.value = true;
		return;
	}
	if (isOpen.value && (e.key === 'ArrowDown' || e.key === 'ArrowUp')) {
		e.preventDefault();
		const currentIndex = props.options.findIndex((o) => String(o.value) === String(props.modelValue));
		const targetIndex =
			e.key === 'ArrowDown'
				? (currentIndex + 1) % props.options.length
				: (currentIndex - 1 + props.options.length) % props.options.length;
		const targetOpt = props.options[targetIndex];
		if (targetOpt) {
			const nextVal = targetOpt.value;
			emit('update:modelValue', nextVal);
			emit('select', nextVal);
		}
	}
}

onMounted(() => {
	window.addEventListener('click', handleWindowClick);
});

onUnmounted(() => {
	window.removeEventListener('click', handleWindowClick);
});
</script>

<template>
	<div ref="containerEl" class="relative w-full">
		<input v-if="name" type="hidden" :name="name" :value="modelValue" />

		<button
			:id="id"
			type="button"
			:disabled="disabled"
			aria-haspopup="listbox"
			:aria-expanded="isOpen"
			class="flex h-11 w-full items-center justify-between gap-2 rounded-2xl border px-3.5 text-right text-sm font-bold transition focus:outline-none"
			:class="[
				isOpen
					? 'border-border-strong bg-surface ring-2 ring-accent-border'
					: 'border-border bg-elevated hover:border-border-strong',
				disabled ? 'opacity-60 cursor-not-allowed' : '',
				triggerClass
			]"
			@click="toggleDropdown"
			@keydown="handleKeydown"
		>
			<span class="truncate">{{ selectedOption ? selectedOption.label : placeholder }}</span>
			<ChevronDown
				class="h-4 w-4 shrink-0 text-fg-muted transition-transform duration-200"
				:class="{ 'rotate-180 text-accent-text': isOpen }"
			/>
		</button>

		<div
			v-if="isOpen"
			role="listbox"
			class="absolute right-0 left-0 z-50 mt-2 max-h-60 overflow-y-auto rounded-2xl border border-border-strong bg-surface p-1.5 shadow-(--shadow-modal)"
		>
			<button
				v-for="opt in options"
				:key="String(opt.value)"
				type="button"
				role="option"
				:aria-selected="String(opt.value) === String(modelValue)"
				class="flex w-full items-center justify-between gap-2 rounded-xl px-3 py-2.5 text-right text-xs font-extrabold transition"
				:class="
					String(opt.value) === String(modelValue)
						? 'bg-accent-soft text-accent-text'
						: 'text-fg hover:bg-elevated'
				"
				@click="selectOption(opt)"
			>
				<span class="truncate">{{ opt.label }}</span>
				<Check v-if="String(opt.value) === String(modelValue)" class="h-4 w-4 shrink-0 text-accent-text" />
			</button>
		</div>
	</div>
</template>

================================================================
PATH: app/components/ui/BaseModal.vue
================================================================

<script setup lang="ts">
import { onMounted, onUnmounted } from 'vue';
import { X } from 'lucide-vue-next';

interface Props {
	title?: string;
	subtitle?: string;
	maxWidthClass?: string;
}

withDefaults(defineProps<Props>(), {
	title: '',
	subtitle: '',
	maxWidthClass: 'max-w-lg'
});

const emit = defineEmits<{
	(e: 'close'): void;
}>();

function handleKeydown(e: KeyboardEvent) {
	if (e.key === 'Escape') {
		e.preventDefault();
		emit('close');
	}
}

onMounted(() => {
	window.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeydown);
});

onUnmounted(() => {
	window.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeydown);
});
</script>

<template>
	<Teleport to="body">
		<div
			class="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-end justify-center bg-overlay p-2.5 backdrop-blur-xs transition-opacity sm:items-center sm:p-5"
			role="dialog"
			aria-modal="true"
			tabindex="-1"
			@click.self="emit('close')"
		>
			<div
				class="flex max-h-[90dvh] w-full flex-col overflow-hidden rounded-3xl border border-border bg-surface shadow-(--shadow-modal) transition-all"
				:class="maxWidthClass"
			>
				<div class="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 border-b border-border px-5 py-4 sm:px-6">
					<slot name="headerContent">
						<div class="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-2.5">
							<div
								v-if="$slots.headerIcon"
								class="flex h-9 w-9 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl border border-accent-border bg-accent-soft text-accent-text"
							>
								<slot name="headerIcon" />
							</div>
							<div class="min-w-0">
								<h2 class="truncate text-base font-black text-fg sm:text-lg">{{ title }}</h2>
								<p v-if="subtitle" class="mt-0.5 text-xs font-bold text-fg-secondary">{{ subtitle }}</p>
							</div>
						</div>
					</slot>

					<button
						type="button"
						class="flex h-9 w-9 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-elevated text-fg-secondary transition hover:text-fg"
						aria-label="بستن"
						@click="emit('close')"
					>
						<X class="h-4 w-4" />
					</button>
				</div>

				<slot />
			</div>
		</div>
	</Teleport>
</template>

================================================================
PATH: app/components/analytics/HabitTrendChart.vue

<script setup lang="ts">
import { ref, computed } from 'vue';
import {
	CHART_DIMENSIONS,
	buildRollingAreaPath,
	buildRollingPolylinePoints,
	computeMaxCadenceMinutes,
	getChartX as getX,
	getChartYFromMinutes,
	getChartYFromScore as getYFromScore
} from '~/core/chart-math';
import { toPersianDigits } from '~/domain/calendar';
import { getOutcomeBadgeMeta } from '~/domain/constants';
import type { HabitDailyPoint, SelectedHabitAnalytics } from '~/domain/schemas';
import { GitCommitHorizontal } from 'lucide-vue-next';

interface Props {
	report: SelectedHabitAnalytics;
}

const props = defineProps<Props>();

const manualPointIndex = ref<number | null>(null);

const isCadence = computed(() => props.report.habit.trackingMode === 'interval_cadence');

const activePointIndex = computed<number | null>(() => {
	const series = props.report.dailySeries;
	if (series.length === 0) return null;

	if (manualPointIndex.value !== null && manualPointIndex.value >= 0 && manualPointIndex.value < series.length) {
		return manualPointIndex.value;
	}

	for (let i = series.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
		const item = series[i];
		if (item && item.outcome !== 'unlogged') {
			return i;
		}
	}
	return series.length - 1;
});

const { SVG_WIDTH, SVG_HEIGHT, PAD_TOP, PAD_LEFT, PAD_RIGHT, PLOT_W, PLOT_H } = CHART_DIMENSIONS;

const maxCadenceMinutes = computed(() =>
	isCadence.value
		? computeMaxCadenceMinutes(props.report.dailySeries, props.report.habit.defaultMinutesPerInterval)
		: 100
);

function getYFromMins(minutes: number): number {
	return getChartYFromMinutes(minutes, maxCadenceMinutes.value);
}

const rollingPolylinePoints = computed(() =>
	buildRollingPolylinePoints(props.report.dailySeries, isCadence.value, maxCadenceMinutes.value)
);

const rollingAreaPath = computed(() =>
	buildRollingAreaPath(props.report.dailySeries, isCadence.value, maxCadenceMinutes.value)
);

const inspectedPoint = computed<HabitDailyPoint | null>(() =>
	activePointIndex.value !== null ? props.report.dailySeries[activePointIndex.value] ?? null : null
);

const barWidth = computed(() => {
	const count = Math.max(1, props.report.dailySeries.length);
	return Math.max(4, Math.min(20, (PLOT_W / count) * 0.62));
});

const hitAreaWidth = computed(() => Math.max(barWidth.value, 12));

const renderedBars = computed(() => {
	const series = props.report.dailySeries;
	const total = series.length;
	const bw = barWidth.value;

	return series.map((pt, idx) => {
		const cx = getX(idx, total);
		const hasVisibleBar =
			(isCadence.value && pt.actualMinutes !== null && pt.actualMinutes > 0) ||
			(!isCadence.value && pt.score !== null && pt.score > 0) ||
			pt.outcome === 'failed' ||
			pt.outcome === 'skipped';

		const barY = isCadence.value
			? pt.actualMinutes !== null && pt.actualMinutes > 0
				? getYFromMins(pt.actualMinutes)
				: PAD_TOP + PLOT_H - 6
			: pt.score !== null && pt.score > 0
				? getYFromScore(pt.score)
				: pt.outcome === 'failed'
					? PAD_TOP + PLOT_H - 7
					: PAD_TOP + PLOT_H - 12;

		const barHeight = Math.max(0, PAD_TOP + PLOT_H - barY);
		const showAxisLabel =
			idx === 0 ||
			idx === total - 1 ||
			idx === Math.floor(total / 2) ||
			(total <= 14 && idx % 2 === 0);

		return {
			pt,
			idx,
			cx,
			hasVisibleBar,
			barX: cx - bw / 2,
			barY,
			barHeight,
			barRx: Math.min(5, bw / 2),
			showAxisLabel
		};
	});
});

function getBarColorVar(outcome: HabitDailyPoint['outcome']): string {
	if (outcome === 'gold') return 'var(--tier-gold)';
	if (outcome === 'silver') return 'var(--tier-silver)';
	if (outcome === 'bronze') return 'var(--tier-bronze)';
	if (outcome === 'failed') return 'var(--state-failed)';
	if (outcome === 'skipped') return 'var(--text-muted)';
	return 'var(--border-hairline)';
}
</script>

<template>
	<section class="rounded-3xl border border-border bg-surface p-5 shadow-(--shadow-soft) sm:p-6">
		<div class="flex flex-col justify-between gap-3 sm:flex-row sm:items-center">
			<div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
				<h2 class="text-base font-black text-fg sm:text-lg">
					روند زمانی «{{ report.habit.title }}»
				</h2>
				<span
					v-if="report.parentTitle"
					class="rounded-xl border border-border bg-elevated px-2.5 py-0.5 text-[11px] font-bold text-fg-secondary"
				>
					{{ report.parentTitle }}
				</span>
			</div>

			<div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 text-[11px] font-extrabold">
				<span class="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-gold-text">
					<span class="h-2.5 w-2.5 rounded-full bg-gold"></span>
					<span>{{ isCadence ? 'تکمیل هدف' : 'طلا' }}</span>
				</span>
				<span v-if="!isCadence && report.maxSupportedTier >= 2" class="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-silver-text">
					<span class="h-2.5 w-2.5 rounded-full bg-silver"></span>
					<span>نقره</span>
				</span>
				<span v-if="isCadence || report.maxSupportedTier > 1" class="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-bronze-text">
					<span class="h-2.5 w-2.5 rounded-full bg-bronze"></span>
					<span>{{ isCadence ? 'کمتر از هدف' : 'برنز' }}</span>
				</span>
				<span class="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-failed">
					<span class="h-2.5 w-2.5 rounded-full bg-failed"></span>
					<span>نشد</span>
				</span>
				<span class="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-accent-text">
					<span class="h-0.5 w-4 rounded-full bg-accent"></span>
					<span>{{ isCadence ? 'خط هدف' : 'میانگین روند' }}</span>
				</span>
			</div>
		</div>

		<div class="mt-5 overflow-x-auto pb-2" dir="ltr">
			<svg
				:viewBox="`0 0 ${SVG_WIDTH} ${SVG_HEIGHT}`"
				class="h-60 min-w-[600px] w-full select-none overflow-visible"
				role="img"
				aria-label="نمودار روند طرح در طول زمان"
			>
				<defs>
					<linearGradient id="trendAreaGrad" x1="0" y1="0" x2="0" y2="1">
						<stop offset="0%" stop-color="var(--surface-accent)" stop-opacity="0.24" />
						<stop offset="100%" stop-color="var(--surface-accent)" stop-opacity="0.0" />
					</linearGradient>
				</defs>

				<template v-if="isCadence">
					<g v-for="ratio in [0, 0.5, 1]" :key="ratio">
						<line
							:x1="PAD_LEFT"
							:y1="getYFromMins(Math.round(maxCadenceMinutes * ratio))"
							:x2="SVG_WIDTH - PAD_RIGHT"
							:y2="getYFromMins(Math.round(maxCadenceMinutes * ratio))"
							stroke="var(--border-hairline)"
							:stroke-dasharray="ratio === 0 ? 'none' : '4 4'"
							stroke-width="1"
						/>
						<text
							:x="SVG_WIDTH - PAD_RIGHT + 8"
							:y="getYFromMins(Math.round(maxCadenceMinutes * ratio)) + 4"
							fill="var(--text-muted)"
							font-size="10"
							font-weight="700"
						>
							{{ toPersianDigits(Math.round(maxCadenceMinutes * ratio)) }}′
						</text>
					</g>
				</template>

				<template v-else>
					<g
						v-for="grid in [
							{ score: 100, label: 'طلا' },
							{ score: 68, label: 'نقره' },
							{ score: 38, label: 'برنز' },
							{ score: 0, label: 'صفر' }
						]"
						:key="grid.score"
					>
						<line
							:x1="PAD_LEFT"
							:y1="getYFromScore(grid.score)"
							:x2="SVG_WIDTH - PAD_RIGHT"
							:y2="getYFromScore(grid.score)"
							stroke="var(--border-hairline)"
							:stroke-dasharray="grid.score === 0 ? 'none' : '4 4'"
							stroke-width="1"
						/>
						<text
							:x="SVG_WIDTH - PAD_RIGHT + 8"
							:y="getYFromScore(grid.score) + 4"
							fill="var(--text-muted)"
							font-size="10"
							font-weight="700"
						>
							{{ grid.label }}
						</text>
					</g>
				</template>

				<path v-if="rollingAreaPath" :d="rollingAreaPath" fill="url(#trendAreaGrad)" />

				<g
					v-for="item in renderedBars"
					:key="item.pt.dateIso"
					class="cursor-pointer transition-opacity"
					role="button"
					tabindex="0"
					:aria-label="item.pt.humanLabel"
					@click="manualPointIndex = item.idx"
					@mouseenter="manualPointIndex = item.idx"
				>
					<rect
						:x="item.cx - hitAreaWidth / 2"
						:y="PAD_TOP"
						:width="hitAreaWidth"
						:height="PLOT_H"
						fill="transparent"
					/>

					<line
						v-if="activePointIndex === item.idx"
						:x1="item.cx"
						:y1="PAD_TOP"
						:x2="item.cx"
						:y2="PAD_TOP + PLOT_H"
						stroke="var(--surface-accent)"
						stroke-width="1.5"
						stroke-dasharray="2 2"
					/>

					<rect
						v-if="item.hasVisibleBar"
						:x="item.barX"
						:y="item.barY"
						:width="barWidth"
						:height="item.barHeight"
						:rx="item.barRx"
						:fill="getBarColorVar(item.pt.outcome)"
						:opacity="activePointIndex === item.idx ? '1' : '0.82'"
					/>

					<circle
						v-else-if="item.pt.outcome === 'unlogged'"
						:cx="item.cx"
						:cy="PAD_TOP + PLOT_H - 2"
						r="2"
						fill="var(--border-hairline)"
					/>

					<circle
						v-if="item.pt.lifecycleEventsOnDay.length > 0"
						:cx="item.cx"
						:cy="PAD_TOP - 10"
						r="6"
						fill="var(--surface-accent)"
						stroke="var(--bg-surface)"
						stroke-width="2"
					/>

					<text
						v-if="item.showAxisLabel"
						:x="item.cx"
						:y="SVG_HEIGHT - 8"
						text-anchor="middle"
						:fill="activePointIndex === item.idx ? 'var(--text-primary)' : 'var(--text-muted)'"
						font-size="10"
						:font-weight="activePointIndex === item.idx ? '800' : '600'"
					>
						{{ toPersianDigits(item.pt.jalaliSlash.slice(5)) }}
					</text>
				</g>

				<polyline
					v-if="rollingPolylinePoints"
					fill="none"
					stroke="var(--surface-accent)"
					stroke-width="2.5"
					stroke-linecap="round"
					stroke-linejoin="round"
					:stroke-dasharray="isCadence ? '5 4' : 'none'"
					:points="rollingPolylinePoints"
				/>
			</svg>
		</div>

		<div
			v-if="inspectedPoint"
			class="mt-3 flex flex-col justify-between gap-3 rounded-2xl border border-border bg-elevated p-4 sm:flex-row sm:items-center"
		>
			<div class="space-y-1">
				<div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
					<span class="text-sm font-black text-fg">{{ inspectedPoint.humanLabel }}</span>
					<span
						class="rounded-xl border px-2.5 py-0.5 text-[11px] font-extrabold"
						:class="getOutcomeBadgeMeta(inspectedPoint.outcome, report.maxSupportedTier).badgeClass"
					>
						{{ getOutcomeBadgeMeta(inspectedPoint.outcome, report.maxSupportedTier).label }}
					</span>
					<span v-if="!isCadence && inspectedPoint.rollingAvgScore !== null" class="text-xs font-bold text-accent-text">
						(میانگین روند ۷ روزه: %{{ toPersianDigits(inspectedPoint.rollingAvgScore) }})
					</span>
				</div>

				<p v-if="inspectedPoint.ruleSnapshot" class="text-xs font-extrabold text-fg">
					{{ inspectedPoint.ruleSnapshot }}
				</p>
				<p v-else class="text-xs text-fg-muted">عملکردی ثبت نشده است.</p>

				<div v-if="inspectedPoint.lifecycleEventsOnDay.length > 0" class="mt-2 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 pt-1">
					<span
						v-for="ev in inspectedPoint.lifecycleEventsOnDay"
						:key="ev.id"
						class="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl border border-accent-border bg-accent-soft px-3 py-1 text-xs font-extrabold text-accent-text"
					>
						<GitCommitHorizontal class="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
						<span>{{ ev.summary }}</span>
					</span>
				</div>
			</div>

			<div v-if="inspectedPoint.dayTags.length > 0" class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5">
				<span
					v-for="tag in inspectedPoint.dayTags"
					:key="tag"
					class="rounded-xl border border-border bg-surface px-2.5 py-0.5 text-[11px] font-bold text-fg-secondary"
				>
					#{{ tag }}
				</span>
			</div>
		</div>
	</section>
</template>
