================================================================
PATH: app/components/analytics/HabitHeatmapMatrix.vue
================================================================

<script setup lang="ts">
import { computed } from 'vue';
import { toPersianDigits } from '~/domain/calendar';
import type { HabitDailyPoint, SelectedHabitAnalytics } from '~/domain/schemas';
import { Calendar, Hash, Layers, TrendingDown, TrendingUp } from 'lucide-vue-next';

interface Props {
	report: SelectedHabitAnalytics;
}

const props = defineProps<Props>();

const totalLogged = computed(() => Math.max(1, props.report.loggedDaysCount));
const goldPct = computed(() => Math.round((props.report.goldCount / totalLogged.value) * 100));
const silverPct = computed(() => Math.round((props.report.silverCount / totalLogged.value) * 100));
const bronzePct = computed(() => Math.round((props.report.bronzeCount / totalLogged.value) * 100));
const failedPct = computed(() => Math.round((props.report.failedCount / totalLogged.value) * 100));

function getCellClasses(outcome: HabitDailyPoint['outcome']): string {
	if (outcome === 'gold') return 'border-gold-border bg-gold text-on-solid';
	if (outcome === 'silver') return 'border-silver-border bg-silver text-on-solid';
	if (outcome === 'bronze') return 'border-bronze-border bg-bronze text-on-solid';
	if (outcome === 'failed') return 'border-failed-border bg-failed-soft text-failed';
	if (outcome === 'skipped') return 'border-border bg-muted text-fg-secondary';
	return 'border-border/50 bg-elevated/40 text-fg-muted';
}
</script>

<template>
	<div class="grid grid-cols-1 gap-6 lg:grid-cols-12">
		<div class="space-y-5 rounded-3xl border border-border bg-surface p-5 shadow-(--shadow-soft) sm:p-6 lg:col-span-7">
			<div class="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
				<div class="flex items-center gap-2">
					<Layers class="h-4.5 w-4.5 text-accent-text" />
					<h3 class="text-base font-black text-fg">کالبدشکافی سطوح و ماتریس روزها</h3>
				</div>
				<span class="text-xs font-bold text-fg-muted">
					{{ toPersianDigits(report.loggedDaysCount) }} روز ثبت‌شده
				</span>
			</div>

			<div v-if="report.loggedDaysCount > 0" class="space-y-2">
				<div class="flex h-4 w-full overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-elevated p-0.5">
					<div
						v-if="report.goldCount > 0"
						class="h-full bg-gold transition-all duration-300 first:rounded-r-full last:rounded-l-full"
						:style="{ width: `${goldPct}%` }"
						:title="`طلا: ${toPersianDigits(report.goldCount)} روز (%${toPersianDigits(goldPct)})`"
					></div>
					<div
						v-if="report.silverCount > 0"
						class="h-full bg-silver transition-all duration-300 first:rounded-r-full last:rounded-l-full"
						:style="{ width: `${silverPct}%` }"
						:title="`نقره: ${toPersianDigits(report.silverCount)} روز (%${toPersianDigits(silverPct)})`"
					></div>
					<div
						v-if="report.bronzeCount > 0"
						class="h-full bg-bronze transition-all duration-300 first:rounded-r-full last:rounded-l-full"
						:style="{ width: `${bronzePct}%` }"
						:title="`برنز: ${toPersianDigits(report.bronzeCount)} روز (%${toPersianDigits(bronzePct)})`"
					></div>
					<div
						v-if="report.failedCount > 0"
						class="h-full bg-failed transition-all duration-300 first:rounded-r-full last:rounded-l-full"
						:style="{ width: `${failedPct}%` }"
						:title="`عدم انجام: ${toPersianDigits(report.failedCount)} روز (%${toPersianDigits(failedPct)})`"
					></div>
				</div>

				<div class="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between text-[11px] font-bold text-fg-secondary px-1">
					<span class="text-gold-text">طلا: %{{ toPersianDigits(goldPct) }}</span>
					<span v-if="report.silverCount > 0" class="text-silver-text">نقره: %{{ toPersianDigits(silverPct) }}</span>
					<span class="text-bronze-text">برنز: %{{ toPersianDigits(bronzePct) }}</span>
					<span class="text-failed">نشد: %{{ toPersianDigits(failedPct) }}</span>
				</div>
			</div>

			<div class="space-y-2 border-t border-border pt-4">
				<div class="text-xs font-extrabold text-fg-secondary">
					ماتریس روزهای بازه
				</div>
				<div class="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
					<div
						v-for="pt in report.dailySeries"
						:key="pt.dateIso"
						class="flex h-8 w-8 items-center justify-center rounded-xl border text-[11px] font-black transition hover:scale-110"
						:class="getCellClasses(pt.outcome)"
						:title="`${pt.humanLabel} — ${pt.ruleSnapshot || 'ثبت‌نشده'}`"
					>
						{{ toPersianDigits(pt.jalaliSlash.slice(8)) }}
					</div>
				</div>
			</div>
		</div>

		<div class="space-y-5 rounded-3xl border border-border bg-surface p-5 shadow-(--shadow-soft) sm:p-6 lg:col-span-5">
			<div class="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
				<div class="flex items-center gap-2">
					<Calendar class="h-4.5 w-4.5 text-accent-text" />
					<h3 class="text-base font-black text-fg">الگوی روزهای هفته و عوامل موثر</h3>
				</div>
			</div>

			<div class="space-y-2.5">
				<div v-for="w in report.weekdayStats" :key="w.weekdayIndex" class="flex items-center gap-3 text-xs">
					<span class="w-16 shrink-0 font-bold text-fg-secondary">{{ w.weekdayLabel }}</span>
					<div class="h-2.5 flex-1 overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-elevated">
						<div
							class="h-full rounded-full transition-all duration-300"
							:class="w.avgScore >= 80 ? 'bg-optimal' : w.avgScore >= 50 ? 'bg-accent' : w.avgScore > 0 ? 'bg-bronze' : 'bg-transparent'"
							:style="{ width: `${w.avgScore}%` }"
						></div>
					</div>
					<span class="w-12 shrink-0 text-left font-mono font-extrabold text-fg">
						{{ w.loggedDays > 0 ? `%${toPersianDigits(w.avgScore)}` : '—' }}
					</span>
				</div>
			</div>

			<div v-if="report.bestWeekday || report.weakestWeekday" class="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2.5 border-t border-border pt-3.5 text-xs">
				<div v-if="report.bestWeekday" class="flex items-center justify-between rounded-2xl border border-optimal-border bg-optimal-soft p-3">
					<div>
						<div class="text-[11px] font-bold text-optimal">بهترین روز</div>
						<div class="mt-0.5 font-black text-fg">{{ report.bestWeekday.weekdayLabel }}</div>
					</div>
					<span class="font-mono text-sm font-black text-optimal">%{{ toPersianDigits(report.bestWeekday.avgScore) }}</span>
				</div>
				<div v-if="report.weakestWeekday && report.weakestWeekday.weekdayIndex !== report.bestWeekday?.weekdayIndex" class="flex items-center justify-between rounded-2xl border border-failed-border bg-failed-soft p-3">
					<div>
						<div class="text-[11px] font-bold text-failed">ضعیف‌ترین روز</div>
						<div class="mt-0.5 font-black text-fg">{{ report.weakestWeekday.weekdayLabel }}</div>
					</div>
					<span class="font-mono text-sm font-black text-failed">%{{ toPersianDigits(report.weakestWeekday.avgScore) }}</span>
				</div>
			</div>

			<div v-if="report.tagCorrelations.length > 0" class="space-y-2 border-t border-border pt-3.5">
				<div class="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-extrabold text-fg-secondary">
					<Hash class="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-accent-text" />
					<span>تاثیر تگ‌ها</span>
				</div>
				<div class="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
					<span
						v-for="item in report.tagCorrelations.slice(0, 6)"
						:key="item.tag"
						class="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl border px-2.5 py-1 text-[11px] font-extrabold"
						:class="item.impactDelta >= 0 ? 'border-optimal-border bg-optimal-soft text-optimal' : 'border-failed-border bg-failed-soft text-failed'"
					>
						<TrendingUp v-if="item.impactDelta >= 0" class="h-3 w-3" />
						<TrendingDown v-else class="h-3 w-3" />
						<span>#{{ item.tag }}</span>
						<span dir="ltr" class="font-mono">({{ item.impactDelta > 0 ? '+' : '' }}{{ toPersianDigits(item.impactDelta) }}%)</span>
					</span>
				</div>
			</div>
		</div>
	</div>
</template>

================================================================
PATH: app/components/analytics/HabitsComparisonTable.vue
================================================================

<script setup lang="ts">
import { computed } from 'vue';
import { toPersianDigits } from '~/domain/calendar';
import type { HabitComparisonRow } from '~/domain/schemas';
import { BarChart3, Flame, TrendingDown, TrendingUp } from 'lucide-vue-next';

interface Props {
	rows: HabitComparisonRow[];
	selectedHabitId?: string;
}

const props = defineProps<Props>();

const emit = defineEmits<{
	(e: 'selectHabit', habitId: string): void;
}>();

const sortedRows = computed(() => [...props.rows].sort((a, b) => b.avgQualityScore - a.avgQualityScore));
</script>

<template>
	<section class="rounded-3xl border border-border bg-surface p-5 shadow-(--shadow-soft) sm:p-6">
		<div class="mb-4 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2">
			<div class="flex items-center gap-2.5">
				<div class="flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-xl border border-accent-border bg-accent-soft text-accent-text">
					<BarChart3 class="h-5 w-5" />
				</div>
				<h3 class="text-base font-black text-fg">مقایسه همه طرح‌ها</h3>
			</div>
		</div>

		<div class="space-y-2.5">
			<button
				v-for="row in sortedRows"
				:key="row.habit.id"
				type="button"
				class="flex w-full flex-col justify-between gap-3 rounded-2xl border p-3.5 text-right transition sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:px-4"
				:class="
					row.habit.id === selectedHabitId
						? 'border-accent bg-accent-soft/50 ring-1 ring-accent-border'
						: 'border-border bg-elevated hover:border-border-strong'
				"
				@click="emit('selectHabit', row.habit.id)"
			>
				<div class="min-w-0 flex-1 space-y-1">
					<div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
						<span class="text-sm font-black text-fg">{{ row.habit.title }}</span>
						<span v-if="row.parentTitle" class="rounded-lg border border-border bg-surface px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-bold text-fg-muted">
							{{ row.parentTitle }}
						</span>
						<span v-if="row.habit.status === 'archived'" class="rounded-lg border border-border bg-surface px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-bold text-fg-muted">
							بایگانی
						</span>
					</div>

					<div class="flex h-2 w-full max-w-md overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-surface">
						<div v-if="row.goldCount > 0" class="h-full bg-gold" :style="{ width: `${Math.round((row.goldCount / Math.max(1, row.loggedDays)) * 100)}%` }"></div>
						<div v-if="row.silverCount > 0" class="h-full bg-silver" :style="{ width: `${Math.round((row.silverCount / Math.max(1, row.loggedDays)) * 100)}%` }"></div>
						<div v-if="row.bronzeCount > 0" class="h-full bg-bronze" :style="{ width: `${Math.round((row.bronzeCount / Math.max(1, row.loggedDays)) * 100)}%` }"></div>
						<div v-if="row.failedCount > 0" class="h-full bg-failed" :style="{ width: `${Math.round((row.failedCount / Math.max(1, row.loggedDays)) * 100)}%` }"></div>
					</div>
				</div>

				<div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 text-xs sm:justify-end">
					<div class="flex items-center gap-1 font-bold text-fg-secondary" title="رکورد فعلی">
						<Flame class="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-bronze-text" />
						<span>{{ toPersianDigits(row.currentStreak) }} روز</span>
					</div>

					<div class="rounded-xl border border-border bg-surface px-2.5 py-1 font-mono font-black text-fg">
						استمرار: %{{ toPersianDigits(row.consistencyRate) }}
					</div>

					<div
						class="w-16 text-center font-mono font-black"
						:class="row.avgQualityScore >= 80 ? 'text-gold-text' : row.avgQualityScore >= 50 ? 'text-accent-text' : 'text-fg-secondary'"
					>
						امتیاز {{ toPersianDigits(row.avgQualityScore) }}
					</div>

					<span
						v-if="row.trendDelta !== 0"
						class="inline-flex w-14 items-center justify-center gap-0.5 rounded-lg py-0.5 font-mono text-[11px] font-black"
						:class="row.trendDelta > 0 ? 'bg-optimal-soft text-optimal' : 'bg-failed-soft text-failed'"
					>
						<TrendingUp v-if="row.trendDelta > 0" class="h-3 w-3" />
						<TrendingDown v-else class="h-3 w-3" />
						<span>{{ row.trendDelta > 0 ? '+' : '' }}{{ toPersianDigits(row.trendDelta) }}</span>
					</span>
				</div>
			</button>
		</div>
	</section>
</template>

<script setup lang="ts">
import { toPersianDigits } from '~/domain/calendar';
import type { HabitEvolutionItem } from '~/domain/schemas';
import { ArrowLeft, GitCommitHorizontal, TrendingDown, TrendingUp } from 'lucide-vue-next';

interface Props {
	habitTitle: string;
	evolutionTimeline: HabitEvolutionItem[];
}

defineProps<Props>();
</script>

<template>
	<section class="rounded-3xl border border-border bg-surface p-5 shadow-(--shadow-soft) sm:p-6">
		<div class="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 border-b border-border pb-4">
			<div class="flex items-center gap-2.5">
				<div class="flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-xl border border-accent-border bg-accent-soft text-accent-text">
					<GitCommitHorizontal class="h-5 w-5" />
				</div>
				<h3 class="text-base font-black text-fg">تاریخچه تغییرات «{{ habitTitle }}»</h3>
			</div>

			<span class="rounded-xl border border-border bg-elevated px-3 py-1 text-xs font-extrabold text-fg-secondary">
				{{ toPersianDigits(evolutionTimeline.length) }} رویداد ثبت‌شده
			</span>
		</div>

		<div v-if="evolutionTimeline.length === 0" class="mt-4 rounded-2xl border border-dashed border-border bg-elevated p-6 text-center text-xs font-bold text-fg-muted">
			برای این طرح هنوز تغییر قوانین یا رویداد تکاملی در تاریخچه ثبت نشده است.
		</div>

		<div v-else class="relative mt-5 space-y-4 before:absolute before:top-3 before:right-4 before:bottom-3 before:w-0.5 before:bg-border">
			<div v-for="item in evolutionTimeline" :key="item.id" class="relative pr-10">
				<div class="absolute top-3.5 right-2.5 h-3.5 w-3.5 rounded-full border-2 border-accent bg-surface"></div>

				<div class="rounded-2xl border border-border bg-elevated p-4 transition hover:border-border-strong">
					<div class="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2">
						<div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
							<span class="rounded-lg border px-2.5 py-0.5 text-[11px] font-extrabold" :class="item.badgeClasses">
								{{ item.eventTypeLabel }}
							</span>
							<span class="text-sm font-black text-fg">{{ item.summary }}</span>
						</div>

						<div class="flex items-center gap-2">
							<span
								v-if="item.impactDelta !== null && item.beforeSuccessRate !== null && item.afterSuccessRate !== null"
								class="inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-xl border px-2.5 py-1 text-[11px] font-black"
								:class="item.impactDelta >= 0 ? 'border-optimal-border bg-optimal-soft text-optimal' : 'border-failed-border bg-failed-soft text-failed'"
								title="مقایسه کیفیت ۱۴ روز قبل و ۱۴ روز بعد از این تغییر"
							>
								<TrendingUp v-if="item.impactDelta >= 0" class="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
								<TrendingDown v-else class="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
								<span>قبل: %{{ toPersianDigits(item.beforeSuccessRate) }} ← بعد: %{{ toPersianDigits(item.afterSuccessRate) }}</span>
							</span>
							<span class="font-mono text-xs font-bold text-fg-muted">{{ item.humanDateLabel }}</span>
						</div>
					</div>

					<div v-if="item.diffs.length > 0" class="mt-3 grid grid-cols-1 gap-2 sm:grid-cols-2">
						<div
							v-for="diff in item.diffs"
							:key="diff.fieldLabel"
							class="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 rounded-xl border border-border bg-surface px-3 py-2 text-xs"
						>
							<span class="font-extrabold text-fg-secondary">{{ diff.fieldLabel }}:</span>
							<div class="flex items-center gap-1.5 font-bold">
								<span class="text-fg-muted line-through">{{ diff.previousValue }}</span>
								<ArrowLeft class="h-3 w-3 text-accent-text" />
								<span class="font-extrabold text-accent-text">{{ diff.newValue }}</span>
							</div>
						</div>
					</div>

					<div v-if="item.reason" class="mt-2.5 text-xs text-fg-secondary">
						<strong class="font-extrabold text-fg">دلیل تغییر: </strong>
						<span>{{ item.reason }}</span>
					</div>
				</div>
			</div>
		</div>
	</section>

PATH: app/components/analytics/AnalyticsSummaryCards.vue

<script setup lang="ts">
import { computed } from 'vue';
import { toPersianDigits } from '~/domain/calendar';
import type { SelectedHabitAnalytics } from '~/domain/schemas';
import { Award, Flame, TrendingDown, TrendingUp, Zap } from 'lucide-vue-next';

interface Props {
	report: SelectedHabitAnalytics;
}

const props = defineProps<Props>();

const isCadence = computed(() => props.report.habit.trackingMode === 'interval_cadence');
</script>

<template>
	<div class="grid grid-cols-1 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-3 sm:gap-4">
		<div class="rounded-3xl border border-border bg-surface p-4 shadow-(--shadow-card) sm:p-5">
			<div class="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
				<span class="text-xs font-extrabold text-fg-secondary">نرخ استمرار طرح</span>
				<div class="flex h-8 w-8 items-center justify-center rounded-xl border border-optimal-border bg-optimal-soft text-optimal">
					<Zap class="h-4 w-4" />
				</div>
			</div>

			<div class="mt-3 flex items-baseline gap-2">
				<span class="font-mono text-2xl font-black text-fg sm:text-3xl">
					%{{ toPersianDigits(report.consistencyRate) }}
				</span>
				<span
					v-if="report.trendDelta !== 0"
					class="inline-flex items-center gap-0.5 rounded-lg px-2 py-0.5 text-[11px] font-black"
					:class="report.trendDelta > 0 ? 'bg-optimal-soft text-optimal' : 'bg-failed-soft text-failed'"
					title="تغییر کیفیت نسبت به نیمه اول بازه"
				>
					<TrendingUp v-if="report.trendDelta > 0" class="h-3 w-3" />
					<TrendingDown v-else class="h-3 w-3" />
					<span>{{ report.trendDelta > 0 ? '+' : '' }}{{ toPersianDigits(report.trendDelta) }}%</span>
				</span>
			</div>

			<p class="mt-1.5 text-[11px] font-bold text-fg-muted">
				{{ toPersianDigits(report.successDaysCount) }} روز انجام از {{ toPersianDigits(report.loggedDaysCount) }} روز ثبت‌شده
			</p>
		</div>

		<div class="rounded-3xl border border-border bg-surface p-4 shadow-(--shadow-card) sm:p-5">
			<div class="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
				<span class="text-xs font-extrabold text-fg-secondary">
					{{ isCadence ? 'میانگین دقایق روزانه' : 'نرخ دست‌یابی به اوج (طلا)' }}
				</span>
				<div class="flex h-8 w-8 items-center justify-center rounded-xl border border-gold-border bg-gold-soft text-gold-text">
					<Award class="h-4 w-4" />
				</div>
			</div>

			<div class="mt-3 flex items-baseline gap-2">
				<template v-if="isCadence">
					<span class="font-mono text-2xl font-black text-gold-text sm:text-3xl">
						{{ toPersianDigits(report.avgActualMinutes) }}
					</span>
					<span class="text-xs font-bold text-fg-secondary">
						از هدف {{ toPersianDigits(report.avgPlannedMinutes) }} دقیقه
					</span>
				</template>
				<template v-else>
					<span class="font-mono text-2xl font-black text-gold-text sm:text-3xl">
						%{{ toPersianDigits(report.goldRate) }}
					</span>
					<span class="text-xs font-bold text-fg-secondary">
						({{ toPersianDigits(report.goldCount) }} بار سطح کامل)
					</span>
				</template>
			</div>

			<p class="mt-1.5 text-[11px] font-bold text-fg-muted">
				<template v-if="isCadence">
					مجموع کل: {{ toPersianDigits(report.totalActualMinutes) }} دقیقه در این بازه
				</template>
				<template v-else>
					میانگین شاخص کیفیت: %{{ toPersianDigits(report.avgQualityScore) }}
				</template>
			</p>
		</div>

		<div class="rounded-3xl border border-border bg-surface p-4 shadow-(--shadow-card) sm:p-5">
			<div class="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
				<span class="text-xs font-extrabold text-fg-secondary">زنجیره پیوستگی (Streak)</span>
				<div class="flex h-8 w-8 items-center justify-center rounded-xl border border-bronze-border bg-bronze-soft text-bronze-text">
					<Flame class="h-4 w-4" />
				</div>
			</div>

			<div class="mt-3 flex items-baseline gap-2">
				<span class="font-mono text-2xl font-black text-fg sm:text-3xl">
					{{ toPersianDigits(report.currentStreak) }}
				</span>
				<span class="text-xs font-bold text-fg-secondary">روز پیاپی فعلی</span>
			</div>

			<p class="mt-1.5 text-[11px] font-bold text-fg-muted">
				بهترین رکورد ثبت‌شده: {{ toPersianDigits(report.bestStreak) }} روز بدون شکست
			</p>
		</div>
	</div>
</template>

PATH: app/components/notes/NoteItemCard.vue

<script setup lang="ts">
import { ref, computed, onUnmounted } from 'vue';
import { detectNoteSmartSignals } from '~/core/note-signals';
import { isoToJalaliSlash, toPersianDigits } from '~/domain/calendar';
import type { NoteEntity } from '~/domain/schemas';
import { Check, Copy, ExternalLink, Pencil, Pin, Trash2 } from 'lucide-vue-next';

interface Props {
	note: NoteEntity;
}

const props = defineProps<Props>();

const emit = defineEmits<{
	(e: 'edit', note: NoteEntity): void;
	(e: 'togglePin', noteId: string, isPinned: boolean): void;
	(e: 'delete', noteId: string): void;
}>();

const copied = ref(false);
let copyTimer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null = null;

const signals = computed(() => detectNoteSmartSignals(`${props.note.title} ${props.note.content}`));

async function copyContent() {
	try {
		await navigator.clipboard.writeText(
			props.note.title ? `${props.note.title}\n${props.note.content}` : props.note.content
		);
		copied.value = true;
		if (copyTimer) clearTimeout(copyTimer);
		copyTimer = setTimeout(() => {
			copied.value = false;
			copyTimer = null;
		}, 1600);
	} catch {
		// ignore
	}
}

onUnmounted(() => {
	if (copyTimer) clearTimeout(copyTimer);
});
</script>

<template>
	<article
		class="group flex flex-col justify-between rounded-3xl border p-5 shadow-(--shadow-soft) transition hover:border-border-strong"
		:class="
			note.isPinned
				? 'border-accent-border bg-surface ring-1 ring-accent-border/40'
				: 'border-border bg-surface'
		"
	>
		<div>
			<div class="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
				<span class="truncate rounded-xl border border-border bg-elevated px-2.5 py-1 text-[11px] font-extrabold text-fg-secondary">
					{{ note.category }}
				</span>

				<div class="flex shrink-0 items-center gap-1">
					<button
						type="button"
						class="flex h-8 w-8 items-center justify-center rounded-xl text-fg-muted transition hover:bg-elevated hover:text-fg active:scale-95"
						title="کپی متن"
						@click="copyContent"
					>
						<Check v-if="copied" class="h-4 w-4 text-optimal" />
						<Copy v-else class="h-4 w-4" />
					</button>

					<button
						type="button"
						class="flex h-8 w-8 items-center justify-center rounded-xl transition active:scale-95"
						:class="
							note.isPinned
								? 'border border-accent-border bg-accent-soft text-accent-text'
								: 'text-fg-muted hover:bg-elevated hover:text-fg'
						"
						:title="note.isPinned ? 'برداشتن سنجاق' : 'سنجاق به بالای لیست'"
						@click="emit('togglePin', note.id, !note.isPinned)"
					>
						<Pin class="h-4 w-4" :class="{ 'fill-current': note.isPinned }" />
					</button>
				</div>
			</div>

			<h3 v-if="note.title" class="mt-3 text-base font-black leading-snug text-fg">{{ note.title }}</h3>

			<p
				dir="rtl"
				class="mt-2.5 line-clamp-6 whitespace-pre-line break-words text-right text-xs leading-6 font-medium text-fg-secondary select-text"
			>
				{{ note.content }}
			</p>
		</div>

		<div class="mt-4 flex items-center justify-between gap-2 border-t border-border pt-3 text-xs text-fg-muted">
			<span dir="ltr" class="shrink-0 font-mono text-[11px] font-bold">
				{{ toPersianDigits(isoToJalaliSlash(note.updatedAtIso.slice(0, 10))) }}
			</span>

			<div class="flex shrink-0 items-center gap-1">
				<a
					v-if="signals.firstUrl"
					:href="signals.firstUrl"
					target="_blank"
					rel="noopener noreferrer"
					class="flex h-8 shrink-0 items-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl border border-accent-border bg-accent-soft px-2.5 text-[11px] font-extrabold whitespace-nowrap text-accent-text transition hover:opacity-90 active:scale-95"
					title="باز کردن لینک"
				>
					<span>باز کردن لینک</span>
					<ExternalLink class="h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0" />
				</a>

				<button
					type="button"
					class="flex h-8 w-8 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl text-fg-muted transition hover:bg-elevated hover:text-fg active:scale-95"
					title="ویرایش یادداشت"
					@click="emit('edit', note)"
				>
					<Pencil class="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
				</button>

				<button
					type="button"
					class="flex h-8 w-8 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl text-fg-muted transition hover:bg-failed-soft hover:text-failed active:scale-95"
					title="حذف یادداشت"
					@click="emit('delete', note.id)"
				>
					<Trash2 class="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
				</button>
			</div>
		</div>
	</article>
</template>

PATH: app/components/notes/NoteUpsertModal.vue

<script setup lang="ts">
import { ref } from 'vue';
import type { NoteEntity, UpsertNoteInput } from '~/domain/schemas';
import BaseModal from '~/components/ui/BaseModal.vue';
import { Tag } from 'lucide-vue-next';

interface Props {
	editingNote?: NoteEntity | null;
	existingCategories: string[];
	isSubmitting?: boolean;
}

const props = withDefaults(defineProps<Props>(), {
	editingNote: null,
	existingCategories: () => [],
	isSubmitting: false
});

const emit = defineEmits<{
	(e: 'close'): void;
	(e: 'submit', payload: UpsertNoteInput): void;
}>();

const title = ref(props.editingNote?.title ?? '');
const content = ref(props.editingNote?.content ?? '');
const category = ref(props.editingNote?.category || 'عمومی');

function handleSubmit() {
	if (!content.value.trim() || props.isSubmitting) return;

	emit('submit', {
		noteId: props.editingNote?.id,
		title: title.value.trim(),
		content: content.value.trim(),
		category: category.value.trim() || 'عمومی'
	});
}
</script>

<template>
	<BaseModal :title="editingNote ? 'ویرایش یادداشت' : 'افزودن یادداشت جدید'" @close="emit('close')">
		<form class="space-y-4 overflow-y-auto p-5 sm:p-6" @submit.prevent="handleSubmit">
			<div class="flex flex-col gap-3 sm:flex-row sm:items-center">
				<div class="flex-1">
					<label for="note-title" class="mb-1 block text-xs font-bold text-fg-secondary">عنوان (اختیاری)</label>
					<input
						id="note-title"
						v-model="title"
						placeholder="مثلاً: سایت ابزار هوش مصنوعی / شماره پیگیری..."
						class="h-10 w-full rounded-xl border border-border bg-elevated px-3.5 text-xs font-extrabold text-fg placeholder:font-normal placeholder:text-fg-muted focus:border-border-strong focus:outline-none"
					/>
				</div>

				<div class="w-full sm:w-36">
					<label for="note-cat" class="mb-1 flex items-center gap-1 text-xs font-bold text-fg-secondary">
						<Tag class="h-3 w-3 text-fg-muted" />
						<span>دسته‌بندی</span>
					</label>
					<input
						id="note-cat"
						v-model="category"
						list="note-categories-list"
						placeholder="عمومی"
						class="h-10 w-full rounded-xl border border-border bg-elevated px-3 text-xs font-extrabold text-fg placeholder:font-normal placeholder:text-fg-muted focus:border-border-strong focus:outline-none"
					/>
					<datalist id="note-categories-list">
						<option v-for="cat in existingCategories" :key="cat" :value="cat" />
					</datalist>
				</div>
			</div>

			<div>
				<label for="note-content" class="mb-1.5 block text-xs font-bold text-fg-secondary">
					متن یادداشت، لینک یا شماره
				</label>
				<textarea
					id="note-content"
					v-model="content"
					required
					rows="5"
					placeholder="هر لینک، شماره، دستور یا نکته‌ای که می‌خواهید ذخیره شود را اینجا بنویسید..."
					class="w-full rounded-2xl border border-border bg-elevated p-3.5 text-sm leading-7 text-fg placeholder:text-fg-muted focus:border-border-strong focus:bg-surface focus:outline-none"
				></textarea>
			</div>

			<div class="flex items-center justify-end gap-2 pt-1">
				<button
					type="button"
					class="h-10 rounded-xl px-4 text-xs font-bold text-fg-secondary hover:bg-elevated"
					@click="emit('close')"
				>
					انصراف
				</button>
				<button
					type="submit"
					:disabled="isSubmitting"
					class="h-10 rounded-xl bg-accent px-6 text-xs font-extrabold text-accent-fg shadow-xs transition hover:opacity-90 disabled:opacity-60"
				>
					{{ isSubmitting ? 'در حال ثبت...' : editingNote ? 'ذخیره تغییرات' : 'ثبت یادداشت' }}
				</button>
			</div>
		</form>
	</BaseModal>
</template>

================================================================
PATH: app/components/architect/HabitUpsertModal.vue
================================================================

<script setup lang="ts">
import { ref, computed } from 'vue';
import type { CreateHabitInput, HabitEntity, HabitStatus, HabitTimeSlot, MutateHabitStructureInput } from '~/domain/schemas';
import BaseModal from '~/components/ui/BaseModal.vue';
import CustomSelect from '~/components/ui/CustomSelect.vue';

type FormStructureKind = 'tiered' | 'composite' | 'interval_cadence';

interface Props {
	mode: 'create' | 'edit';
	editingHabit?: HabitEntity | null;
	hasSubHabits?: boolean;
	initialParentId?: string;
	rootHabits: HabitEntity[];
	isSubmitting?: boolean;
}

const props = withDefaults(defineProps<Props>(), {
	editingHabit: null,
	hasSubHabits: false,
	initialParentId: '',
	isSubmitting: false
});

const emit = defineEmits<{
	(e: 'close'): void;
	(e: 'create', payload: CreateHabitInput): void;
	(e: 'update', payload: MutateHabitStructureInput): void;
}>();

const structureKind = ref<FormStructureKind>(
	props.mode === 'edit' && props.editingHabit
		? props.editingHabit.trackingMode === 'interval_cadence'
			? 'interval_cadence'
			: props.hasSubHabits || (!props.editingHabit.bronzeDesc && !props.editingHabit.parentId)
				? 'composite'
				: 'tiered'
		: 'tiered'
);

const selectedParentId = ref(
	props.mode === 'edit' && props.editingHabit ? (props.editingHabit.parentId ?? '') : props.initialParentId
);
const selectedStatus = ref<HabitStatus>(
	props.mode === 'edit' && props.editingHabit ? props.editingHabit.status : 'active'
);
const selectedTimeSlot = ref<HabitTimeSlot>(
	props.mode === 'edit' && props.editingHabit ? (props.editingHabit.timeSlot ?? 'day') : 'day'
);
const isSituational = ref(
	props.mode === 'edit' && props.editingHabit ? Boolean(props.editingHabit.isSituational) : false
);

const title = ref(props.editingHabit?.title ?? '');
const scheduleLabel = ref(props.editingHabit?.scheduleLabel ?? '');
const bronzeDesc = ref(props.editingHabit?.bronzeDesc ?? '');
const silverDesc = ref(props.editingHabit?.silverDesc ?? '');
const goldDesc = ref(props.editingHabit?.goldDesc ?? '');
const emergencyMinDesc = ref(props.editingHabit?.emergencyMinDesc ?? '');
const notes = ref(props.editingHabit?.notes ?? '');
const reason = ref('');

const modalTitle = computed(() =>
	props.mode === 'edit' && props.editingHabit
		? `ویرایش «${props.editingHabit.title}»`
		: selectedParentId.value
			? 'افزودن زیرطرح جدید'
			: 'افزودن طرح جدید'
);

const eligibleParents = computed(() =>
	props.rootHabits.filter(
		(h) => h.trackingMode === 'tiered' && (props.mode === 'create' || h.id !== props.editingHabit?.id)
	)
);

const structureKindOptions = computed(() => {
	const base = [
		{ value: 'tiered', label: 'طرح سطح‌بندی شده (برنز / نقره / طلا)' },
		{ value: 'composite', label: 'مجموعه‌طرح (شامل چند زیرطرح)' }
	];
	if (props.mode === 'create' || props.editingHabit?.trackingMode === 'interval_cadence') {
		base.push({
			value: 'interval_cadence',
			label: 'طرح تکرارشونده (هدف‌گذاری از شب قبل)'
		});
	}
	return base;
});

const parentOptions = computed(() => [
	{ value: '', label: 'طرح اصلی و مستقل' },
	...eligibleParents.value.map((p) => ({ value: p.id, label: `زیرمجموعهٔ «${p.title}»` }))
]);

const statusOptions = [
	{ value: 'active', label: 'فعال' },
	{ value: 'paused', label: 'متوقف موقت' },
	{ value: 'archived', label: 'حذف / بایگانی' }
] as const;

const timeSlotOptions = [
	{ value: 'morning', label: 'صبحگاهی' },
	{ value: 'day', label: 'در طول روز' },
	{ value: 'night', label: 'شبانه' }
] as const;

function handleSubmit() {
	if (!title.value.trim() || props.isSubmitting) return;

	if (props.mode === 'edit' && props.editingHabit) {
		emit('update', {
			habitId: props.editingHabit.id,
			title: title.value.trim(),
			parentId: selectedParentId.value || null,
			category: props.editingHabit.category,
			timeSlot: selectedTimeSlot.value,
			isSituational: isSituational.value,
			status: selectedStatus.value,
			scheduleLabel: scheduleLabel.value.trim() || null,
			notes: notes.value.trim() || null,
			bronzeDesc: bronzeDesc.value.trim() || null,
			silverDesc: silverDesc.value.trim() || null,
			goldDesc: goldDesc.value.trim() || null,
			emergencyMinDesc: emergencyMinDesc.value.trim() || null,
			reason: reason.value.trim() || 'ویرایش قوانین طرح'
		});
	} else {
		emit('create', {
			title: title.value.trim(),
			parentId: selectedParentId.value || null,
			category: 'عمومی',
			trackingMode: structureKind.value === 'interval_cadence' ? 'interval_cadence' : 'tiered',
			timeSlot: selectedTimeSlot.value,
			isSituational: isSituational.value,
			scheduleLabel: scheduleLabel.value.trim() || undefined,
			notes: notes.value.trim() || undefined,
			bronzeDesc: bronzeDesc.value.trim() || undefined,
			silverDesc: silverDesc.value.trim() || undefined,
			goldDesc: goldDesc.value.trim() || undefined,
			emergencyMinDesc: emergencyMinDesc.value.trim() || undefined,
			defaultIntervals: 1,
			defaultMinutesPerInterval: 10,
			cadenceWindowMinutes: 60,
			reason: reason.value.trim() || undefined
		});
	}
}
</script>

<template>
	<BaseModal :title="modalTitle" max-width-class="max-w-xl" @close="emit('close')">
		<form class="space-y-4 overflow-y-auto p-4 sm:space-y-5 sm:p-8" @submit.prevent="handleSubmit">
			<div class="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
				<div>
					<label for="h-title" class="mb-1.5 block text-sm font-bold text-fg">عنوان طرح</label>
					<input
						id="h-title"
						v-model="title"
						required
						placeholder="مثلاً: مطالعه شبانه"
						class="h-12 w-full rounded-2xl border border-border bg-elevated px-4 text-base font-bold text-fg focus:border-border-strong focus:outline-none"
					/>
				</div>

				<div>
					<label for="h-sched" class="mb-1.5 block text-sm font-bold text-fg">زمان انجام (اختیاری)</label>
					<input
						id="h-sched"
						v-model="scheduleLabel"
						placeholder="مثلاً: بعد از نماز صبح"
						class="h-12 w-full rounded-2xl border border-border bg-elevated px-4 text-base text-fg focus:border-border-strong focus:outline-none"
					/>
				</div>
			</div>

			<div class="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
				<div v-if="!selectedParentId">
					<label for="h-kind" class="mb-1.5 block text-sm font-bold text-fg">نوع طرح</label>
					<CustomSelect
						id="h-kind"
						v-model="structureKind"
						:disabled="hasSubHabits"
						:options="structureKindOptions"
						trigger-class="h-12 text-fg"
					/>
				</div>

				<div v-if="structureKind === 'tiered'">
					<label for="h-parent" class="mb-1.5 block text-sm font-bold text-fg">جایگاه در ساختار</label>
					<CustomSelect
						id="h-parent"
						v-model="selectedParentId"
						:options="parentOptions"
						trigger-class="h-12 text-fg"
					/>
				</div>

				<div v-if="structureKind !== 'interval_cadence' && !selectedParentId">
					<label for="h-timeslot" class="mb-1.5 block text-sm font-bold text-fg">بازهٔ زمانی در روز</label>
					<CustomSelect
						id="h-timeslot"
						v-model="selectedTimeSlot"
						:options="timeSlotOptions"
						trigger-class="h-12 text-fg"
					/>
				</div>

				<div v-if="mode === 'edit' && editingHabit">
					<label for="h-status" class="mb-1.5 block text-sm font-bold text-fg">وضعیت طرح</label>
					<CustomSelect
						id="h-status"
						v-model="selectedStatus"
						:options="statusOptions"
						trigger-class="h-12 text-fg"
					/>
				</div>
			</div>

			<!-- شرایط سطوح طرح رتبه‌ای -->
			<div v-if="structureKind === 'tiered'" class="space-y-3 rounded-2xl border border-border bg-elevated p-4">
				<div>
					<label for="h-bronze" class="mb-1 block text-xs font-bold text-bronze-text">برنز (حداقل)</label>
					<input
						id="h-bronze"
						v-model="bronzeDesc"
						required
						placeholder="مثلاً: ۵ دقیقه مطالعه"
						class="h-11 w-full rounded-xl border border-border bg-surface px-3.5 text-sm font-semibold text-fg"
					/>
				</div>

				<div>
					<label for="h-silver" class="mb-1 block text-xs font-bold text-silver-text">نقره (اختیاری)</label>
					<input
						id="h-silver"
						v-model="silverDesc"
						placeholder="مثلاً: ۱۰ دقیقه مطالعه"
						class="h-11 w-full rounded-xl border border-border bg-surface px-3.5 text-sm font-semibold text-fg"
					/>
				</div>

				<div>
					<label for="h-gold" class="mb-1 block text-xs font-bold text-gold-text">طلا (اختیاری)</label>
					<input
						id="h-gold"
						v-model="goldDesc"
						placeholder="مثلاً: ۲۰ دقیقه مطالعه"
						class="h-11 w-full rounded-xl border border-border bg-surface px-3.5 text-sm font-semibold text-fg"
					/>
				</div>

				<label class="mt-2 flex cursor-pointer items-center gap-3 rounded-xl border border-border bg-surface px-3.5 py-3 transition hover:border-border-strong">
					<input
						v-model="isSituational"
						type="checkbox"
						class="h-4.5 w-4.5 shrink-0 rounded-lg accent-accent"
					/>
					<span class="text-xs font-extrabold text-fg">
						طرح موقعیتی (دارای گزینهٔ «پیش نیامد»)
					</span>
				</label>
			</div>

			<!-- تنظیمات حداقل اضطراری طرح ریتمی -->
			<div v-else-if="structureKind === 'interval_cadence'" class="rounded-2xl border border-border bg-elevated p-4">
				<label for="h-emerg" class="mb-1.5 block text-xs font-bold text-fg">حداقل اضطراری (اختیاری)</label>
				<input
					id="h-emerg"
					v-model="emergencyMinDesc"
					placeholder="مثلاً: ۱۰ دقیقه برای روزهای شلوغ"
					class="h-11 w-full rounded-xl border border-border bg-surface px-3.5 text-sm font-semibold text-fg"
				/>
			</div>

			<div>
				<label for="h-notes" class="mb-1.5 block text-sm font-bold text-fg">تبصره یا توضیح کوتاه (اختیاری)</label>
				<input
					id="h-notes"
					v-model="notes"
					placeholder="مثلاً: اگر مهمان داشتیم استثناست..."
					class="h-12 w-full rounded-2xl border border-border bg-elevated px-4 text-sm text-fg"
				/>
			</div>

			<div v-if="mode === 'edit'" class="rounded-2xl border border-accent-border bg-accent-soft p-4">
				<label for="h-reason-edit" class="mb-1.5 block text-sm font-extrabold text-accent-text">
					دلیل این تغییر چیست؟ (برای ثبت در تاریخچه وقایع)
				</label>
				<input
					id="h-reason-edit"
					v-model="reason"
					required
					placeholder="مثلاً: تغییر شرایط مطالعه یا افزایش ظرفیت روزانه..."
					class="h-12 w-full rounded-xl border border-border bg-surface px-4 text-sm font-semibold text-fg focus:border-border-strong focus:outline-none"
				/>
			</div>

			<div class="flex items-center justify-end gap-3 border-t border-border pt-5">
				<button
					type="button"
					class="h-12 rounded-2xl px-5 text-sm font-bold text-fg-secondary hover:bg-elevated"
					@click="emit('close')"
				>
					انصراف
				</button>
				<button
					type="submit"
					:disabled="isSubmitting"
					class="h-12 rounded-2xl bg-accent px-8 text-sm font-extrabold text-accent-fg disabled:opacity-60 shadow-xs transition hover:opacity-90 active:scale-98"
				>
					{{ isSubmitting ? 'در حال ذخیره...' : mode === 'create' ? 'ایجاد طرح' : 'ذخیره تغییرات' }}
				</button>
			</div>
		</form>
	</BaseModal>
</template>

================================================================
PATH: app/components/tracker/CadenceHabitCard.vue
================================================================

<script setup lang="ts">
import { computed } from 'vue';
import { resolveEffectiveCadenceMinutes } from '~/core/habit-engine';
import { CADENCE_DEFAULTS } from '~/domain/constants';
import { toPersianDigits } from '~/domain/calendar';
import type { HabitEntity, HabitLogItemInput } from '~/domain/schemas';
import { Flame, Timer } from 'lucide-vue-next';

interface Props {
	habit: HabitEntity;
	log?: HabitLogItemInput;
}

const props = defineProps<Props>();

const emit = defineEmits<{
	(e: 'updateActual', habitId: string, minutes: number): void;
}>();

const plannedMinutes = computed(() =>
	props.log?.plannedMinutesSnapshot && props.log.plannedMinutesSnapshot > 0
		? props.log.plannedMinutesSnapshot
		: props.habit.defaultMinutesPerInterval > 0
			? props.habit.defaultMinutesPerInterval
			: 10
);

const actualMinutes = computed(() => props.log?.actualMinutesPerInterval ?? 0);
const effectiveTotalMinutes = computed(() => resolveEffectiveCadenceMinutes(props.log));

const progressPercent = computed(() =>
	plannedMinutes.value > 0
		? Math.min(100, Math.round((effectiveTotalMinutes.value / plannedMinutes.value) * 100))
		: 0
);

const quickPresets = computed(() => {
	const set = new Set<number>([0, 5, 10, 15, 20, 30]);
	if (plannedMinutes.value > 0) set.add(plannedMinutes.value);
	return Array.from(set).sort((a, b) => a - b);
});
</script>

<template>
	<div
		class="relative overflow-hidden rounded-3xl border p-4 shadow-(--shadow-soft) transition-all duration-200 sm:px-6 sm:py-4"
		:class="
			effectiveTotalMinutes >= plannedMinutes
				? 'border-optimal-border bg-optimal-soft'
				: effectiveTotalMinutes > 0
					? 'border-accent-border bg-accent-soft'
					: 'border-border bg-surface'
		"
	>
		<div class="flex flex-col justify-between gap-3.5 lg:flex-row lg:items-center">
			<div class="flex items-center gap-3">
				<div
					class="flex h-11 w-11 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl border bg-surface"
					:class="
						effectiveTotalMinutes >= plannedMinutes
							? 'border-optimal-border text-optimal'
							: 'border-accent-border text-accent-text'
					"
				>
					<Timer class="h-5 w-5" />
				</div>

				<div class="space-y-0.5">
					<h3 class="text-base font-black text-fg sm:text-lg">{{ habit.title }}</h3>
					<p v-if="habit.scheduleLabel" class="text-xs font-semibold text-fg-secondary">
						{{ habit.scheduleLabel }} • هدف: {{ toPersianDigits(plannedMinutes) }} دقیقه
					</p>
					<p v-else class="text-xs font-semibold text-fg-secondary">
						هدف: {{ toPersianDigits(plannedMinutes) }} دقیقه
					</p>
				</div>
			</div>

			<div class="flex w-full flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5 rounded-2xl border border-border bg-elevated p-1 lg:w-auto">
				<button
					v-for="preset in quickPresets"
					:key="preset"
					type="button"
					class="relative h-9.5 min-w-10 flex-1 rounded-xl px-2 text-xs font-extrabold transition active:scale-95 sm:h-10 sm:min-w-12 sm:flex-initial sm:px-3"
					:class="
						actualMinutes === preset
							? preset === 0
								? 'bg-failed text-on-solid shadow-xs'
								: preset === plannedMinutes && preset > 0
									? 'bg-optimal text-on-solid shadow-xs'
									: 'bg-accent text-accent-fg shadow-xs'
							: preset === plannedMinutes && preset > 0
								? 'border border-dashed border-accent-border text-accent-text hover:bg-surface'
								: 'text-fg-secondary hover:bg-surface hover:text-fg'
					"
					:title="
						actualMinutes === preset && preset !== 0
							? 'کلیک مجدد برای صفر کردن'
							: preset === plannedMinutes && preset > 0
								? `هدف تعیین‌شده (${toPersianDigits(preset)} دقیقه)`
								: ''
					"
					@click="emit('updateActual', habit.id, actualMinutes === preset && preset !== 0 ? 0 : preset)"
				>
					{{ preset === 0 ? 'صفر' : toPersianDigits(preset) }}
				</button>

				<button
					v-if="habit.emergencyMinDesc"
					type="button"
					class="flex h-9.5 items-center gap-1 rounded-xl px-2.5 text-xs font-extrabold transition sm:h-10"
					:class="
						actualMinutes === CADENCE_DEFAULTS.EMERGENCY_MINUTES
							? 'bg-bronze text-on-solid shadow-xs'
							: 'text-bronze-text hover:bg-surface'
					"
					:title="habit.emergencyMinDesc"
					@click="emit('updateActual', habit.id, CADENCE_DEFAULTS.EMERGENCY_MINUTES)"
				>
					<Flame class="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
					<span>{{ toPersianDigits(CADENCE_DEFAULTS.EMERGENCY_MINUTES) }}</span>
				</button>
			</div>
		</div>

		<div class="mt-3.5 h-1.5 w-full overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-elevated">
			<div
				class="h-full rounded-full transition-all duration-300"
				:class="
					effectiveTotalMinutes >= plannedMinutes
						? 'bg-optimal'
						: effectiveTotalMinutes > 0
							? 'bg-accent'
							: 'bg-transparent'
				"
				:style="{ width: `${progressPercent}%` }"
			></div>
		</div>
	</div>
</template>


----------------------------------------------------------------

================================================================
PATH: app/components/tracker/TieredHabitCard.vue
================================================================

<script setup lang="ts">
import { ref, computed } from 'vue';
import { getMaxSupportedTier, resolveHabitRuleSnapshot } from '~/core/habit-engine';
import { COMPOSITE_FULL_LABEL, TIER_SCORES } from '~/domain/constants';
import type { HabitLogItemInput, HabitTreeNode } from '~/domain/schemas';
import { Check, ChevronDown, ChevronUp, Minus, X } from 'lucide-vue-next';

interface Props {
	node: HabitTreeNode;
	logsByHabitId: Record<string, HabitLogItemInput>;
	compositeNotation?: string;
}

const props = defineProps<Props>();

const emit = defineEmits<{
	(e: 'selectTier', habitId: string, tier: number): void;
	(e: 'selectGroupTier', parentId: string, tier: number): void;
}>();

const isExpanded = ref(false);
const hasChildren = computed(() => props.node.children && props.node.children.length > 0);
const maxTier = computed(() => getMaxSupportedTier(props.node));
const currentLog = computed(() => props.logsByHabitId[props.node.id]);
const currentTier = computed(() => currentLog.value?.achievedTier ?? null);
const displayTitle = computed(() => currentLog.value?.habitTitleSnapshot || props.node.title);
const hasSituationalGroup = computed(
	() => Boolean(props.node.isSituational) || props.node.children.some((c) => Boolean(c.isSituational))
);

const uniformChildrenTier = computed<number | null>(() => {
	if (!hasChildren.value) return null;
	const tiers = props.node.children.map((c) => props.logsByHabitId[c.id]?.achievedTier ?? null);
	const first = tiers[0];
	if (first === null || first === undefined) return null;
	return tiers.every((t) => t === first) ? first : null;
});

const hasMixedChildren = computed<boolean>(() => {
	if (!hasChildren.value) return false;
	const tiers = props.node.children.map((c) => props.logsByHabitId[c.id]?.achievedTier ?? null);
	const hasAnyLogged = tiers.some((t) => t !== null && t !== undefined);
	return hasAnyLogged && uniformChildrenTier.value === null;
});

const maxChildSupportedTier = computed<1 | 2 | 3>(() => {
	if (!hasChildren.value) return maxTier.value;
	return props.node.children.reduce<1 | 2 | 3>((acc, child) => {
		const m = getMaxSupportedTier(child);
		return m > acc ? m : acc;
	}, 1);
});

const isNodeLogged = computed(() => {
	if (hasChildren.value) {
		return props.node.children.every((c) => {
			const t = props.logsByHabitId[c.id]?.achievedTier;
			return t !== null && t !== undefined;
		});
	}
	return currentTier.value !== null && currentTier.value !== undefined;
});

const activeRuleSubtitle = computed(() => {
	if (hasChildren.value) {
		return props.node.scheduleLabel || '';
	}
	if (currentTier.value !== null && currentTier.value !== undefined) {
		return currentLog.value?.achievedRuleSnapshot || resolveHabitRuleSnapshot(props.node, currentTier.value) || '';
	}
	return props.node.bronzeDesc || props.node.scheduleLabel || '';
});

const effectiveCardTier = computed(() => (hasChildren.value ? uniformChildrenTier.value : currentTier.value));

const cardSurfaceClasses = computed(() => {
	const spacing = 'py-3.5 sm:py-4 shadow-(--shadow-soft)';

	if (hasChildren.value && props.compositeNotation === COMPOSITE_FULL_LABEL) {
		return `${spacing} border-gold-border bg-gold-soft`;
	}
	if (effectiveCardTier.value === TIER_SCORES.SKIPPED) return `${spacing} border-border bg-elevated`;
	if (effectiveCardTier.value === TIER_SCORES.GOLD) return `${spacing} border-gold-border bg-gold-soft`;
	if (effectiveCardTier.value === TIER_SCORES.SILVER) return `${spacing} border-silver-border bg-silver-soft`;
	if (effectiveCardTier.value === TIER_SCORES.BRONZE) return `${spacing} border-bronze-border bg-bronze-soft`;
	if (effectiveCardTier.value === TIER_SCORES.FAILED) return `${spacing} border-failed-border bg-failed-soft`;
	return 'py-3.5 sm:py-4 border-border-strong bg-surface shadow-(--shadow-soft)';
});

function getOptionStyle(selected: number | null, target: number): string {
	if (selected !== target) {
		return 'text-fg-secondary hover:bg-surface hover:text-fg';
	}
	if (target === TIER_SCORES.SKIPPED) return 'bg-muted text-fg font-extrabold border border-border-strong shadow-xs';
	if (target === TIER_SCORES.FAILED) return 'bg-failed text-on-solid font-extrabold shadow-xs';
	if (target === TIER_SCORES.BRONZE) return 'bg-bronze text-on-solid font-extrabold shadow-xs';
	if (target === TIER_SCORES.SILVER) return 'bg-silver text-on-solid font-extrabold shadow-xs';
	return 'bg-gold text-on-solid font-black shadow-xs';
}

interface TierButtonDescriptor {
	tier: number;
	label: string;
	minWidthClass: string;
}

function getTierButtons(
	supportedMaxTier: 1 | 2 | 3,
	situational: boolean,
	useBinaryBronzeLabel = false
): TierButtonDescriptor[] {
	const items: TierButtonDescriptor[] = [];
	if (situational) {
		items.push({
			tier: TIER_SCORES.SKIPPED,
			label: 'پیش نیامد',
			minWidthClass: 'sm:px-3'
		});
	}
	items.push({
		tier: TIER_SCORES.FAILED,
		label: 'نشد',
		minWidthClass: 'sm:min-w-14 sm:px-3.5'
	});
	items.push({
		tier: TIER_SCORES.BRONZE,
		label: useBinaryBronzeLabel && supportedMaxTier === TIER_SCORES.BRONZE ? 'انجام شد' : 'برنز',
		minWidthClass: 'sm:min-w-16 sm:px-3.5'
	});
	if (supportedMaxTier >= TIER_SCORES.SILVER) {
		items.push({
			tier: TIER_SCORES.SILVER,
			label: 'نقره',
			minWidthClass: 'sm:min-w-16 sm:px-3.5'
		});
	}
	if (supportedMaxTier === TIER_SCORES.GOLD) {
		items.push({
			tier: TIER_SCORES.GOLD,
			label: 'طلا',
			minWidthClass: 'sm:min-w-16 sm:px-3.5'
		});
	}
	return items;
}
</script>

<template>
	<div class="rounded-3xl border px-4 transition-all duration-200 sm:px-6" :class="cardSurfaceClasses">
		<div class="flex flex-col justify-between gap-3.5 sm:flex-row sm:items-center">
			<div class="min-w-0 space-y-1">
				<div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2.5">
					<span v-if="!isNodeLogged" class="h-2.5 w-2.5 shrink-0 rounded-full bg-accent"></span>
					<span
						v-else-if="effectiveCardTier === TIER_SCORES.FAILED"
						class="flex h-5 w-5 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-lg border border-failed-border bg-surface text-failed"
					>
						<X class="h-3.5 w-3.5 stroke-[2.75]" />
					</span>
					<span
						v-else-if="effectiveCardTier === TIER_SCORES.SKIPPED"
						class="flex h-5 w-5 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-lg border border-border bg-surface text-fg-secondary"
					>
						<Minus class="h-3.5 w-3.5 stroke-[2.75]" />
					</span>
					<span
						v-else
						class="flex h-5 w-5 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-lg border bg-surface"
						:class="
							effectiveCardTier === TIER_SCORES.GOLD
								? 'border-gold-border text-gold-text'
								: effectiveCardTier === TIER_SCORES.SILVER
									? 'border-silver-border text-silver-text'
									: 'border-bronze-border text-bronze-text'
						"
					>
						<Check class="h-3.5 w-3.5 stroke-[2.75]" />
					</span>

					<h3 class="text-base font-black text-fg sm:text-lg">{{ displayTitle }}</h3>

					<span
						v-if="node.status === 'archived'"
						class="rounded-lg border border-border bg-surface px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-bold text-fg-muted"
					>
						بایگانی‌شده
					</span>

					<span
						v-if="hasChildren && compositeNotation"
						dir="ltr"
						class="rounded-xl border border-gold-border bg-surface px-2.5 py-0.5 font-mono text-xs font-black text-gold-text"
					>
						{{ compositeNotation }}
					</span>
				</div>
				<p v-if="activeRuleSubtitle" class="text-xs font-semibold text-fg-secondary">{{ activeRuleSubtitle }}</p>
			</div>

			<!-- دکمه‌های سطوح گروه والد -->
			<div v-if="hasChildren" class="flex w-full flex-wrap items-center gap-2 sm:w-auto">
				<div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1 border border-border min-w-0 flex-1 rounded-2xl bg-elevated p-1 sm:w-auto sm:flex-initial sm:gap-1.5 sm:p-1.5">
					<button
						v-for="btn in getTierButtons(maxChildSupportedTier, hasSituationalGroup, false)"
						:key="btn.tier"
						type="button"
						class="flex-1 text-xs whitespace-nowrap transition h-10 rounded-xl px-2.5 sm:h-11"
						:class="[btn.minWidthClass, getOptionStyle(uniformChildrenTier, btn.tier)]"
						@click.stop="emit('selectGroupTier', node.id, btn.tier)"
					>
						{{ btn.label }}
					</button>
				</div>

				<button
					type="button"
					class="flex h-11 shrink-0 items-center gap-1.5 rounded-2xl border border-border bg-elevated px-3 text-xs font-bold text-fg transition hover:border-border-strong sm:px-3.5"
					@click="isExpanded = !isExpanded"
				>
					<span>تک‌به‌تک</span>
					<ChevronUp v-if="isExpanded || hasMixedChildren" class="h-4 w-4 text-fg-secondary" />
					<ChevronDown v-else class="h-4 w-4 text-fg-secondary" />
				</button>
			</div>

			<!-- دکمه‌های سطوح عادت مستقل -->
			<div v-else class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1 border border-border min-w-0 flex-1 rounded-2xl bg-elevated p-1 sm:w-auto sm:flex-initial sm:gap-1.5 sm:p-1.5">
				<button
					v-for="btn in getTierButtons(maxTier, Boolean(node.isSituational), true)"
					:key="btn.tier"
					type="button"
					class="flex-1 text-xs whitespace-nowrap transition h-10 rounded-xl px-2.5 sm:h-11"
					:class="[btn.minWidthClass, getOptionStyle(currentTier, btn.tier)]"
					@click.stop="emit('selectTier', node.id, btn.tier)"
				>
					{{ btn.label }}
				</button>
			</div>
		</div>

		<!-- ردیف‌های زیرطرح‌های درون گروه -->
		<div v-if="hasChildren && (isExpanded || hasMixedChildren)" class="mt-4 space-y-2.5 border-t border-border pt-4">
			<div
				v-for="sub in node.children"
				:key="sub.id"
				class="flex flex-col justify-between gap-3 rounded-2xl border border-border bg-elevated px-4 py-3 sm:flex-row sm:items-center"
			>
				<div>
					<div class="flex items-center gap-2">
						<span class="text-sm font-extrabold text-fg">{{ logsByHabitId[sub.id]?.habitTitleSnapshot || sub.title }}</span>
						<span v-if="sub.status === 'archived'" class="rounded-md border border-border bg-surface px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-bold text-fg-muted">
							بایگانی‌شده
						</span>
					</div>
					<div class="mt-0.5 text-xs font-medium text-fg-secondary">
						{{
							logsByHabitId[sub.id]?.achievedTier !== null && logsByHabitId[sub.id]?.achievedTier !== undefined
								? logsByHabitId[sub.id]?.achievedRuleSnapshot || resolveHabitRuleSnapshot(sub, logsByHabitId[sub.id]?.achievedTier) || sub.bronzeDesc
								: sub.bronzeDesc
						}}
					</div>
				</div>

				<div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1 border border-border w-full rounded-xl bg-surface p-1 sm:w-auto">
					<button
						v-for="btn in getTierButtons(getMaxSupportedTier(sub), Boolean(sub.isSituational || node.isSituational), false)"
						:key="btn.tier"
						type="button"
						class="flex-1 text-xs whitespace-nowrap transition h-9 rounded-lg px-2.5 sm:px-3"
						:class="getOptionStyle(logsByHabitId[sub.id]?.achievedTier ?? null, btn.tier)"
						@click.stop="emit('selectTier', sub.id, btn.tier)"
					>
						{{ btn.label }}
					</button>
				</div>
			</div>
		</div>
	</div>
</template>

<script setup lang="ts">
import { computed } from 'vue';
import { toPersianDigits } from '~/domain/calendar';
import type { CalendarEventEntity, UpcomingEventBannerItem } from '~/domain/schemas';
import { BellRing, CalendarDays, Check, Clock } from 'lucide-vue-next';

interface Props {
	upcomingItems?: UpcomingEventBannerItem[];
	selectedDayEvents?: Array<Pick<CalendarEventEntity, 'id' | 'title' | 'isAnnual' | 'isCompleted'>>;
	currentDateIso: string;
}

const props = withDefaults(defineProps<Props>(), {
	upcomingItems: () => [],
	selectedDayEvents: () => []
});

const emit = defineEmits<{
	(e: 'toggleComplete', eventId: string, isCompleted: boolean): void;
	(e: 'openCalendarDate', dateIso: string): void;
}>();

const hasAnythingToShow = computed(
	() => props.upcomingItems.length > 0 || props.selectedDayEvents.length > 0
);

function getRelativeText(days: number): string {
	if (days === -1) return 'دیروز (انجام‌نشده)';
	if (days === 0) return 'امروز';
	if (days === 1) return 'فردا';
	if (days === 2) return 'پس‌فردا';
	return `${toPersianDigits(days)} روز دیگر`;
}
</script>

<template>
	<section
		v-if="hasAnythingToShow"
		class="rounded-3xl border border-accent-border bg-surface p-3.5 shadow-(--shadow-card) sm:px-5 sm:py-4"
	>
		<div class="flex flex-col justify-between gap-3 sm:flex-row sm:items-center">
			<div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
				<div
					class="flex h-8 w-8 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl border border-accent-border bg-accent-soft text-accent-text"
				>
					<BellRing class="h-4 w-4" />
				</div>

				<div
					v-for="dayEv in selectedDayEvents"
					:key="dayEv.id"
					class="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-2xl border border-accent-border bg-accent-soft px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-extrabold text-accent-text"
				>
					<CalendarDays class="h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0" />
					<span>در این روز: {{ dayEv.title }}</span>
					<button
						v-if="!dayEv.isAnnual"
						type="button"
						class="flex h-5 w-5 items-center justify-center rounded-md border border-current transition active:scale-95"
						:class="dayEv.isCompleted ? 'bg-optimal text-on-solid' : 'bg-surface'"
						:title="dayEv.isCompleted ? 'انجام شد' : 'علامت به عنوان انجام‌شده'"
						@click="emit('toggleComplete', dayEv.id, !dayEv.isCompleted)"
					>
						<Check v-if="dayEv.isCompleted" class="h-3 w-3" />
					</button>
				</div>

				<button
					v-for="item in upcomingItems.filter((u) => !selectedDayEvents.some((s) => s.id === u.id))"
					:key="item.id + item.occurrenceDateIso"
					type="button"
					class="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-2xl border border-border bg-elevated px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-bold text-fg transition hover:border-border-strong"
					@click="emit('openCalendarDate', item.occurrenceDateIso)"
				>
					<span class="h-2 w-2 shrink-0 rounded-full bg-accent"></span>
					<span class="font-extrabold text-accent-text"> {{ getRelativeText(item.daysRemaining) }}: </span>
					<span>{{ item.title }}</span>
				</button>
			</div>

			<button
				type="button"
				class="flex shrink-0 items-center justify-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl border border-border bg-elevated px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-extrabold text-fg-secondary transition hover:border-border-strong hover:text-fg"
				@click="emit('openCalendarDate', currentDateIso)"
			>
				<Clock class="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-accent-text" />
				<span>مشاهده در تقویم</span>
			</button>
		</div>
	</section>
</template>

<script setup lang="ts">
import { ref, computed } from 'vue';
import { formatJalaliHuman, toPersianDigits } from '~/domain/calendar';
import type { HabitEntity } from '~/domain/schemas';
import { CalendarClock, Copy, Eraser, MessageSquarePlus, Sparkles } from 'lucide-vue-next';

interface Props {
	cadenceHabits: HabitEntity[];
	tomorrowIso: string;
	availableTags: string[];
	selectedTags: string[];
	reflectionNote: string;
	tomorrowPlans: Record<string, { plannedMinutesPerInterval: number }>;
}

const props = defineProps<Props>();

const emit = defineEmits<{
	(e: 'markAllOptimal'): void;
	(e: 'clearDay'): void;
	(e: 'toggleTag', tag: string): void;
	(e: 'updateReflectionNote', text: string): void;
	(e: 'updateTomorrowPlan', habitId: string, minutes: number): void;
	(e: 'copyTodayToTomorrow'): void;
	(e: 'saveTomorrowPlans'): void;
}>();

const showReflectionDrawer = ref(false);
const showTomorrowDrawer = ref(false);

const tomorrowHuman = computed(() => formatJalaliHuman(props.tomorrowIso));
const activeNotesBadgeCount = computed(
	() => props.selectedTags.length + (props.reflectionNote.trim().length > 0 ? 1 : 0)
);
</script>

<template>
	<div class="rounded-3xl border border-border bg-surface p-4 shadow-(--shadow-soft) sm:px-6">
		<div class="flex flex-col gap-2.5 sm:flex-row sm:flex-wrap sm:items-center sm:justify-between sm:gap-3">
			<div class="flex w-full items-center gap-2 sm:w-auto">
				<button
					type="button"
					class="flex h-11 flex-1 items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-2xl bg-accent px-4 text-xs font-black text-accent-fg shadow-xs transition hover:opacity-90 active:scale-98 sm:flex-initial sm:px-5 sm:text-sm"
					@click="emit('markAllOptimal')"
				>
					<Sparkles class="h-4 w-4 shrink-0" />
					<span>همه عالی انجام شد</span>
				</button>

				<button
					type="button"
					class="flex h-11 shrink-0 items-center justify-center gap-1.5 rounded-2xl border border-border bg-elevated px-3 text-xs font-extrabold text-fg-secondary transition hover:border-failed-border hover:bg-failed-soft hover:text-failed sm:px-3.5"
					title="پاک کردن کامل ثبت‌های این روز و بازگشت به حالت ثبت‌نشده"
					@click="emit('clearDay')"
				>
					<Eraser class="h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0" />
					<span>پاک کردن روز</span>
				</button>
			</div>

			<div class="flex w-full flex-wrap items-center gap-2 sm:w-auto">
				<button
					type="button"
					class="flex h-11 flex-1 items-center justify-center gap-1.5 rounded-2xl border px-3 text-xs font-extrabold transition sm:flex-initial sm:gap-2 sm:px-4"
					:class="
						showReflectionDrawer || activeNotesBadgeCount > 0
							? 'border-accent-border bg-accent-soft text-accent-text'
							: 'border-border bg-elevated text-fg-secondary hover:text-fg'
					"
					@click="showReflectionDrawer = !showReflectionDrawer"
				>
					<MessageSquarePlus class="h-4 w-4 shrink-0" />
					<span class="truncate">علت‌ها و یادداشت</span>
					<span
						v-if="activeNotesBadgeCount > 0"
						class="flex h-5 w-5 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-accent text-[11px] font-black text-accent-fg"
					>
						{{ toPersianDigits(activeNotesBadgeCount) }}
					</span>
				</button>

				<button
					v-if="cadenceHabits.length > 0"
					type="button"
					class="flex h-11 flex-1 items-center justify-center gap-1.5 rounded-2xl border px-3 text-xs font-extrabold transition sm:flex-initial sm:gap-2 sm:px-4"
					:class="
						showTomorrowDrawer
							? 'border-accent-border bg-accent-soft text-accent-text'
							: 'border-border bg-elevated text-fg-secondary hover:text-fg'
					"
					@click="showTomorrowDrawer = !showTomorrowDrawer"
				>
					<CalendarClock class="h-4 w-4 shrink-0" />
					<span class="truncate">تنظیم ریتم فردا</span>
				</button>
			</div>
		</div>

		<!-- کشوی علت‌ها و یادداشت روز -->
		<div v-if="showReflectionDrawer" class="mt-4 space-y-3.5 border-t border-border pt-4">
			<div class="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
				<button
					v-for="tag in availableTags"
					:key="tag"
					type="button"
					class="h-9 rounded-xl border px-3 text-xs font-bold transition"
					:class="
						selectedTags.includes(tag)
							? 'border-accent-border bg-accent text-accent-fg'
							: 'border-border bg-elevated text-fg-secondary hover:text-fg'
					"
					@click="emit('toggleTag', tag)"
				>
					#{{ tag }}
				</button>
			</div>

			<div>
				<textarea
					rows="2"
					:value="reflectionNote"
					placeholder="یادداشت کوتاه درباره اتفاقات یا دلایل امروز..."
					class="w-full rounded-2xl border border-border bg-elevated p-3.5 text-sm text-fg placeholder:text-fg-muted focus:border-border-strong focus:outline-none"
					@input="emit('updateReflectionNote', ($event.target as HTMLTextAreaElement).value)"
				></textarea>
			</div>
		</div>

		<!-- کشوی تنظیم ریتم فردا -->
		<div v-if="showTomorrowDrawer && cadenceHabits.length > 0" class="mt-4 space-y-4 border-t border-border pt-4">
			<div class="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2">
				<h3 class="text-sm font-black text-fg">
					هدف فردا ({{ tomorrowHuman.fullLabel }})
				</h3>

				<button
					type="button"
					class="flex h-9 items-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl border border-border bg-elevated px-3 text-xs font-bold text-fg hover:border-border-strong"
					@click="emit('copyTodayToTomorrow')"
				>
					<Copy class="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-accent-text" />
					<span>کپی از امروز</span>
				</button>
			</div>

			<div class="grid grid-cols-1 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2">
				<div
					v-for="habit in cadenceHabits"
					:key="habit.id"
					class="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 rounded-2xl border border-border bg-elevated px-4 py-3"
				>
					<span class="text-sm font-extrabold text-fg">{{ habit.title }}</span>
					<label class="flex items-center gap-2">
						<input
							v-if="tomorrowPlans[habit.id]"
							:value="tomorrowPlans[habit.id]?.plannedMinutesPerInterval"
							type="number"
							min="0"
							step="1"
							class="h-10 w-20 rounded-xl border border-border bg-surface px-3 text-center font-mono text-base font-black text-fg focus:border-border-strong focus:outline-none"
							@input="emit('updateTomorrowPlan', habit.id, Math.max(0, Number(($event.target as HTMLInputElement).value) || 0))"
						/>
						<span class="text-xs font-bold text-fg-secondary">دقیقه</span>
					</label>
				</div>
			</div>

			<div class="flex justify-end">
				<button
					type="button"
					class="h-10 rounded-xl bg-accent px-5 text-xs font-black text-accent-fg transition active:scale-98"
					@click="emit('saveTomorrowPlans')"
				>
					ذخیره برنامه فردا
				</button>
			</div>
		</div>
	</div>
</template>

================================================================
PATH: app/components/tracker/JalaliDateScrubber.vue
================================================================

<script setup lang="ts">
import { computed, onMounted, onUnmounted } from 'vue';
import {
	buildRecentCalendarDays,
	formatJalaliHuman,
	getTodayIso,
	getYesterdayIso,
	shiftIsoDate,
	toPersianDigits,
	type CalendarScrubberDay
} from '~/domain/calendar';
import { getDayStatusMeta } from '~/domain/constants';
import type { DayStatusColor } from '~/domain/schemas';
import { Check, ChevronLeft, ChevronRight, AlertCircle } from 'lucide-vue-next';

interface Props {
	currentDateIso: string;
	recentEntries?: Array<{
		dateIso: string;
		dayStatus: DayStatusColor;
		reflectionNote: string;
		tags: string[];
	}>;
	liveDayStatus: DayStatusColor;
	isLiveDirty?: boolean;
	hasExistingEntry?: boolean;
	saveState?: 'idle' | 'saving' | 'saved' | 'error';
	completionStats: { completed: number; goldCount: number; total: number; percent: number };
}

const props = withDefaults(defineProps<Props>(), {
	recentEntries: () => [],
	isLiveDirty: false,
	hasExistingEntry: false,
	saveState: 'idle'
});

const emit = defineEmits<{
	(e: 'selectDate', iso: string): void;
	(e: 'retrySave'): void;
}>();

const human = computed(() => formatJalaliHuman(props.currentDateIso));
const todayIso = computed(() => getTodayIso());
const yesterdayIso = computed(() => getYesterdayIso());
const prevDayIso = computed(() => shiftIsoDate(props.currentDateIso, -1));
const nextDayIso = computed(() => shiftIsoDate(props.currentDateIso, 1));

const savedStatusByIso = computed(() => new Map(props.recentEntries.map((e) => [e.dateIso, e.dayStatus])));

const calendarDays = computed(() =>
	buildRecentCalendarDays<DayStatusColor>({
		currentDateIso: props.currentDateIso,
		todayIso: todayIso.value,
		savedStatusByIso: savedStatusByIso.value,
		liveStatus: props.liveDayStatus,
		useLiveStatusForSelected: props.isLiveDirty || props.hasExistingEntry,
		defaultStatus: 'failed',
		daysCount: 14
	})
);

function handleKeydown(e: KeyboardEvent) {
	const tag = (e.target as HTMLElement)?.tagName;
	if (tag === 'INPUT' || tag === 'TEXTAREA' || tag === 'SELECT') return;
	if (e.key === '[') {
		e.preventDefault();
		emit('selectDate', prevDayIso.value);
	} else if (e.key === ']') {
		e.preventDefault();
		emit('selectDate', nextDayIso.value);
	}
}

function getDayCellClasses(day: CalendarScrubberDay<DayStatusColor>): string {
	const selectedGlow = day.isSelected
		? 'border-2 -translate-y-0.5 shadow-(--shadow-card)'
		: 'border hover:border-border-strong';

	if (day.visualState === 'optimal') {
		return `${selectedGlow} ${day.isSelected ? 'border-optimal bg-optimal-soft' : 'border-optimal-border bg-optimal-soft'}`;
	}
	if (day.visualState === 'partial') {
		return `${selectedGlow} ${day.isSelected ? 'border-partial bg-partial-soft' : 'border-partial-border bg-partial-soft'}`;
	}
	return `${selectedGlow} ${day.isSelected ? 'border-failed bg-failed-soft' : 'border-failed-border bg-failed-soft'}`;
}

onMounted(() => {
	window.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeydown);
});

onUnmounted(() => {
	window.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeydown);
});
</script>

<template>
	<section class="rounded-3xl border border-border bg-surface p-4 shadow-(--shadow-soft) sm:p-6">
		<div class="flex flex-col gap-3.5 sm:flex-row sm:flex-wrap sm:items-center sm:justify-between sm:gap-4">
			<div class="flex w-full items-center justify-between gap-1.5 sm:w-auto sm:justify-start sm:gap-2">
				<button
					type="button"
					class="flex h-10 w-10 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl border border-border bg-elevated text-fg transition hover:border-border-strong active:scale-95 sm:h-11 sm:w-11"
					aria-label="روز قبل"
					@click="emit('selectDate', prevDayIso)"
				>
					<ChevronRight class="h-5 w-5" />
				</button>

				<div class="flex min-w-0 flex-1 items-center justify-center sm:min-w-80 sm:flex-initial">
					<div class="flex w-full min-w-0 flex-wrap items-center justify-center gap-1.5 px-2 py-1.5 text-center sm:gap-2.5 sm:px-3">
						<h1 class="truncate text-base font-black tracking-tight text-fg sm:text-2xl">
							{{ human.fullLabel }}
						</h1>
						<span
							v-if="human.relativeBadge"
							class="shrink-0 rounded-xl border border-accent-border bg-accent-soft px-2 py-0.5 text-[11px] font-extrabold text-accent-text sm:px-2.5 sm:text-xs"
						>
							{{ human.relativeBadge }}
						</span>
					</div>
				</div>

				<button
					type="button"
					class="flex h-10 w-10 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl border border-border bg-elevated text-fg transition hover:border-border-strong active:scale-95 sm:h-11 sm:w-11"
					aria-label="روز بعد"
					@click="emit('selectDate', nextDayIso)"
				>
					<ChevronLeft class="h-5 w-5" />
				</button>
			</div>

			<div class="flex w-full items-center justify-between gap-2 sm:w-auto sm:justify-end sm:gap-3">
				<div class="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-2xl border border-border bg-elevated px-3 py-2 sm:gap-2.5 sm:px-3.5">
					<div class="text-xs font-black text-fg">
						{{ toPersianDigits(completionStats.completed) }} از {{ toPersianDigits(completionStats.total) }}
					</div>

					<span
						v-if="completionStats.total > 0 && completionStats.completed === completionStats.total"
						class="flex h-5 w-5 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-optimal-soft text-optimal"
						title="تمام طرح‌های امروز تکمیل شد"
					>
						<Check class="h-3 w-3 stroke-[2.75]" />
					</span>
					<button
						v-else-if="saveState === 'error'"
						type="button"
						class="flex h-5 w-5 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-failed-soft text-failed transition hover:scale-105"
						title="خطا در ذخیره‌سازی — کلیک برای تلاش مجدد"
						@click="emit('retrySave')"
					>
						<AlertCircle class="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
					</button>
				</div>

				<div class="flex items-center gap-1 rounded-2xl border border-border bg-elevated p-1">
					<button
						type="button"
						class="rounded-xl px-3.5 py-2 text-xs font-extrabold transition"
						:class="
							currentDateIso === yesterdayIso
								? 'border border-accent-border bg-accent-soft text-accent-text shadow-xs'
								: 'text-fg-secondary hover:text-fg'
						"
						@click="emit('selectDate', yesterdayIso)"
					>
						دیروز
					</button>
					<button
						type="button"
						class="rounded-xl px-3.5 py-2 text-xs font-extrabold transition"
						:class="
							currentDateIso === todayIso
								? 'border border-accent-border bg-accent-soft text-accent-text shadow-xs'
								: 'text-fg-secondary hover:text-fg'
						"
						@click="emit('selectDate', todayIso)"
					>
						امروز
					</button>
				</div>
			</div>
		</div>

		<div class="mt-4 grid grid-cols-7 gap-1.5 sm:mt-5 sm:grid-cols-14 sm:gap-2">
			<button
				v-for="day in calendarDays"
				:key="day.iso"
				type="button"
				class="flex flex-col items-center justify-between rounded-2xl px-0.5 py-2 transition-all duration-150 sm:px-1 sm:py-2.5"
				:class="getDayCellClasses(day)"
				:title="`${day.weekdayShort} ${toPersianDigits(day.jd)} ${day.monthName}`"
				@click="emit('selectDate', day.iso)"

					{{ day.weekdayShort }}

					{{ toPersianDigits(day.jd) }}

			</button>
		</div>
	</section>

PATH: app/components/media/MediaUpsertModal.vue
