<script setup lang="ts">
import { ref, computed } from 'vue';
import { getTodayIso, isoToJalaliSlash, toPersianDigits } from '~/domain/calendar';
import { BOOK_RATING_OPTIONS, MEDIA_CATEGORY_OPTIONS, MEDIA_STATUS_OPTIONS } from '~/domain/constants';
import type { CreateMediaInput, MediaCategory, MediaEntity, MediaStatus, UpdateMediaInput } from '~/domain/schemas';
import BaseModal from '~/components/ui/BaseModal.vue';
import CustomSelect from '~/components/ui/CustomSelect.vue';
import { Clapperboard, History } from 'lucide-vue-next';

interface Props {
	editingMedia?: MediaEntity | null;
	existingSuggestions?: Array<{ title: string; category: MediaCategory }>;
	isSubmitting?: boolean;
}

const props = withDefaults(defineProps<Props>(), {
	editingMedia: null,
	existingSuggestions: () => [],
	isSubmitting: false
});

const emit = defineEmits<{
	(e: 'close'): void;
	(e: 'submit', payload: CreateMediaInput | UpdateMediaInput): void;
}>();

const defaultJalaliToday = toPersianDigits(isoToJalaliSlash(getTodayIso()));
const initialStatus: MediaStatus = props.editingMedia?.status ?? 'completed';

const title = ref(props.editingMedia?.title ?? '');
const selectedCategory = ref<MediaCategory>(props.editingMedia?.category ?? 'movie');
const selectedStatus = ref<MediaStatus>(initialStatus);
const selectedRating = ref<number>(props.editingMedia?.rating ?? 5);
const startedJalali = ref(
	props.editingMedia?.startedAtIso ? toPersianDigits(isoToJalaliSlash(props.editingMedia.startedAtIso)) : ''
);
const finishedJalali = ref(
	props.editingMedia?.finishedAtIso
		? toPersianDigits(isoToJalaliSlash(props.editingMedia.finishedAtIso))
		: initialStatus === 'completed'
			? defaultJalaliToday
			: ''
);
const notes = ref(props.editingMedia?.notes ?? '');

const matchedSuggestion = computed(() =>
	props.existingSuggestions.find(
		(s) => s.title.trim().toLowerCase() === title.value.trim().toLowerCase()
	)
);

function handleTitleChange(e: Event) {
	const val = (e.target as HTMLInputElement).value;
	title.value = val;
	const match = props.existingSuggestions.find(
		(s) => s.title.trim().toLowerCase() === val.trim().toLowerCase()
	);
	if (match && !props.editingMedia) {
		selectedCategory.value = match.category;
	}
}

function handleSubmit() {
	if (!title.value.trim() || props.isSubmitting) return;

	if (props.editingMedia) {
		emit('submit', {
			mediaId: props.editingMedia.id,
			title: title.value.trim(),
			category: selectedCategory.value,
			status: selectedStatus.value,
			startedJalali: startedJalali.value.trim() || undefined,
			finishedJalali: finishedJalali.value.trim() || undefined,
			rating: selectedRating.value,
			notes: notes.value.trim() || undefined
		});
	} else {
		emit('submit', {
			title: title.value.trim(),
			category: selectedCategory.value,
			status: selectedStatus.value,
			startedJalali: startedJalali.value.trim() || undefined,
			finishedJalali: finishedJalali.value.trim() || undefined,
			rating: selectedRating.value,
			notes: notes.value.trim() || undefined
		});
	}
}
</script>

<template>
	<BaseModal :title="editingMedia ? 'ویرایش اطلاعات اثر' : 'ثبت اثر جدید'" @close="emit('close')">
		<template #headerIcon>
			<Clapperboard class="h-4.5 w-4.5" />
		</template>

		<form class="space-y-4 overflow-y-auto p-5 sm:p-6" @submit.prevent="handleSubmit">
			<div>
				<span class="mb-1.5 block text-xs font-bold text-fg-secondary">نوع اثر</span>
				<div class="grid grid-cols-4 gap-1.5 rounded-2xl border border-border bg-elevated p-1">
					<button
						v-for="catOpt in MEDIA_CATEGORY_OPTIONS"
						:key="catOpt.value"
						type="button"
						class="h-9 rounded-xl text-xs font-extrabold transition"
						:class="
							selectedCategory === catOpt.value
								? 'border border-accent-border bg-accent-soft text-accent-text shadow-xs'
								: 'text-fg-secondary hover:text-fg'
						"
						@click="selectedCategory = catOpt.value"
					>
						{{ catOpt.label }}
					</button>
				</div>
			</div>

			<div>
				<div class="mb-1.5 flex items-center justify-between">
					<label for="md-title" class="block text-xs font-bold text-fg-secondary">نام اثر</label>
					<span
						v-if="matchedSuggestion && !editingMedia"
						class="inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-md bg-accent-soft px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-black text-accent-text"
					>
						<History class="h-3 w-3" />
						<span>قبلاً در آرشیو ثبت شده (تماشای مجدد)</span>
					</span>
				</div>

				<input
					id="md-title"
					v-model="title"
					list="existing-media-datalist"
					required
					placeholder="مثلاً: Interstellar یا Attack on Titan..."
					class="h-11 w-full rounded-2xl border border-border bg-elevated px-4 text-xs font-bold text-fg placeholder:font-normal placeholder:text-fg-muted focus:border-border-strong focus:outline-none"
					@input="handleTitleChange"
				/>

				<datalist id="existing-media-datalist">
					<option v-for="item in existingSuggestions" :key="item.title" :value="item.title" />
				</datalist>
			</div>

			<div class="grid grid-cols-1 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2">
				<div>
					<span class="mb-1.5 block text-xs font-bold text-fg-secondary">وضعیت</span>
					<div class="grid grid-cols-2 gap-1 rounded-2xl border border-border bg-elevated p-1">
						<button
							v-for="statusOpt in MEDIA_STATUS_OPTIONS"
							:key="statusOpt.value"
							type="button"
							class="h-9 rounded-xl text-xs font-extrabold transition"
							:class="
								selectedStatus === statusOpt.value
									? 'border border-accent-border bg-accent-soft text-accent-text shadow-xs'
									: 'text-fg-secondary hover:text-fg'
							"
							@click="selectedStatus = statusOpt.value"
						>
							{{ statusOpt.label }}
						</button>
					</div>
				</div>
				<div>
					<label for="md-rating" class="mb-1.5 block text-xs font-bold text-fg-secondary">امتیاز</label>
					<CustomSelect
						id="md-rating"
						v-model="selectedRating"
						:options="BOOK_RATING_OPTIONS"
						trigger-class="text-gold"
					/>
				</div>
			</div>

			<div class="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
				<div>
					<label for="md-start" class="mb-1.5 block text-xs font-bold text-fg-secondary">تاریخ شروع</label>
					<input
						id="md-start"
						v-model="startedJalali"
						v-persian-digits
						dir="ltr"
						:placeholder="defaultJalaliToday"
						class="h-11 w-full rounded-2xl border border-border bg-elevated px-3 text-center font-mono text-xs font-bold text-fg placeholder:font-normal placeholder:text-fg-muted focus:border-border-strong focus:outline-none"
					/>
				</div>
				<div>
					<label for="md-end" class="mb-1.5 block text-xs font-bold text-fg-secondary">تاریخ تماشا</label>
					<input
						id="md-end"
						v-model="finishedJalali"
						v-persian-digits
						dir="ltr"
						:placeholder="defaultJalaliToday"
						class="h-11 w-full rounded-2xl border border-border bg-elevated px-3 text-center font-mono text-xs font-bold text-fg placeholder:font-normal placeholder:text-fg-muted focus:border-border-strong focus:outline-none"
					/>
				</div>
			</div>

			<div>
				<label for="md-notes" class="mb-1.5 block text-xs font-bold text-fg-secondary">
					یادداشت (اختیاری)
				</label>
				<textarea
					id="md-notes"
					v-model="notes"
					rows="3"
					placeholder="نظر یا نکته کوتاه درباره این اثر..."
					class="w-full rounded-2xl border border-border bg-elevated p-3.5 text-xs text-fg placeholder:text-fg-muted focus:border-border-strong focus:outline-none"
				></textarea>
			</div>

			<div class="flex items-center justify-end gap-2.5 pt-2">
				<button
					type="button"
					class="h-11 rounded-2xl px-5 text-xs font-bold text-fg-secondary hover:bg-elevated"
					@click="emit('close')"
				>
					انصراف
				</button>
				<button
					type="submit"
					:disabled="isSubmitting"
					class="h-11 rounded-2xl bg-accent px-7 text-xs font-extrabold text-accent-fg shadow-xs transition hover:opacity-90 active:scale-98 disabled:opacity-60"
				>
					{{ isSubmitting ? 'در حال ثبت...' : editingMedia ? 'ذخیره تغییرات' : 'ثبت در آرشیو' }}
				</button>
			</div>
		</form>
	</BaseModal>
</template>

<script setup lang="ts">
import { toPersianDigits } from '~/domain/calendar';
import { MEDIA_CATEGORY_OPTIONS } from '~/domain/constants';
import type { MediaCategory, MediaSortBy, MediaStatusFilter } from '~/domain/schemas';
import CustomSelect from '~/components/ui/CustomSelect.vue';
import { Clapperboard, Plus } from 'lucide-vue-next';

interface Props {
	totalCount: number;
	unseenCount: number;
	categoryFilter: MediaCategory | 'ALL';
	statusFilter: MediaStatusFilter;
	sortBy: MediaSortBy;
}

defineProps<Props>();

const emit = defineEmits<{
	(e: 'update:categoryFilter', val: MediaCategory | 'ALL'): void;
	(e: 'update:statusFilter', val: MediaStatusFilter): void;
	(e: 'update:sortBy', val: MediaSortBy): void;
	(e: 'openCreate'): void;
}>();

const sortOptions: ReadonlyArray<{ value: MediaSortBy; label: string }> = [
	{ value: 'rating_desc', label: 'بیشترین امتیاز' },
	{ value: 'newest', label: 'جدیدترین‌ها' },
	{ value: 'oldest', label: 'قدیمی‌ترین‌ها' },
	{ value: 'title_asc', label: 'حروف الفبا' }
];
</script>

<template>

				<div class="flex items-baseline gap-2.5">

					@click="emit('openCreate')"
				>
					<Plus class="h-4 w-4" />
					<span>ثبت اثر جدید</span>
				</button>

				<div class="flex w-full items-center gap-1 rounded-2xl border border-border bg-elevated p-1 sm:w-auto">
					<button
						type="button"
						class="flex-1 rounded-xl px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs font-extrabold transition sm:flex-initial sm:px-3"
						:class="statusFilter === 'ALL' ? 'border border-accent-border bg-accent-soft text-accent-text shadow-xs' : 'text-fg-secondary hover:text-fg'"
						@click="emit('update:statusFilter', 'ALL')"
					>
						همه
					</button>
					<button
						type="button"
						class="flex flex-1 items-center justify-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs font-extrabold whitespace-nowrap transition sm:flex-initial sm:px-3"
						:class="statusFilter === 'UNSEEN' ? 'border border-accent-border bg-accent-soft text-accent-text shadow-xs' : 'text-fg-secondary hover:text-fg'"
						@click="emit('update:statusFilter', 'UNSEEN')"
					>
						<span>فقط ندیده‌ها</span>

				:class="categoryFilter === 'ALL' ? 'bg-accent text-accent-fg shadow-xs' : 'bg-elevated text-fg-secondary hover:text-fg'"
				@click="emit('update:categoryFilter', 'ALL')"
			>
				همه دسته‌ها

				v-for="cat in MEDIA_CATEGORY_OPTIONS"
				:key="cat.value"

				{{ cat.label }}

PATH: app/components/media/MediaItemCard.vue

<script setup lang="ts">
import { computed } from 'vue';
import { isoToJalaliSlash, toPersianDigits } from '~/domain/calendar';
import { MEDIA_CATEGORY_OPTIONS, MEDIA_STATUS_OPTIONS } from '~/domain/constants';
import type { MediaEntity } from '~/domain/schemas';
import { CheckCircle2, Pencil, Star, Trash2 } from 'lucide-vue-next';

interface Props {
	item: MediaEntity;
}

const props = defineProps<Props>();

const emit = defineEmits<{
	(e: 'edit', item: MediaEntity): void;
	(e: 'updateStatus', mediaId: string, status: 'completed'): void;
	(e: 'delete', mediaId: string): void;
}>();

const categoryMeta = computed(
	() => MEDIA_CATEGORY_OPTIONS.find((c) => c.value === props.item.category) ?? MEDIA_CATEGORY_OPTIONS[0]!
);

const statusMeta = computed(
	() => MEDIA_STATUS_OPTIONS.find((s) => s.value === props.item.status) ?? MEDIA_STATUS_OPTIONS[0]!
);
</script>

<template>
	<div
		class="group flex flex-col justify-between gap-3 rounded-2xl border border-border bg-surface px-4 py-3 shadow-(--shadow-card) transition hover:border-border-strong sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:px-5 sm:py-3.5"
	>
		<div class="min-w-0 flex-1 space-y-1">
			<div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2.5">
				<span class="rounded-lg border px-2 py-0.5 text-[11px] font-extrabold" :class="categoryMeta.badgeClasses">
					{{ categoryMeta.label }}
				</span>

				<h3 class="truncate text-sm font-black text-fg sm:text-base">{{ item.title }}</h3>

				<span v-if="item.status !== 'completed'" class="rounded-lg px-2.5 py-0.5 text-[11px] font-extrabold" :class="statusMeta.badgeClasses">
					{{ statusMeta.badgeLabel }}
				</span>

				<div v-if="item.rating" class="flex items-center gap-0.5 text-gold" :title="`${item.rating} ستاره`">
					<Star v-for="idx in item.rating" :key="idx" class="h-3 w-3 fill-current" />
				</div>
			</div>

			<div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 text-xs text-fg-secondary">
				<span v-if="item.finishedAtIso" class="text-fg-muted">
					تماشا:
					<strong class="font-mono font-bold text-fg-secondary">
						{{ toPersianDigits(isoToJalaliSlash(item.finishedAtIso)) }}
					</strong>
				</span>
				<span v-else-if="item.startedAtIso" class="text-fg-muted">
					شروع:
					<strong class="font-mono font-bold text-fg-secondary">
						{{ toPersianDigits(isoToJalaliSlash(item.startedAtIso)) }}
					</strong>
				</span>
				<span v-if="item.notes" class="truncate text-[11px] text-fg-muted" :title="item.notes">
					«{{ item.notes }}»
				</span>
			</div>
		</div>

		<div class="flex shrink-0 items-center justify-end gap-2 border-t border-border pt-2 sm:border-t-0 sm:pt-0">
			<button
				v-if="item.status !== 'completed'"
				type="button"
				class="flex h-8 items-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl bg-optimal-soft px-3 text-xs font-extrabold text-optimal transition hover:opacity-90 active:scale-95"
				@click="emit('updateStatus', item.id, 'completed')"
			>
				<CheckCircle2 class="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
				<span>دیده شد</span>
			</button>

			<button
				type="button"
				class="flex h-8 w-8 items-center justify-center rounded-xl text-fg-muted transition hover:bg-elevated hover:text-fg active:scale-95"
				title="ویرایش اطلاعات اثر"
				@click="emit('edit', item)"
			>
				<Pencil class="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
			</button>

			<button
				type="button"
				class="flex h-8 w-8 items-center justify-center rounded-xl text-fg-muted transition hover:bg-failed-soft hover:text-failed active:scale-95"
				title="حذف اثر"
				@click="emit('delete', item.id)"
			>
				<Trash2 class="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
			</button>
		</div>
	</div>
</template>

================================================================
PATH: app/components/calendar/CalendarMonthGrid.vue
================================================================

<script setup lang="ts">
import {
	JALALI_GRID_WEEKDAYS,
	JALALI_GRID_WEEKDAYS_SHORT,
	toPersianDigits
} from '~/domain/calendar';
import { getDayStatusMeta } from '~/domain/constants';
import type { CalendarDayAggregate } from '~/domain/schemas';
import { BookHeart, BookOpen, CalendarClock, Clapperboard } from 'lucide-vue-next';

interface Props {
	leadingEmptyCells: number;
	days: CalendarDayAggregate[];
	selectedIso: string;
}

defineProps<Props>();

const emit = defineEmits<{
	(e: 'selectDay', iso: string): void;
}>();

function getDayCellClasses(day: CalendarDayAggregate, isSelected: boolean): string {
	const ring = isSelected
		? 'border-2 border-accent ring-2 ring-accent-border/60 shadow-(--shadow-card) scale-[1.02] z-10'
		: day.isToday
			? 'border-2 border-accent-border'
			: 'border border-border hover:border-border-strong';

	if (day.entry?.dayStatus === 'optimal') {
		return `${ring} bg-optimal-soft`;
	}
	if (day.entry?.dayStatus === 'partial') {
		return `${ring} bg-partial-soft`;
	}
	if (day.entry?.dayStatus === 'failed') {
		return `${ring} bg-failed-soft`;
	}
	return `${ring} bg-elevated/85 hover:bg-elevated`;
}
</script>

<template>
	<div class="rounded-3xl border border-border bg-surface p-4 shadow-(--shadow-soft) sm:p-5">
		<div class="mb-2.5 grid grid-cols-7 gap-1.5 text-center sm:gap-2">
			<div
				v-for="(weekday, idx) in JALALI_GRID_WEEKDAYS"
				:key="weekday"
				class="py-1 text-[11px] font-extrabold text-fg-secondary sm:text-xs"
			>
				<span class="hidden sm:inline">{{ weekday }}</span>
				<span class="sm:hidden">{{ JALALI_GRID_WEEKDAYS_SHORT[idx] }}</span>
			</div>
		</div>

		<div class="grid grid-cols-7 gap-1.5 sm:gap-2">
			<div
				v-for="idx in leadingEmptyCells"
				:key="`empty-${idx}`"
				class="aspect-square rounded-2xl border border-dashed border-border/40 bg-elevated/20"
			></div>

			<button
				v-for="day in days"
				:key="day.iso"
				type="button"
				class="group relative flex aspect-square flex-col justify-between overflow-hidden rounded-2xl p-1.5 text-right transition-all duration-150 sm:p-2"
				:class="getDayCellClasses(day, day.iso === selectedIso)"
				@click="emit('selectDay', day.iso)"
			>
				<div class="flex w-full items-center justify-between gap-1">
					<span
						class="leading-none font-black tracking-tight"
						:class="
							day.isToday
								? 'rounded-lg bg-accent px-1.5 py-0.5 text-xs text-accent-fg sm:text-sm'
								: 'text-sm text-fg sm:text-base'
						"
					>
						{{ toPersianDigits(day.jd) }}
					</span>

					<span
						v-if="day.entry"
						class="h-2 w-2 shrink-0 rounded-full"
						:class="getDayStatusMeta(day.entry.dayStatus).dotClass"
					></span>
				</div>

				<div class="mt-auto flex w-full items-center justify-start gap-1">
					<span
						v-if="day.events.length > 0"
						class="flex h-4 w-4 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-md bg-accent text-accent-fg shadow-2xs"
						:title="day.events.map((e) => e.title).join('، ')"
					>
						<CalendarClock class="h-2.5 w-2.5" />
					</span>
					<span
						v-if="day.booksActivity.length > 0"
						class="flex h-4 w-4 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-md bg-optimal text-on-solid shadow-2xs"
						title="کتاب"
					>
						<BookOpen class="h-2.5 w-2.5" />
					</span>
					<span
						v-if="day.mediaActivity.length > 0"
						class="flex h-4 w-4 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-md bg-bronze text-on-solid shadow-2xs"
						title="فیلم / انیمه"
					>
						<Clapperboard class="h-2.5 w-2.5" />
					</span>
					<span
						v-if="day.entry?.journalText"
						class="flex h-4 w-4 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-md bg-silver text-on-solid shadow-2xs"
						title="خاطره ثبت‌شده"
					>
						<BookHeart class="h-2.5 w-2.5" />
					</span>
				</div>
			</button>
		</div>
	</div>
</template>

================================================================
PATH: app/components/calendar/CalendarDayInspector.vue
================================================================

<script setup lang="ts">
import { ref } from 'vue';
import { toPersianDigits } from '~/domain/calendar';
import { getDayStatusMeta, getTierBadgeMeta, TIER_SCORES } from '~/domain/constants';
import type { CalendarDayAggregate, CreateCalendarEventInput } from '~/domain/schemas';
import BaseModal from '~/components/ui/BaseModal.vue';
import {
	ArrowLeft,
	BookOpen,
	Check,
	ChevronDown,
	ChevronUp,
	Clapperboard,
	Clock,
	Eye,
	Plus,
	Star,
	Trash2
} from 'lucide-vue-next';

interface Props {
	day: CalendarDayAggregate;
	isSubmittingEvent?: boolean;
}

const props = withDefaults(defineProps<Props>(), {
	isSubmittingEvent: false
});

const emit = defineEmits<{
	(e: 'createEvent', payload: CreateCalendarEventInput): void;
	(e: 'toggleEventComplete', eventId: string, isCompleted: boolean): void;
	(e: 'deleteEvent', eventId: string): void;
	(e: 'openTrackerDate', dateIso: string): void;
}>();

const isAnnualChecked = ref(false);
const showAddEventForm = ref(false);
const eventTitleInput = ref('');
const eventNotesInput = ref('');
const isModalOpen = ref(false);
const expandedHabitId = ref<string | null>(null);

function handleAddEvent() {
	if (!eventTitleInput.value.trim() || props.isSubmittingEvent) return;

	emit('createEvent', {
		title: eventTitleInput.value.trim(),
		eventType: 'occasion',
		isAnnual: isAnnualChecked.value,
		dateIso: props.day.iso,
		notes: eventNotesInput.value.trim() || undefined
	});

	eventTitleInput.value = '';
	eventNotesInput.value = '';
	isAnnualChecked.value = false;
	showAddEventForm.value = false;
}

function openModalWithHabit(habitId?: string) {
	expandedHabitId.value = habitId ?? null;
	isModalOpen.value = true;
}

function closeModal() {
	isModalOpen.value = false;
	expandedHabitId.value = null;
}

function toggleHabitExpanded(habitId: string) {
	expandedHabitId.value = expandedHabitId.value === habitId ? null : habitId;
}
</script>

<template>
	<aside class="space-y-4 rounded-3xl border border-border bg-surface p-5 shadow-(--shadow-soft) sm:p-6">
		<div class="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 border-b border-border pb-4">
			<div>
				<div class="flex items-center gap-2">
					<span
						v-if="day.entry"
						class="h-3 w-3 shrink-0 rounded-full"
						:class="getDayStatusMeta(day.entry.dayStatus).dotClass"
					></span>
					<h2 class="text-lg font-black text-fg sm:text-xl">{{ day.fullHumanLabel }}</h2>
				</div>
			</div>

			<div class="flex items-center gap-2">
				<button
					type="button"
					class="flex h-10 items-center gap-1.5 rounded-2xl bg-accent px-3.5 text-xs font-extrabold text-accent-fg shadow-xs transition hover:opacity-90"
					@click="showAddEventForm = !showAddEventForm"
				>
					<Plus class="h-4 w-4" />
					<span>ثبت رویداد</span>
				</button>

				<button
					type="button"
					class="flex h-10 items-center gap-1 rounded-2xl border border-border bg-elevated px-3 text-xs font-extrabold text-fg transition hover:border-border-strong"
					title="باز کردن در صفحه ثبت روزانه"
					@click="emit('openTrackerDate', day.iso)"
				>
					<span>ثبت روزانه</span>
					<ArrowLeft class="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
				</button>
			</div>
		</div>

		<!-- فرم افزودن رویداد به این تاریخ -->
		<form
			v-if="showAddEventForm"
			class="space-y-3 rounded-2xl border border-accent-border bg-accent-soft p-4"
			@submit.prevent="handleAddEvent"
		>
			<div>
				<input
					v-model="eventTitleInput"
					required
					placeholder="مثلاً: تولد، تحویل پروژه، سالگرد..."
					class="h-10 w-full rounded-xl border border-border bg-surface px-3.5 text-xs font-bold text-fg placeholder:font-normal placeholder:text-fg-muted focus:border-border-strong focus:outline-none"
				/>
			</div>

			<div>
				<input
					v-model="eventNotesInput"
					placeholder="توضیح کوتاه (اختیاری)..."
					class="h-9 w-full rounded-xl border border-border bg-surface px-3.5 text-xs text-fg placeholder:text-fg-muted focus:border-border-strong focus:outline-none"
				/>
			</div>

			<div class="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 pt-1">
				<label class="flex cursor-pointer items-center gap-2 text-xs font-bold text-fg">
					<input v-model="isAnnualChecked" type="checkbox" class="h-4 w-4 rounded accent-accent" />
					<span>تکرار هرساله در همین تاریخ شمسی</span>
				</label>

				<div class="flex items-center gap-1.5">
					<button
						type="button"
						class="h-9 rounded-xl px-3 text-xs font-bold text-fg-secondary hover:bg-surface"
						@click="showAddEventForm = false"
					>
						لغو
					</button>
					<button
						type="submit"
						:disabled="isSubmittingEvent"
						class="h-9 rounded-xl bg-accent px-4 text-xs font-extrabold text-accent-fg disabled:opacity-60"
					>
						{{ isSubmittingEvent ? 'در حال ثبت...' : 'ذخیره رویداد' }}
					</button>
				</div>
			</div>
		</form>

		<!-- فهرست رویدادهای این روز -->
		<div class="space-y-3">
			<h3 class="text-xs font-extrabold text-fg-secondary">رویدادها و مناسبت‌های این روز</h3>
			<div
				v-if="day.events.length === 0"
				class="rounded-2xl border border-dashed border-border bg-elevated p-4 text-center text-xs text-fg-muted"
			>
				رویداد یا مناسبتی برای این تاریخ ثبت نشده است.
			</div>
			<div v-else class="space-y-2">
				<div
					v-for="ev in day.events"
					:key="ev.id"
					class="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 rounded-2xl border border-accent-border bg-accent-soft p-3 text-accent-text"
				>
					<div class="min-w-0 space-y-0.5">
						<div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
							<span class="text-sm font-black text-fg" :class="{ 'line-through opacity-75': ev.isCompleted }">
								{{ ev.title }}
							</span>
							<span v-if="ev.isAnnual" class="rounded-lg border border-current px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-extrabold">
								هرساله
							</span>
						</div>
						<p v-if="ev.notes" class="text-xs opacity-90">{{ ev.notes }}</p>
					</div>

					<div class="flex shrink-0 items-center gap-1.5">
						<button
							v-if="!ev.isAnnual"
							type="button"
							class="flex h-8 w-8 items-center justify-center rounded-xl border border-current bg-surface text-fg transition hover:scale-105 active:scale-95"
							:title="ev.isCompleted ? 'انجام شد' : 'علامت انجام شدن'"
							@click="emit('toggleEventComplete', ev.id, !ev.isCompleted)"
						>
							<Check class="h-4 w-4" :class="ev.isCompleted ? 'text-optimal' : 'text-fg-muted'" />
						</button>

						<button
							type="button"
							class="flex h-8 w-8 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-surface text-fg-muted transition hover:text-failed active:scale-95"
							title="حذف رویداد"
							@click="emit('deleteEvent', ev.id)"
						>
							<Trash2 class="h-4 w-4" />
						</button>
					</div>
				</div>
			</div>
		</div>

		<!-- فعالیت‌های مطالعه و فیلم این روز -->
		<div v-if="day.booksActivity.length > 0 || day.mediaActivity.length > 0" class="space-y-2.5 border-t border-border pt-4">
			<h3 class="text-xs font-extrabold text-fg-secondary">کتاب‌ها و آثار تماشا شده در این روز</h3>
			<div class="space-y-2">
				<div
					v-for="item in day.booksActivity"
					:key="item.book.id + item.activityKind"
					class="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 rounded-2xl border border-optimal-border bg-optimal-soft px-3.5 py-2.5 text-xs"
				>
					<div class="flex items-center gap-2 font-extrabold text-fg">
						<BookOpen class="h-4 w-4 shrink-0 text-optimal" />
						<span>
							{{ item.activityKind === 'finished' ? 'اتمام کتاب:' : 'شروع کتاب:' }}
							{{ item.book.title }}
						</span>
					</div>
					<span v-if="item.book.rating" class="flex items-center gap-0.5 font-black text-gold-text">
						<Star class="h-3.5 w-3.5 fill-current" />
						{{ toPersianDigits(item.book.rating) }}
					</span>
				</div>

				<div
					v-for="item in day.mediaActivity"
					:key="item.media.id + item.activityKind"
					class="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 rounded-2xl border border-bronze-border bg-bronze-soft px-3.5 py-2.5 text-xs"
				>
					<div class="flex items-center gap-2 font-extrabold text-fg">
						<Clapperboard class="h-4 w-4 shrink-0 text-bronze-text" />
						<span>
							{{ item.activityKind === 'finished' ? 'تماشای کامل:' : 'شروع تماشا:' }}
							{{ item.media.title }}
						</span>
					</div>
					<span v-if="item.media.rating" class="flex items-center gap-0.5 font-black text-gold-text">
						<Star class="h-3.5 w-3.5 fill-current" />
						{{ toPersianDigits(item.media.rating) }}
					</span>
				</div>
			</div>
		</div>

		<!-- عملکرد عادات در این تاریخ -->
		<div class="space-y-2.5 border-t border-border pt-4">
			<div class="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2">
				<h3 class="text-xs font-extrabold text-fg-secondary">عملکرد طرح‌ها</h3>

				<button
					v-if="day.habitPerformances.length > 0"
					type="button"
					class="flex items-center gap-1 rounded-xl border border-border bg-elevated px-2.5 py-1 text-[11px] font-extrabold text-fg-secondary transition hover:border-accent-border hover:text-accent-text"
					@click="openModalWithHabit()"
				>
					<Eye class="h-3 w-3" />
					<span>دیدن جزئیات</span>
				</button>
			</div>

			<div
				v-if="day.habitPerformances.length === 0"
				class="rounded-2xl border border-dashed border-border bg-elevated p-4 text-center text-xs text-fg-muted"
			>
				عملکردی برای طرح‌ها در این تاریخ ثبت نشده است.
			</div>
			<div v-else class="grid grid-cols-1 gap-2 sm:grid-cols-2">
				<button
					v-for="perf in day.habitPerformances"
					:key="perf.habitId"
					type="button"
					class="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 rounded-2xl border border-border bg-elevated px-3 py-2 text-right text-xs transition hover:border-border-strong hover:bg-muted/60 active:scale-98"
					title="کلیک برای مشاهده جزئیات این طرح"
					@click="openModalWithHabit(perf.habitId)"
				>
					<span class="truncate font-bold text-fg">{{ perf.title }}</span>

					<span
						v-if="perf.trackingMode === 'tiered'"
						class="shrink-0 rounded-lg border px-2 py-0.5 text-[11px] font-black"
						:class="getTierBadgeMeta(perf.achievedTier).classes"
					>
						{{ getTierBadgeMeta(perf.achievedTier).label }}
					</span>
					<span
						v-else
						class="shrink-0 rounded-lg border border-accent-border bg-accent-soft px-2 py-0.5 text-[11px] font-black text-accent-text"
					>
						{{ toPersianDigits(perf.actualMinutes ?? 0) }} دقیقه
					</span>
				</button>
			</div>
		</div>
	</aside>

	<!-- مودال نمایش جزئیات قوانین تاریخی و عملکرد طرح‌ها -->
	<BaseModal
		v-if="isModalOpen"
		title="جزئیات عملکرد طرح‌ها"
		:subtitle="day.fullHumanLabel"
		max-width-class="max-w-xl"
		@close="closeModal"
	>
		<div class="space-y-3 overflow-y-auto p-4 sm:p-6 max-h-[75vh]">
			<div
				v-for="perf in day.habitPerformances"
				:key="perf.habitId"
				class="overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-border bg-elevated transition hover:border-border-strong"
			>
				<button
					type="button"
					class="flex w-full items-center justify-between gap-3 p-3.5 text-right transition hover:bg-muted/40"
					@click="toggleHabitExpanded(perf.habitId)"
				>
					<div class="min-w-0 space-y-1">
						<div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
							<span class="text-sm font-black text-fg">{{ perf.title }}</span>
							<span
								v-if="perf.trackingMode === 'tiered'"
								class="rounded-lg border px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-black"
								:class="getTierBadgeMeta(perf.achievedTier).classes"
							>
								{{ getTierBadgeMeta(perf.achievedTier).label }}
							</span>
							<span
								v-else
								class="rounded-lg border border-accent-border bg-accent-soft px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-black text-accent-text"
							>
								{{ toPersianDigits(perf.actualMinutes ?? 0) }} دقیقه
							</span>
							<span
								v-if="perf.isArchived"
								class="rounded-md border border-border bg-surface px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-bold text-fg-muted"
							>
								بایگانی‌شده
							</span>
						</div>

						<p class="text-xs font-medium text-fg-secondary">
							<template v-if="perf.trackingMode === 'tiered'">
								{{ perf.achievedRule || 'بدون توضیح ثبت‌شده' }}
							</template>
							<template v-else>
								انجام‌شده: {{ toPersianDigits(perf.actualMinutes ?? 0) }} دقیقه از هدفِ
								{{ toPersianDigits(perf.plannedMinutes ?? 0) }} دقیقه
							</template>
						</p>
					</div>

					<div class="flex items-center text-fg-secondary shrink-0">
						<ChevronUp v-if="expandedHabitId === perf.habitId" class="h-4 w-4 text-accent-text" />
						<ChevronDown v-else class="h-4 w-4 text-fg-muted" />
					</div>
				</button>

				<div
					v-if="expandedHabitId === perf.habitId"
					class="space-y-3 border-t border-border bg-surface p-4 text-xs"
				>
					<div
						v-if="perf.specSnapshot.scheduleLabel"
						class="flex items-center justify-between rounded-xl border border-border bg-elevated px-3.5 py-2"
					>
						<span class="flex items-center gap-1.5 font-bold text-fg-secondary">
							<Clock class="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-fg-muted" />
							<span>زمان انجام:</span>
						</span>
						<span class="font-extrabold text-fg">{{ perf.specSnapshot.scheduleLabel }}</span>
					</div>

					<div v-if="perf.trackingMode === 'tiered'" class="space-y-1.5">
						<div class="text-[11px] font-extrabold text-fg-secondary">شرایط سطوح در این روز:</div>
						<div
							v-if="perf.specSnapshot.bronzeDesc"
							class="flex items-center justify-between rounded-xl border px-3 py-2"
							:class="
								perf.achievedTier === TIER_SCORES.BRONZE
									? 'border-bronze-border bg-bronze-soft'
									: 'border-border bg-elevated/60'
							"
						>
							<span class="font-extrabold text-bronze-text">برنز:</span>
							<span class="font-bold text-fg">{{ perf.specSnapshot.bronzeDesc }}</span>
						</div>
						<div
							v-if="perf.specSnapshot.silverDesc && perf.specSnapshot.silverDesc !== '~'"
							class="flex items-center justify-between rounded-xl border px-3 py-2"
							:class="
								perf.achievedTier === TIER_SCORES.SILVER
									? 'border-silver-border bg-silver-soft'
									: 'border-border bg-elevated/60'
							"
						>
							<span class="font-extrabold text-silver-text">نقره:</span>
							<span class="font-bold text-fg">{{ perf.specSnapshot.silverDesc }}</span>
						</div>
						<div
							v-if="perf.specSnapshot.goldDesc && perf.specSnapshot.goldDesc !== '~'"
							class="flex items-center justify-between rounded-xl border px-3 py-2"
							:class="
								perf.achievedTier === TIER_SCORES.GOLD
									? 'border-gold-border bg-gold-soft'
									: 'border-border bg-elevated/60'
							"
						>
							<span class="font-extrabold text-gold-text">طلا:</span>
							<span class="font-bold text-fg">{{ perf.specSnapshot.goldDesc }}</span>
						</div>
					</div>

					<div v-else class="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 text-xs">
						<div class="rounded-xl border border-border bg-elevated p-2.5 text-center">
							<div class="text-[10px] font-bold text-fg-muted">انجام‌شده</div>
							<div class="mt-0.5 font-mono text-sm font-black text-accent-text">
								{{ toPersianDigits(perf.actualMinutes ?? 0) }} دقیقه
							</div>
						</div>
						<div class="rounded-xl border border-border bg-elevated p-2.5 text-center">
							<div class="text-[10px] font-bold text-fg-muted">هدف روز</div>
							<div class="mt-0.5 font-mono text-sm font-black text-fg">
								{{ toPersianDigits(perf.plannedMinutes ?? 0) }} دقیقه
							</div>
						</div>
					</div>

					<div
						v-if="perf.specSnapshot.notes"
						class="rounded-xl border border-border bg-elevated px-3 py-2 text-fg-secondary"
					>
						<strong class="font-extrabold text-fg">تبصره: </strong>
						<span>{{ perf.specSnapshot.notes }}</span>
					</div>
				</div>
			</div>
		</div>
	</BaseModal>
</template>

<script setup lang="ts">
import { ref, computed } from 'vue';
import { getTodayIso, isoToJalaliSlash, toPersianDigits } from '~/domain/calendar';
import { BOOK_RATING_OPTIONS, BOOK_STATUS_OPTIONS } from '~/domain/constants';
import type { BookEntity, BookStatus, CreateBookInput, UpdateBookInput } from '~/domain/schemas';
import BaseModal from '~/components/ui/BaseModal.vue';
import CustomSelect from '~/components/ui/CustomSelect.vue';
import { BookOpen, History } from 'lucide-vue-next';

interface Props {
	editingBook?: BookEntity | null;
	existingSuggestions?: Array<{ title: string; author: string | null }>;
	isSubmitting?: boolean;
}

const props = withDefaults(defineProps<Props>(), {
	editingBook: null,
	existingSuggestions: () => [],
	isSubmitting: false
});

const emit = defineEmits<{
	(e: 'close'): void;
	(e: 'submit', payload: CreateBookInput | UpdateBookInput): void;
}>();

const defaultJalaliToday = toPersianDigits(isoToJalaliSlash(getTodayIso()));

const title = ref(props.editingBook?.title ?? '');
const author = ref(props.editingBook?.author ?? '');
const selectedStatus = ref<BookStatus>(props.editingBook?.status ?? 'completed');
const selectedRating = ref<number>(props.editingBook?.rating ?? 5);
const startedJalali = ref(
	props.editingBook?.startedAtIso ? toPersianDigits(isoToJalaliSlash(props.editingBook.startedAtIso)) : ''
);
const finishedJalali = ref(
	props.editingBook?.finishedAtIso
		? toPersianDigits(isoToJalaliSlash(props.editingBook.finishedAtIso))
		: defaultJalaliToday
);
const notes = ref(props.editingBook?.notes ?? '');

const matchedSuggestion = computed(() =>
	props.existingSuggestions.find(
		(s) => s.title.trim().toLowerCase() === title.value.trim().toLowerCase()
	)
);

function handleTitleChange(e: Event) {
	const val = (e.target as HTMLInputElement).value;
	title.value = val;
	const match = props.existingSuggestions.find(
		(s) => s.title.trim().toLowerCase() === val.trim().toLowerCase()
	);
	if (match && match.author && !props.editingBook) {
		author.value = match.author;
	}
}

function handleSubmit() {
	if (!title.value.trim() || props.isSubmitting) return;

	if (props.editingBook) {
		emit('submit', {
			bookId: props.editingBook.id,
			title: title.value.trim(),
			author: author.value.trim() || undefined,
			status: selectedStatus.value,
			startedJalali: startedJalali.value.trim() || undefined,
			finishedJalali: finishedJalali.value.trim() || undefined,
			rating: selectedRating.value,
			notes: notes.value.trim() || undefined
		});
	} else {
		emit('submit', {
			title: title.value.trim(),
			author: author.value.trim() || undefined,
			status: selectedStatus.value,
			startedJalali: startedJalali.value.trim() || undefined,
			finishedJalali: finishedJalali.value.trim() || undefined,
			rating: selectedRating.value,
			notes: notes.value.trim() || undefined
		});
	}
}
</script>

<template>
	<BaseModal :title="editingBook ? 'ویرایش اطلاعات کتاب' : 'افزودن کتاب جدید'" @close="emit('close')">
		<template #headerIcon>
			<BookOpen class="h-4.5 w-4.5" />
		</template>

		<form class="space-y-4 overflow-y-auto p-5 sm:p-6" @submit.prevent="handleSubmit">
			<div>
				<div class="mb-1.5 flex items-center justify-between">
					<label for="bk-title" class="block text-xs font-bold text-fg-secondary">نام کتاب</label>
					<span
						v-if="matchedSuggestion && !editingBook"
						class="inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-md bg-accent-soft px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-black text-accent-text"
					>
						<History class="h-3 w-3" />
						<span>قبلاً در کتابخانه ثبت شده (خواندن مجدد)</span>
					</span>
				</div>

				<input
					id="bk-title"
					v-model="title"
					list="existing-books-datalist"
					required
					placeholder="مثلاً: عادت‌های اتمی یا صحیفه سجادیه..."
					class="h-11 w-full rounded-2xl border border-border bg-elevated px-4 text-xs font-bold text-fg placeholder:font-normal placeholder:text-fg-muted focus:border-border-strong focus:outline-none"
					@input="handleTitleChange"
				/>

				<datalist id="existing-books-datalist">
					<option v-for="s in existingSuggestions" :key="s.title" :value="s.title">
						{{ s.author ? `اثر: ${s.author}` : '' }}
					</option>
				</datalist>
			</div>

			<div>
				<label for="bk-author" class="mb-1.5 block text-xs font-bold text-fg-secondary">نویسنده / مترجم</label>
				<input
					id="bk-author"
					v-model="author"
					placeholder="مثلاً: جیمز کلیر"
					class="h-11 w-full rounded-2xl border border-border bg-elevated px-4 text-xs text-fg placeholder:text-fg-muted focus:border-border-strong focus:outline-none"
				/>
			</div>

			<div class="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
				<div>
					<label for="bk-status" class="mb-1.5 block text-xs font-bold text-fg-secondary">وضعیت</label>
					<CustomSelect
						id="bk-status"
						v-model="selectedStatus"
						:options="BOOK_STATUS_OPTIONS"
						trigger-class="text-fg"
					/>
				</div>
				<div>
					<label for="bk-rating" class="mb-1.5 block text-xs font-bold text-fg-secondary">امتیاز</label>
					<CustomSelect
						id="bk-rating"
						v-model="selectedRating"
						:options="BOOK_RATING_OPTIONS"
						trigger-class="text-gold"
					/>
				</div>
			</div>

			<div class="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
				<div>
					<label for="bk-start" class="mb-1.5 block text-xs font-bold text-fg-secondary">تاریخ شروع</label>
					<input
						id="bk-start"
						v-model="startedJalali"
						v-persian-digits
						dir="ltr"
						:placeholder="defaultJalaliToday"
						class="h-11 w-full rounded-2xl border border-border bg-elevated px-3 text-center font-mono text-xs font-bold text-fg placeholder:font-normal placeholder:text-fg-muted focus:border-border-strong focus:outline-none"
					/>
				</div>
				<div>
					<label for="bk-end" class="mb-1.5 block text-xs font-bold text-fg-secondary">تاریخ اتمام</label>
					<input
						id="bk-end"
						v-model="finishedJalali"
						v-persian-digits
						dir="ltr"
						class="h-11 w-full rounded-2xl border border-border bg-elevated px-3 text-center font-mono text-xs font-bold text-fg focus:border-border-strong focus:outline-none"
					/>
				</div>
			</div>

			<div>
				<label for="bk-notes" class="mb-1.5 block text-xs font-bold text-fg-secondary">
					یادداشت (اختیاری)
				</label>
				<textarea
					id="bk-notes"
					v-model="notes"
					rows="3"
					placeholder="مثلاً: نوبت دوم مطالعه در سال ۱۴۰۵ / نکات مهم فصل سوم..."
					class="w-full rounded-2xl border border-border bg-elevated p-3.5 text-xs text-fg placeholder:text-fg-muted focus:border-border-strong focus:outline-none"
				></textarea>
			</div>

			<div class="flex items-center justify-end gap-2.5 pt-2">
				<button
					type="button"
					class="h-11 rounded-2xl px-5 text-xs font-bold text-fg-secondary hover:bg-elevated"
					@click="emit('close')"
				>
					انصراف
				</button>
				<button
					type="submit"
					:disabled="isSubmitting"
					class="h-11 rounded-2xl bg-accent px-7 text-xs font-extrabold text-accent-fg shadow-xs transition hover:opacity-90 active:scale-98 disabled:opacity-60"
				>
					{{ isSubmitting ? 'در حال ثبت...' : editingBook ? 'ذخیره تغییرات' : 'ثبت در کتابخانه' }}
				</button>
			</div>
		</form>
	</BaseModal>
</template>

<script setup lang="ts">

	(e: 'delete', bookId: string): void;

const statusMeta = computed(

		class="group flex flex-col justify-between gap-3 rounded-2xl border border-border bg-surface px-4 py-3 shadow-(--shadow-card) transition hover:border-border-strong sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:px-5 sm:py-3.5"

================================================================
PATH: app/pages/login/index.vue
================================================================

<script setup lang="ts">
import { ref, computed, onMounted, onUnmounted } from 'vue';
import { useRoute, useRouter } from 'vue-router';
import { useClientAuth } from '~/composables/useClientAuth';
import { useSyncEngine } from '~/composables/useSyncEngine';
import { toPersianDigits } from '~/domain/calendar';
import { Eye, EyeOff, KeyRound, Lock, ShieldCheck, Timer, WifiOff } from 'lucide-vue-next';

definePageMeta({
	layout: false
});

const route = useRoute();
const router = useRouter();
const clientAuth = useClientAuth();
const syncEngine = useSyncEngine();

function resolvePostLoginPath(): string {
	const target = route.query.redirectTo;
	if (typeof target === 'string' && target.startsWith('/') && !target.startsWith('//')) {
		return target;
	}
	return '/';
}

const password = ref('');
const confirmPassword = ref('');
const honeypotWebsite = ref('');
const showPassword = ref(false);
const isSubmitting = ref(false);
const errorMessage = ref<string | null>(null);
const failedAttempts = ref(0);
const activeAttemptNumber = ref(0);
const countdownRemainingMs = ref(0);
const countdownTotalMs = ref(0);
let countdownInterval: ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | null = null;

const isClientOffline = computed(() => !syncEngine.isOnline.value);

const showMinimalTimer = computed(
	() => activeAttemptNumber.value >= 4 && countdownRemainingMs.value > 0
);

const formattedCountdownSeconds = computed(() => {
	const secs = (countdownRemainingMs.value / 1000).toFixed(1);
	return toPersianDigits(secs);
});

const timerProgressPercent = computed(() => {
	if (countdownTotalMs.value <= 0) return 0;
	return Math.max(0, Math.min(100, (countdownRemainingMs.value / countdownTotalMs.value) * 100));
});

function resolveAttemptDelayMs(attemptNumber: number): number {
	if (attemptNumber <= 1) return 0;
	if (attemptNumber === 2) return 2000;
	if (attemptNumber === 3) return 3000;
	return 5000;
}

function stopCountdownTimer() {
	if (countdownInterval) {
		clearInterval(countdownInterval);
		countdownInterval = null;
	}
	countdownRemainingMs.value = 0;
	countdownTotalMs.value = 0;
}

function startCountdownTimer(durationMs: number): Promise<void> {
	stopCountdownTimer();
	if (durationMs <= 0) return Promise.resolve();

	countdownTotalMs.value = durationMs;
	countdownRemainingMs.value = durationMs;
	const endTime = Date.now() + durationMs;

	return new Promise((resolve) => {
		countdownInterval = setInterval(() => {
			const remaining = Math.max(0, endTime - Date.now());
			countdownRemainingMs.value = remaining;
			if (remaining <= 0) {
				stopCountdownTimer();
				resolve();
			}
		}, 80);
	});
}

function extractErrorText(err: unknown, fallback: string): string {
	if (err && typeof err === 'object' && 'data' in err) {
		const data = (err as {
			data?: {
				statusMessage?: string;
				data?: { failedAttempts?: number };
			};
		}).data;
		if (typeof data?.data?.failedAttempts === 'number') {
			failedAttempts.value = Math.max(failedAttempts.value, data.data.failedAttempts);
		}
		if (data?.statusMessage) return data.statusMessage;
	}
	return err instanceof Error ? err.message : fallback;
}

onMounted(async () => {
	syncEngine.init();
	const state = await clientAuth.ensureSessionChecked();
	if (state.isAuthenticated) {
		router.push(resolvePostLoginPath());
	}
});

onUnmounted(() => {
	stopCountdownTimer();
});

async function handleLogin() {
	if (!password.value.trim() || isSubmitting.value) return;

	if (honeypotWebsite.value.trim().length > 0) {
		isSubmitting.value = true;
		errorMessage.value = null;
		await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 2000));
		errorMessage.value = 'رمز عبور وارد شده نادرست است';
		isSubmitting.value = false;
		return;
	}

	isSubmitting.value = true;
	errorMessage.value = null;

	const currentAttempt = failedAttempts.value + 1;
	activeAttemptNumber.value = currentAttempt;
	const delayMs = resolveAttemptDelayMs(currentAttempt);
	const timerPromise = startCountdownTimer(delayMs);

	try {
		if (navigator.onLine) {
			const [res] = await Promise.all([
				$fetch('/api/auth/login', {
					method: 'POST',
					body: {
						password: password.value,
						website: honeypotWebsite.value
					}
				}),
				timerPromise
			]);

			if (res) {
				failedAttempts.value = 0;
				await clientAuth.saveClientCredential(password.value);
				router.push(resolvePostLoginPath());
				return;
			}
		}

		const [offlineRes] = await Promise.all([
			clientAuth.loginOffline(password.value),
			timerPromise
		]);

		if (offlineRes.success) {
			failedAttempts.value = 0;
			router.push(resolvePostLoginPath());
		} else {
			failedAttempts.value = currentAttempt;
			errorMessage.value = offlineRes.error || 'رمز عبور وارد شده نادرست است';
		}
	} catch (err: unknown) {
		await timerPromise;
		failedAttempts.value = Math.max(failedAttempts.value, currentAttempt);
		errorMessage.value = extractErrorText(err, 'رمز عبور وارد شده نادرست است');
	} finally {
		stopCountdownTimer();
		isSubmitting.value = false;
	}
}

async function handleSetup() {
	if (honeypotWebsite.value.trim().length > 0) {
		errorMessage.value = 'درخواست نامعتبر است';
		return;
	}
	if (password.value !== confirmPassword.value) {
		errorMessage.value = 'رمز عبور و تکرار آن یکسان نیستند';
		return;
	}
	isSubmitting.value = true;
	errorMessage.value = null;

	try {
		await $fetch('/api/auth/setup', {
			method: 'POST',
			body: {
				password: password.value,
				confirmPassword: confirmPassword.value,
				website: honeypotWebsite.value
			}
		});

		await clientAuth.saveClientCredential(password.value);
		router.push(resolvePostLoginPath());
	} catch (err: unknown) {
		errorMessage.value = extractErrorText(err, 'خطا در ثبت رمز عبور');
	} finally {
		isSubmitting.value = false;
	}
}
</script>

<template>
	<div class="flex min-h-screen items-center justify-center bg-base p-4 text-fg">
		<div class="w-full max-w-md rounded-3xl border border-border bg-surface p-6 shadow-(--shadow-modal) sm:p-8">
			<div class="mb-6 flex flex-col items-center text-center">
				<div class="mb-4 flex h-14 w-14 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl border border-accent-border bg-accent-soft text-accent-text shadow-xs">
					<Lock v-if="clientAuth.isPasswordConfigured.value" class="h-7 w-7" />
					<ShieldCheck v-else class="h-7 w-7" />
				</div>

				<div class="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-center gap-2">
					<h1 class="text-xl font-black tracking-tight text-fg sm:text-2xl">
						{{ clientAuth.isPasswordConfigured.value ? 'ورود به فضای شخصی' : 'تعیین رمز عبور اولیه' }}
					</h1>
					<span
						v-if="isClientOffline"
						class="inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-lg border border-gold-border bg-gold-soft px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-black text-gold-text"
					>
						<WifiOff class="h-3 w-3" />
						<span>ورود آفلاین</span>
					</span>
				</div>

				<p v-if="!clientAuth.isPasswordConfigured.value" class="mt-1.5 text-xs leading-6 font-bold text-fg-secondary">
					یک رمز عبور برای محافظت از اطلاعات شخصی خود تعیین کنید.
				</p>
			</div>

			<div v-if="errorMessage" class="mb-5 rounded-2xl border border-failed-border bg-failed-soft px-4 py-3 text-center text-xs font-extrabold text-failed">
				{{ errorMessage }}
			</div>

			<!-- فرم لاگین -->
			<form v-if="clientAuth.isPasswordConfigured.value" class="space-y-4" @submit.prevent="handleLogin">
				<!-- فیلد مخفی تله برای ربات‌ها (Honeypot) -->
				<div class="pointer-events-none absolute -z-10 h-0 w-0 overflow-hidden opacity-0" aria-hidden="true">
					<label for="login-website">Website</label>
					<input
						id="login-website"
						v-model="honeypotWebsite"
						type="text"
						name="website"
						tabindex="-1"
						autocomplete="off"
					/>
				</div>

				<div>
					<label for="login-pass" class="mb-1.5 block text-xs font-extrabold text-fg">رمز عبور</label>
					<div class="relative">
						<input
							id="login-pass"
							v-model="password"
							:type="showPassword ? 'text' : 'password'"
							required
							dir="ltr"
							placeholder="••••••••"
							class="h-12 w-full rounded-2xl border border-border bg-elevated pr-4 pl-11 text-center font-mono text-sm font-bold text-fg focus:border-border-strong focus:bg-surface focus:outline-none"
						/>
						<button
							type="button"
							class="absolute top-1/2 left-3 -translate-y-1/2 rounded-lg p-1.5 text-fg-muted transition hover:text-fg"
							aria-label="نمایش یا مخفی‌سازی رمز"
							@click="showPassword = !showPassword"
						>
							<EyeOff v-if="showPassword" class="h-4 w-4" />
							<Eye v-else class="h-4 w-4" />
						</button>
					</div>
				</div>

				<!-- تایمر مینیمال از تلاش چهارم به بعد -->
				<div
					v-if="showMinimalTimer"
					class="overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-border bg-elevated px-3.5 py-2.5 transition-all"
				>
					<div class="flex items-center justify-between text-xs font-extrabold text-fg-secondary">
						<span class="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5">
							<Timer class="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-accent-text animate-pulse" />
							<span>بررسی امنیتی</span>
						</span>
						<span class="font-mono text-fg">{{ formattedCountdownSeconds }} ثانیه</span>
					</div>
					<div class="mt-2 h-1 w-full overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-surface">
						<div
							class="h-full rounded-full bg-accent transition-all duration-75"
							:style="{ width: `${timerProgressPercent}%` }"
						></div>
					</div>
				</div>

				<button
					type="submit"
					:disabled="isSubmitting"
					class="flex h-12 w-full items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-2xl bg-accent text-sm font-extrabold text-accent-fg shadow-xs transition hover:opacity-90 active:scale-98 disabled:opacity-60"
				>
					<KeyRound class="h-4 w-4" />
					<span>{{ isSubmitting ? 'در حال بررسی...' : 'ورود' }}</span>
				</button>
			</form>

			<!-- فرم تعریف رمز اولیه -->
			<form v-else class="space-y-4" @submit.prevent="handleSetup">
				<!-- فیلد مخفی تله برای ربات‌ها (Honeypot) -->
				<div class="pointer-events-none absolute -z-10 h-0 w-0 overflow-hidden opacity-0" aria-hidden="true">
					<label for="setup-website">Website</label>
					<input
						id="setup-website"
						v-model="honeypotWebsite"
						type="text"
						name="website"
						tabindex="-1"
						autocomplete="off"
					/>
				</div>

				<div>
					<label for="setup-pass" class="mb-1.5 block text-xs font-extrabold text-fg">رمز عبور جدید</label>
					<input
						id="setup-pass"
						v-model="password"
						type="password"
						required
						minlength="4"
						dir="ltr"
						placeholder="حداقل ۴ کاراکتر"
						class="h-12 w-full rounded-2xl border border-border bg-elevated px-4 text-center font-mono text-sm font-bold text-fg focus:border-border-strong focus:outline-none"
					/>
				</div>

				<div>
					<label for="setup-conf" class="mb-1.5 block text-xs font-extrabold text-fg">تکرار رمز عبور</label>
					<input
						id="setup-conf"
						v-model="confirmPassword"
						type="password"
						required
						minlength="4"
						dir="ltr"
						placeholder="تکرار رمز عبور"
						class="h-12 w-full rounded-2xl border border-border bg-elevated px-4 text-center font-mono text-sm font-bold text-fg focus:border-border-strong focus:outline-none"
					/>
				</div>

				<button
					type="submit"
					:disabled="isSubmitting"
					class="flex h-12 w-full items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-2xl bg-accent text-sm font-extrabold text-accent-fg shadow-xs transition hover:opacity-90 active:scale-98 disabled:opacity-60"
				>
					<ShieldCheck class="h-4 w-4" />
					<span>{{ isSubmitting ? 'در حال ثبت...' : 'ثبت رمز عبور و ورود' }}</span>
				</button>
			</form>
		</div>
	</div>
</template>

PATH: app/pages/timeline/index.vue

<script setup lang="ts">
import { ref, computed } from 'vue';
import { useAppDatabase } from '~/composables/useAppDatabase';
import { useDexieLiveQuery } from '~/composables/useDexieLiveQuery';
import { extractLifecycleRuleDiffs } from '~/core/habit-engine';
import { formatJalaliHuman } from '~/domain/calendar';
import { LIFECYCLE_EVENT_META, LIFECYCLE_FILTER_OPTIONS } from '~/domain/constants';
import type { LifecycleEventType } from '~/domain/schemas';
import { ArrowLeft, Filter, GitCommitHorizontal } from 'lucide-vue-next';

const db = useAppDatabase();
const events = useDexieLiveQuery(() => db.lifecycleEvents.orderBy('createdAtIso').reverse().toArray(), []);
const isEventsLoading = events.isLoading;

const selectedFilter = ref<LifecycleEventType | 'ALL'>('ALL');

const filteredEvents = computed(() =>
	selectedFilter.value === 'ALL'
		? events.value
		: events.value.filter((e) => e.eventType === selectedFilter.value)
);
</script>

<template>
	<div class="space-y-6 pb-16">
		<div class="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-4 rounded-3xl border border-border bg-surface p-5 shadow-(--shadow-soft) sm:px-7">
			<div class="flex items-center gap-3">
				<div class="flex h-11 w-11 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl border border-accent-border bg-accent-soft text-accent-text">
					<GitCommitHorizontal class="h-6 w-6" />
				</div>
				<h1 class="text-xl font-black text-fg sm:text-2xl">تاریخچه تغییرات</h1>
			</div>

			<div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5 text-xs">
				<Filter class="h-4 w-4 text-fg-muted" />
				<button
					v-for="item in LIFECYCLE_FILTER_OPTIONS"
					:key="item.id"
					type="button"
					class="rounded-xl border px-3 py-1.5 font-extrabold transition"
					:class="
						selectedFilter === item.id
							? 'border-accent bg-accent text-accent-fg shadow-xs'
							: 'border-border bg-elevated text-fg-secondary hover:text-fg'
					"
					@click="selectedFilter = item.id"
				>
					{{ item.label }}
				</button>
			</div>
		</div>

		<div v-if="isEventsLoading" class="space-y-4" aria-busy="true">
			<div v-for="idx in 4" :key="idx" class="h-28 animate-pulse rounded-2xl border border-border bg-surface"></div>
		</div>

		<div v-else-if="filteredEvents.length === 0" class="rounded-3xl border border-dashed border-border bg-surface p-12 text-center text-xs font-bold text-fg-muted">
			رویدادی متناسب با این فیلتر یافت نشد.
		</div>

		<div v-else class="relative space-y-4 before:absolute before:top-4 before:right-5 before:bottom-4 before:w-0.5 before:bg-border">
			<div v-for="ev in filteredEvents" :key="ev.id" class="relative pr-12">
				<div class="absolute top-4 right-3.5 h-3.5 w-3.5 rounded-full border-2 border-accent bg-surface"></div>

				<div class="rounded-2xl border border-border bg-surface p-5 shadow-(--shadow-soft) transition hover:border-border-strong">
					<div class="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2">
						<div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
							<span
								class="rounded-lg border px-2.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-extrabold"
								:class="LIFECYCLE_EVENT_META[ev.eventType]?.badgeClasses"
							>
								{{ LIFECYCLE_EVENT_META[ev.eventType]?.label }}
							</span>
							<h3 class="text-sm font-extrabold text-fg">{{ ev.habitTitleSnapshot }}</h3>
						</div>

						<span class="text-xs font-medium text-fg-muted">{{ formatJalaliHuman(ev.effectiveDateIso).fullLabel }}</span>
					</div>

					<p
						v-if="ev.eventType === 'CONVERTED_TO_SUBHABIT' || ev.eventType === 'CADENCE_UPDATED' || ev.eventType === 'STATUS_CHANGED'"
						class="mt-2 text-xs font-semibold text-fg-secondary"
					>
						{{ ev.summary }}
					</p>

					<!-- جزئیات تغییرات و مقادیر قبلی/جدید (Diff View) -->
					<div v-if="extractLifecycleRuleDiffs(ev.previousStateJson, ev.newStateJson).length > 0" class="mt-3 grid grid-cols-1 gap-2 sm:grid-cols-2">
						<div
							v-for="diff in extractLifecycleRuleDiffs(ev.previousStateJson, ev.newStateJson)"
							:key="diff.fieldLabel"
							class="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 rounded-xl border border-border bg-elevated px-3 py-2 text-xs"
						>
							<span class="font-extrabold text-fg-secondary">{{ diff.fieldLabel }}:</span>
							<div class="flex items-center gap-1.5 font-bold">
								<span class="text-fg-muted line-through">{{ diff.previousValue }}</span>
								<ArrowLeft class="h-3 w-3 text-accent-text" />
								<span class="font-extrabold text-accent-text">{{ diff.newValue }}</span>
							</div>
						</div>
					</div>

					<div v-if="ev.reason && ev.reason !== 'تعریف طرح جدید'" class="mt-2.5 rounded-xl border border-border bg-elevated px-3 py-2 text-xs text-fg-secondary">
						<strong class="font-extrabold text-fg">دلیل: </strong>
						<span>{{ ev.reason }}</span>
					</div>
				</div>
			</div>
		</div>
	</div>
</template>

================================================================
PATH: app/pages/analytics/index.vue

<script setup lang="ts">
import { computed } from 'vue';
import { useRoute, useRouter } from 'vue-router';
import { useAppDatabase } from '~/composables/useAppDatabase';
import { useDexieLiveQuery } from '~/composables/useDexieLiveQuery';
import { buildFullAnalyticsReport } from '~/core/analytics-engine';
import { getYesterdayIso, isoToJalaliSlash, toPersianDigits } from '~/domain/calendar';
import type { AnalyticsTimeRange } from '~/domain/schemas';
import CustomSelect from '~/components/ui/CustomSelect.vue';
import AnalyticsSummaryCards from '~/components/analytics/AnalyticsSummaryCards.vue';
import HabitTrendChart from '~/components/analytics/HabitTrendChart.vue';
import HabitHeatmapMatrix from '~/components/analytics/HabitHeatmapMatrix.vue';
import HabitEvolutionTimeline from '~/components/analytics/HabitEvolutionTimeline.vue';
import HabitsComparisonTable from '~/components/analytics/HabitsComparisonTable.vue';
import { BarChart3 } from 'lucide-vue-next';

const route = useRoute();
const router = useRouter();
const db = useAppDatabase();

const habits = useDexieLiveQuery(() => db.habits.orderBy('sortOrder').toArray(), []);
const isHabitsLoading = habits.isLoading;
const rawEntries = useDexieLiveQuery(() => db.dailyEntries.toArray(), []);
const rawLogs = useDexieLiveQuery(() => db.habitLogs.toArray(), []);
const lifecycleEvents = useDexieLiveQuery(() => db.lifecycleEvents.toArray(), []);

const selectedRange = computed<AnalyticsTimeRange>(() => {
	const r = route.query.range as AnalyticsTimeRange | undefined;
	if (r && ['7d', '30d', '90d', 'all'].includes(r)) return r;
	return '30d';
});

const selectedHabitId = computed(() => {
	const h = route.query.habit as string | undefined;
	if (h && habits.value.some((item) => item.id === h)) return h;
	const firstActive = habits.value.find((item) => item.status !== 'archived') ?? habits.value[0];
	return firstActive?.id ?? '';
});

const rangeOptions: ReadonlyArray<{ value: AnalyticsTimeRange; label: string }> = [
	{ value: '7d', label: '۷ روز اخیر' },
	{ value: '30d', label: '۳۰ روز اخیر' },
	{ value: '90d', label: '۹۰ روز اخیر' },
	{ value: 'all', label: 'کل زمان' }
];

const report = computed(() =>
	buildFullAnalyticsReport({
		habits: habits.value,
		entries: rawEntries.value,
		logs: rawLogs.value,
		lifecycleEvents: lifecycleEvents.value,
		selectedHabitId: selectedHabitId.value,
		range: selectedRange.value,
		fallbackEndDateIso: getYesterdayIso()
	})
);

const habitSelectOptions = computed(() =>
	report.value.selectableHabits.map((item) => ({
		value: item.habit.id,
		label: item.displayLabel
	}))
);

function setRange(rangeVal: AnalyticsTimeRange) {
	router.push({ query: { ...route.query, range: rangeVal } });
}

function selectHabitAndScroll(habitId: string) {
	router.push({ query: { ...route.query, habit: habitId } });
	if (import.meta.client) {
		window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' });
	}
}
</script>

<template>
	<div class="space-y-6 pb-16">
		<section class="rounded-3xl border border-border bg-surface p-5 shadow-(--shadow-soft) sm:p-6">
			<div class="flex flex-col justify-between gap-4 lg:flex-row lg:items-center">
				<div class="flex items-center gap-3.5">
					<div class="flex h-11 w-11 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl border border-accent-border bg-accent-soft text-accent-text">
						<BarChart3 class="h-5 w-5" />
					</div>
					<div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2.5">
						<h1 class="text-xl font-black tracking-tight text-fg sm:text-2xl">آمار و روند طرح‌ها</h1>
						<span dir="ltr" class="rounded-xl border border-border bg-elevated px-2.5 py-0.5 font-mono text-xs font-bold text-fg-secondary">
							{{ toPersianDigits(isoToJalaliSlash(report.startDateIso)) }} → {{ toPersianDigits(isoToJalaliSlash(report.endDateIso)) }}
						</span>
					</div>
				</div>

				<div class="flex flex-col gap-2.5 sm:flex-row sm:flex-wrap sm:items-center">
					<div v-if="habitSelectOptions.length > 0" class="w-full sm:w-64">
						<CustomSelect
							:model-value="selectedHabitId"
							:options="habitSelectOptions"
							trigger-class="h-11 text-fg"
							@select="selectHabitAndScroll(String($event))"
						/>
					</div>

					<div class="flex items-center gap-1 rounded-2xl border border-border bg-elevated p-1">
						<button
							v-for="opt in rangeOptions"
							:key="opt.value"
							type="button"
							class="flex-1 rounded-xl px-3 py-2 text-xs font-extrabold whitespace-nowrap transition sm:flex-initial"
							:class="selectedRange === opt.value ? 'border border-accent-border bg-accent-soft text-accent-text shadow-xs' : 'text-fg-secondary hover:text-fg'"
							@click="setRange(opt.value)"
						>
							{{ opt.label }}
						</button>
					</div>
				</div>
			</div>
		</section>

		<div v-if="isHabitsLoading" class="space-y-6" aria-busy="true">
			<div class="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-3">
				<div v-for="idx in 3" :key="idx" class="h-28 animate-pulse rounded-3xl border border-border bg-surface"></div>
			</div>
			<div class="h-80 animate-pulse rounded-3xl border border-border bg-surface"></div>
		</div>

		<div v-else-if="habits.length === 0" class="rounded-3xl border border-dashed border-border bg-surface p-12 text-center">
			<h2 class="text-base font-extrabold text-fg">هنوز طرحی در سیستم تعریف نشده است</h2>
			<NuxtLink to="/architect" class="mt-4 inline-flex h-11 items-center rounded-2xl bg-accent px-5 text-xs font-extrabold text-accent-fg shadow-xs">
				تعریف اولین طرح
			</NuxtLink>
		</div>

		<template v-else-if="report.selectedHabitReport">
			<AnalyticsSummaryCards :report="report.selectedHabitReport" />

			<HabitTrendChart :report="report.selectedHabitReport" />

			<HabitHeatmapMatrix :report="report.selectedHabitReport" />

			<HabitEvolutionTimeline
				:habit-title="report.selectedHabitReport.habit.title"
				:evolution-timeline="report.selectedHabitReport.evolutionTimeline"
			/>

			<HabitsComparisonTable
				:rows="report.comparisonRows"
				:selected-habit-id="selectedHabitId"
				@select-habit="selectHabitAndScroll"
			/>
		</template>
	</div>
</template>

================================================================
PATH: app/pages/memories/index.vue
================================================================

<script setup lang="ts">
import { ref, computed } from 'vue';
import { useAppDatabase } from '~/composables/useAppDatabase';
import { useDexieLiveQuery } from '~/composables/useDexieLiveQuery';
import { formatJalaliHuman, isoToJalaliSlash, toPersianDigits } from '~/domain/calendar';
import { getDayStatusMeta } from '~/domain/constants';
import { ArrowDownWideNarrow, ArrowUpNarrowWide, BookHeart, Calendar, Search } from 'lucide-vue-next';

const db = useAppDatabase();
const entries = useDexieLiveQuery(
	() =>
		db.dailyEntries
			.filter((e) => Boolean(e.journalText && e.journalText.trim().length > 0))
			.toArray(),
	[]
);
const isEntriesLoading = entries.isLoading;

const sortOrder = ref<'desc' | 'asc'>('desc');
const searchQuery = ref('');

const displayedMemories = computed(() => {
	const q = searchQuery.value.trim().toLowerCase();
	const filtered = q
		? entries.value.filter((m) => m.journalText?.toLowerCase().includes(q))
		: entries.value;

	return [...filtered].sort((a, b) => {
		if (a.dateIso === b.dateIso) return 0;
		if (sortOrder.value === 'desc') return b.dateIso > a.dateIso ? 1 : -1;
		return a.dateIso > b.dateIso ? 1 : -1;
	});
});
</script>

<template>
	<div class="space-y-8 pb-16">
		<div class="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-4 rounded-3xl border border-border bg-surface p-5 shadow-(--shadow-soft) sm:px-7">
			<div class="flex items-center gap-3.5">
				<div class="flex h-12 w-12 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl border border-accent-border bg-accent-soft text-accent-text">
					<BookHeart class="h-6 w-6" />
				</div>
				<div>
					<h1 class="text-xl font-black tracking-tight text-fg sm:text-2xl">دفتر خاطرات</h1>
					<span class="text-xs font-bold text-fg-secondary">
						{{ toPersianDigits(entries.length) }} خاطره ثبت‌شده
					</span>
				</div>
			</div>

			<div class="flex w-full flex-col gap-2.5 sm:w-auto sm:flex-row sm:flex-wrap sm:items-center sm:gap-3">
				<div v-if="entries.length > 1" class="relative w-full sm:w-auto">
					<Search class="pointer-events-none absolute top-1/2 right-3.5 h-4 w-4 -translate-y-1/2 text-fg-muted" />
					<input
						v-model="searchQuery"
						type="search"
						placeholder="جستجو در خاطرات..."
						class="h-11 w-full rounded-2xl border border-border bg-elevated pr-9 pl-3.5 text-xs font-bold text-fg placeholder:font-normal placeholder:text-fg-muted focus:border-border-strong focus:outline-none sm:w-56"
					/>
				</div>

				<div class="flex w-full items-center gap-1 rounded-2xl border border-border bg-elevated p-1 sm:w-auto">
					<button
						type="button"
						class="flex flex-1 items-center justify-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl px-3 py-2 text-xs font-extrabold transition sm:flex-initial sm:px-3.5"
						:class="
							sortOrder === 'desc'
								? 'border border-accent-border bg-accent-soft text-accent-text shadow-xs'
								: 'text-fg-secondary hover:text-fg'
						"
						@click="sortOrder = 'desc'"
					>
						<ArrowDownWideNarrow class="h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0" />
						<span>جدید به قدیم</span>
					</button>
					<button
						type="button"
						class="flex flex-1 items-center justify-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl px-3 py-2 text-xs font-extrabold transition sm:flex-initial sm:px-3.5"
						:class="
							sortOrder === 'asc'
								? 'border border-accent-border bg-accent-soft text-accent-text shadow-xs'
								: 'text-fg-secondary hover:text-fg'
						"
						@click="sortOrder = 'asc'"
					>
						<ArrowUpNarrowWide class="h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0" />
						<span>قدیم به جدید</span>
					</button>
				</div>
			</div>
		</div>

		<div v-if="isEntriesLoading" class="mx-auto max-w-3xl space-y-5" aria-busy="true">
			<div v-for="idx in 3" :key="idx" class="h-44 animate-pulse rounded-3xl border border-border bg-surface"></div>
		</div>

		<div
			v-else-if="entries.length === 0"
			class="flex min-h-80 flex-col items-center justify-center rounded-3xl border border-dashed border-border bg-surface p-12 text-center"
		>
			<div class="mb-4 flex h-16 w-16 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl bg-elevated text-fg-muted">
				<BookHeart class="h-8 w-8" />
			</div>
			<h2 class="text-base font-extrabold text-fg">هنوز خاطره‌ای ثبت نشده است</h2>
			<p class="mt-1.5 text-sm text-fg-muted">
				در صفحهٔ «ثبت روزانه» می‌توانید پایین فهرست طرح‌ها خاطره هر روز را بنویسید.
			</p>
			<NuxtLink
				to="/"
				class="mt-5 inline-flex h-11 items-center rounded-2xl bg-accent px-5 text-xs font-extrabold text-accent-fg transition hover:opacity-90 shadow-xs"
			>
				ثبت اولین خاطره
			</NuxtLink>
		</div>

		<div v-else-if="displayedMemories.length === 0" class="rounded-3xl border border-border bg-surface p-10 text-center text-sm text-fg-muted">
			موردی مطابق با عبارت جستجو شده یافت نشد.
		</div>

		<div v-else class="mx-auto max-w-3xl space-y-5">
			<article
				v-for="item in displayedMemories"
				:key="item.dateIso"
				class="group rounded-3xl border border-border bg-surface p-6 shadow-(--shadow-soft) transition hover:border-border-strong sm:p-8"
			>
				<header class="mb-4 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 border-b border-border pb-4">
					<div class="flex items-center gap-3">
						<span
							class="h-2.5 w-2.5 rounded-full"
							:class="getDayStatusMeta(item.dayStatus).dotClass"
							title="وضعیت روز"
						></span>
						<h2 class="text-lg font-black text-fg">{{ formatJalaliHuman(item.dateIso).fullLabel }}</h2>
					</div>

					<NuxtLink
						:to="`/?date=${item.dateIso}`"
						class="flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl border border-border bg-elevated px-3 py-1.5 font-mono text-xs font-bold text-fg-secondary transition hover:border-border-strong hover:text-fg"
						title="مشاهده یا ویرایش این روز"
					>
						<Calendar class="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-fg-muted" />
						<span dir="ltr">{{ toPersianDigits(isoToJalaliSlash(item.dateIso)) }}</span>
					</NuxtLink>
				</header>

				<p class="whitespace-pre-line text-base leading-8 font-normal text-fg select-text">
					{{ item.journalText }}
				</p>

				<footer v-if="item.tags.length > 0" class="mt-5 flex flex-wrap gap-1.5 pt-2">
					<span
						v-for="tag in item.tags"
						:key="tag"
						class="rounded-xl bg-elevated px-2.5 py-1 text-[11px] font-bold text-fg-secondary"
					>
						#{{ tag }}
					</span>
				</footer>
			</article>
		</div>
	</div>
</template>

<script setup lang="ts">
import { computed } from 'vue';
import { useRoute, useRouter } from 'vue-router';
import { useDailyTracker } from '~/composables/useDailyTracker';
import { useLocalRepository } from '~/composables/useLocalRepository';
import { computeUpcomingEventsBanner } from '~/core/calendar-engine';
import { getTodayIso, getYesterdayIso, toPersianDigits } from '~/domain/calendar';
import type { HabitTimeSlot, HabitTreeNode } from '~/domain/schemas';
import JalaliDateScrubber from '~/components/tracker/JalaliDateScrubber.vue';
import UpcomingEventsBanner from '~/components/tracker/UpcomingEventsBanner.vue';
import TrackerActionToolbar from '~/components/tracker/TrackerActionToolbar.vue';
import CadenceHabitCard from '~/components/tracker/CadenceHabitCard.vue';
import TieredHabitCard from '~/components/tracker/TieredHabitCard.vue';
import DailyJournalSection from '~/components/tracker/DailyJournalSection.vue';
import { Moon, Sun, Sunrise } from 'lucide-vue-next';

const route = useRoute();
const router = useRouter();
const localRepo = useLocalRepository();

async function handleToggleCalendarEvent(eventId: string, isCompleted: boolean) {
	await localRepo.calendar.toggleComplete(eventId, isCompleted);
}

const currentDateIso = computed(() => {
	const param = route.query.date as string | undefined;
	if (param && /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(param)) {
		return param;
	}
	return getYesterdayIso();
});

const tracker = useDailyTracker(currentDateIso);
const isHabitsLoading = tracker.habits.isLoading;

function navigateToDate(iso: string) {
	router.push({ query: { ...route.query, date: iso } });
}

const bannerCalculation = computed(() =>
	computeUpcomingEventsBanner({
		allEvents: tracker.calendarEvents.value,
		referenceDateIso: currentDateIso.value,
		todayIso: getTodayIso(),
		horizonDays: 7
	})
);

const activeSelectedDayEvents = computed(() => bannerCalculation.value.selectedDayEvents);
const upcomingBannerItems = computed(() => bannerCalculation.value.todayAndUpcoming);

const timeSlotOrder: ReadonlyArray<{
	slot: HabitTimeSlot;
	icon: typeof Sunrise;
}> = [
	{ slot: 'morning', icon: Sunrise },
	{ slot: 'day', icon: Sun },
	{ slot: 'night', icon: Moon }
];

const groupedTieredSections = computed(() => {
	const groups: Array<{
		slot: HabitTimeSlot;
		icon: typeof Sunrise;
		nodes: HabitTreeNode[];
		completedCount: number;
	}> = [];

	for (const meta of timeSlotOrder) {
		const nodes = tracker.tree.value.tieredRoots.filter((n) => n.timeSlot === meta.slot);
		if (nodes.length > 0) {
			const completedCount = nodes.filter((n) => tracker.isNodeCompleted(n.id)).length;
			groups.push({
				slot: meta.slot,
				icon: meta.icon,
				nodes,
				completedCount
			});
		}
	}
	return groups;
});
</script>

<template>
	<div class="space-y-6 pb-12">
		<JalaliDateScrubber
			:current-date-iso="currentDateIso"
			:recent-entries="tracker.allEntries.value"
			:live-day-status="tracker.computedDayStatus.value"
			:is-live-dirty="tracker.isDirty.value"
			:has-existing-entry="Boolean(tracker.entry.value)"
			:save-state="tracker.saveState.value"
			:completion-stats="tracker.completionStats.value"
			@select-date="navigateToDate"
			@retry-save="tracker.flushPendingSave"
		/>

		<UpcomingEventsBanner
			:upcoming-items="upcomingBannerItems"
			:selected-day-events="activeSelectedDayEvents"
			:current-date-iso="currentDateIso"
			@toggle-complete="handleToggleCalendarEvent"
			@open-calendar-date="(iso) => router.push({ path: '/calendar', query: { date: iso } })"
		/>

		<div v-if="isHabitsLoading" class="space-y-4" aria-busy="true">
			<div class="h-18 animate-pulse rounded-3xl border border-border bg-surface"></div>
			<div v-for="idx in 3" :key="idx" class="h-24 animate-pulse rounded-3xl border border-border bg-surface"></div>
		</div>

		<div v-else-if="tracker.habits.value.length === 0" class="rounded-3xl border border-border bg-surface p-10 text-center">
			<h2 class="text-xl font-black text-fg">پایگاه داده خالی است</h2>
			<p class="mt-2 text-sm text-fg-secondary">
				طرحی برای ردیابی ثبت نشده است. می‌توانید از بخش «طرح‌ها» اولین طرح خود را ایجاد کنید یا از دیتابیس پشتیبان بگیرید.
			</p>
			<NuxtLink
				to="/architect"
				class="mt-4 inline-flex h-11 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl bg-accent px-5 text-xs font-black text-accent-fg shadow-xs"
			>
				تعریف اولین طرح در معمار طرح‌ها
			</NuxtLink>
		</div>

		<template v-else>
			<TrackerActionToolbar
				:cadence-habits="tracker.tree.value.cadenceHabits"
				:tomorrow-iso="tracker.tomorrowIso.value"
				:available-tags="tracker.availableTags.value"
				:selected-tags="tracker.selectedTags.value"
				:reflection-note="tracker.reflectionNote.value"
				:tomorrow-plans="tracker.tomorrowPlansByHabitId.value"
				@mark-all-optimal="tracker.markAllOptimalFiveSecond"
				@clear-day="tracker.clearDay"
				@toggle-tag="tracker.toggleTag"
				@update-reflection-note="(val) => { tracker.reflectionNote.value = val; tracker.scheduleAutoSave(750); }"
				@update-tomorrow-plan="(id, mins) => tracker.setTomorrowPlanMinutes(id, mins)"
				@copy-today-to-tomorrow="tracker.copyTodayActualsToTomorrowPlan"
				@save-tomorrow-plans="tracker.saveTomorrowCadencePlans"
			/>

			<!-- بخش عادات ریتمی تکرارشونده -->
			<div v-if="tracker.tree.value.cadenceHabits.length > 0" class="space-y-4">
				<CadenceHabitCard
					v-for="habit in tracker.tree.value.cadenceHabits"
					:key="habit.id"
					:habit="habit"
					:log="tracker.logsByHabitId.value[habit.id]"
					@update-actual="(id, mins) => tracker.updateCadenceActual(id, mins)"
				/>
			</div>

			<!-- بخش عادات رتبه‌بندی شده تفکیک نوبت زمانی -->
			<div class="space-y-5">
				<div v-for="section in groupedTieredSections" :key="section.slot" class="space-y-2.5">
					<div v-if="groupedTieredSections.length > 1" class="flex items-center gap-2.5 px-2">
						<div class="flex h-7 w-7 items-center justify-center rounded-xl border border-border bg-elevated text-fg-secondary">
							<component :is="section.icon" class="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
						</div>
						<div class="h-px flex-1 bg-border"></div>
						<span class="font-mono text-[11px] font-extrabold text-fg-muted">
							{{ toPersianDigits(section.completedCount) }}/{{ toPersianDigits(section.nodes.length) }}
						</span>
					</div>

					<div class="space-y-2.5">
						<TieredHabitCard
							v-for="node in section.nodes"
							:key="node.id"
							:node="node"
							:logs-by-habit-id="tracker.logsByHabitId.value"
							:composite-notation="node.children.length > 0 ? tracker.getCompositeNotation(node.id) : undefined"
							@select-tier="(id, tier) => tracker.setTierScore(id, tier)"
							@select-group-tier="(parentId, tier) => tracker.setParentGroupAllTier(parentId, tier)"
						/>
					</div>
				</div>
			</div>

			<!-- بخش خاطره‌نگاری و روایت روزانه -->
			<DailyJournalSection
				v-model="tracker.journalText.value"
				@change="tracker.scheduleAutoSave(750)"
				@open-memories="router.push('/memories')"
			/>
		</template>
	</div>
</template>

PATH: app/pages/notes/index.vue
