<script setup lang="ts">
import { ref, computed } from 'vue';
import { useAppDatabase } from '~/composables/useAppDatabase';
import { useDexieLiveQuery } from '~/composables/useDexieLiveQuery';
import { useLocalRepository } from '~/composables/useLocalRepository';
import { detectNoteSmartSignals } from '~/core/note-signals';
import { toPersianDigits } from '~/domain/calendar';
import type { NoteEntity, NoteSmartFilter, UpsertNoteInput } from '~/domain/schemas';
import NoteItemCard from '~/components/notes/NoteItemCard.vue';
import NoteUpsertModal from '~/components/notes/NoteUpsertModal.vue';
import { Hash, Link2, NotebookPen, Plus, Search } from 'lucide-vue-next';

const db = useAppDatabase();
const localRepo = useLocalRepository();

const rawNotes = useDexieLiveQuery(() => db.notes.orderBy('updatedAtIso').reverse().toArray(), []);
const isNotesLoading = rawNotes.isLoading;

const notes = computed(() =>
	[...rawNotes.value].sort((a, b) => (b.isPinned ? 1 : 0) - (a.isPinned ? 1 : 0))
);

const searchQuery = ref('');
const smartFilter = ref<NoteSmartFilter>('ALL');
const selectedCategory = ref<string>('ALL');
const isModalOpen = ref(false);
const isSubmittingNote = ref(false);
const editingNote = ref<NoteEntity | null>(null);

const categories = computed(() => {
	const set = new Set<string>(['عمومی']);
	for (const n of notes.value) {
		if (n.category.trim()) set.add(n.category.trim());
	}
	return Array.from(set);
});

const filteredNotes = computed(() => {
	const q = searchQuery.value.trim().toLowerCase();

	return notes.value.filter((note) => {
		if (selectedCategory.value !== 'ALL' && note.category !== selectedCategory.value) {
			return false;
		}

		const combinedText = `${note.title} ${note.content}`;
		const signals = detectNoteSmartSignals(combinedText);

		if (smartFilter.value === 'HAS_LINK' && !signals.hasLink) return false;
		if (smartFilter.value === 'HAS_NUMBER' && !signals.hasNumber) return false;

		if (q) {
			const haystack = `${note.title} ${note.content} ${note.category}`.toLowerCase();
			if (!haystack.includes(q)) return false;
		}

		return true;
	});
});

function openCreateModal() {
	editingNote.value = null;
	isModalOpen.value = true;
}

function openEditModal(note: NoteEntity) {
	editingNote.value = note;
	isModalOpen.value = true;
}

function closeModal() {
	isModalOpen.value = false;
	editingNote.value = null;
}

async function handleSaveNote(payload: UpsertNoteInput) {
	if (isSubmittingNote.value) return;
	isSubmittingNote.value = true;
	try {
		if (payload.noteId) {
			await localRepo.notes.update(payload.noteId, payload);
		} else {
			await localRepo.notes.create(payload);
		}
		closeModal();
	} finally {
		isSubmittingNote.value = false;
	}
}

async function handleTogglePin(noteId: string, isPinned: boolean) {
	await localRepo.notes.togglePin(noteId, isPinned);
}

async function handleDeleteNote(noteId: string) {
	await localRepo.notes.delete(noteId);
}
</script>

<template>
	<div class="space-y-6 pb-16">
		<section class="rounded-3xl border border-border bg-surface p-5 shadow-(--shadow-soft) sm:p-6">
			<div class="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-4">
				<div class="flex items-center gap-3.5">
					<div
						class="flex h-12 w-12 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl border border-accent-border bg-accent-soft text-accent-text"
					>
						<NotebookPen class="h-6 w-6" />
					</div>
					<div>
						<h1 class="text-xl font-black tracking-tight text-fg sm:text-2xl">یادداشت‌ها</h1>
						<span class="text-xs font-bold text-fg-secondary">
							{{ toPersianDigits(notes.length) }} یادداشت، لینک و شماره ذخیره‌شده
						</span>
					</div>
				</div>

				<div class="flex w-full flex-wrap items-center justify-end gap-2.5 sm:w-auto">
					<div v-if="notes.length > 0" class="relative flex-1 sm:w-60 sm:flex-initial">
						<Search class="pointer-events-none absolute top-1/2 right-3.5 h-4 w-4 -translate-y-1/2 text-fg-muted" />
						<input
							v-model="searchQuery"
							type="search"
							placeholder="جستجو در یادداشت‌ها..."
							class="h-11 w-full rounded-2xl border border-border bg-elevated pr-9 pl-3.5 text-xs font-bold text-fg placeholder:font-normal placeholder:text-fg-muted focus:border-border-strong focus:outline-none"
						/>
					</div>

					<button
						type="button"
						class="flex h-11 items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-2xl bg-accent px-5 text-xs font-extrabold text-accent-fg shadow-xs transition hover:opacity-90 active:scale-98 sm:text-sm"
						@click="openCreateModal"
					>
						<Plus class="h-4 w-4" />
						<span>یادداشت جدید</span>
					</button>
				</div>
			</div>

			<div v-if="notes.length > 0" class="mt-4 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 border-t border-border pt-4">
				<div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5">
					<button
						type="button"
						class="rounded-xl px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-extrabold transition"
						:class="
							smartFilter === 'ALL'
								? 'border border-accent-border bg-accent-soft text-accent-text shadow-xs'
								: 'bg-elevated text-fg-secondary hover:text-fg'
						"
						@click="smartFilter = 'ALL'"
					>
						همه
					</button>
					<button
						type="button"
						class="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-extrabold transition"
						:class="
							smartFilter === 'HAS_LINK'
								? 'border border-accent-border bg-accent-soft text-accent-text shadow-xs'
								: 'bg-elevated text-fg-secondary hover:text-fg'
						"
						@click="smartFilter = smartFilter === 'HAS_LINK' ? 'ALL' : 'HAS_LINK'"
					>
						<Link2 class="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
						<span>حاوی لینک</span>
					</button>
					<button
						type="button"
						class="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-extrabold transition"
						:class="
							smartFilter === 'HAS_NUMBER'
								? 'border border-accent-border bg-accent-soft text-accent-text shadow-xs'
								: 'bg-elevated text-fg-secondary hover:text-fg'
						"
						@click="smartFilter = smartFilter === 'HAS_NUMBER' ? 'ALL' : 'HAS_NUMBER'"
					>
						<Hash class="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
						<span>حاوی شماره</span>
					</button>
				</div>

				<div v-if="categories.length > 1" class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5">
					<button
						type="button"
						class="rounded-xl px-2.5 py-1 text-[11px] font-bold transition"
						:class="
							selectedCategory === 'ALL'
								? 'bg-accent text-accent-fg shadow-xs'
								: 'bg-elevated text-fg-secondary hover:text-fg'
						"
						@click="selectedCategory = 'ALL'"
					>
						همه دسته‌ها
					</button>
					<button
						v-for="cat in categories"
						:key="cat"
						type="button"
						class="rounded-xl px-2.5 py-1 text-[11px] font-bold transition"
						:class="
							selectedCategory === cat
								? 'bg-accent text-accent-fg shadow-xs'
								: 'bg-elevated text-fg-secondary hover:text-fg'
						"
						@click="selectedCategory = cat"
					>
						{{ cat }}
					</button>
				</div>
			</div>
		</section>

		<div v-if="isNotesLoading" class="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3" aria-busy="true">
			<div v-for="idx in 6" :key="idx" class="h-44 animate-pulse rounded-3xl border border-border bg-surface"></div>
		</div>

		<div
			v-else-if="notes.length === 0"
			class="flex min-h-80 flex-col items-center justify-center rounded-3xl border border-dashed border-border bg-surface p-12 text-center"
		>
			<div class="mb-4 flex h-16 w-16 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl bg-elevated text-fg-muted">
				<NotebookPen class="h-8 w-8" />
			</div>
			<h2 class="text-base font-extrabold text-fg">هنوز یادداشتی ثبت نشده است</h2>
			<p class="mt-1.5 max-w-md text-xs leading-6 text-fg-muted">
				سایت‌های کاربردی، شماره‌های مهم یا یادداشت‌های روزمره را اینجا ذخیره کنید؛ سیستم به صورت خودکار لینک‌ها و شماره‌های ۱۰ رقمی را شناسایی می‌کند.
			</p>
			<button
				type="button"
				class="mt-5 inline-flex h-11 items-center gap-2 rounded-2xl bg-accent px-5 text-xs font-extrabold text-accent-fg transition hover:opacity-90 shadow-xs"
				@click="openCreateModal"
			>
				<Plus class="h-4 w-4" />
				<span>ثبت اولین یادداشت</span>
			</button>
		</div>

		<div
			v-else-if="filteredNotes.length === 0"
			class="rounded-3xl border border-border bg-surface p-10 text-center text-sm font-semibold text-fg-muted"
		>
			موردی با این فیلتر یا عبارت جستجو یافت نشد.
		</div>

		<div v-else class="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3">
			<NoteItemCard
				v-for="note in filteredNotes"
				:key="note.id"
				:note="note"
				@edit="openEditModal"
				@toggle-pin="handleTogglePin"
				@delete="handleDeleteNote"
			/>
		</div>

		<NoteUpsertModal
			v-if="isModalOpen"
			:key="editingNote?.id ?? 'new'"
			:editing-note="editingNote"
			:existing-categories="categories"
			:is-submitting="isSubmittingNote"
			@submit="handleSaveNote"
			@close="closeModal"
		/>
	</div>
</template>

PATH: app/pages/architect/index.vue

<script setup lang="ts">
import { ref, computed } from 'vue';
import { useRouter } from 'vue-router';
import { useAppDatabase } from '~/composables/useAppDatabase';
import { useDexieLiveQuery } from '~/composables/useDexieLiveQuery';
import { useLocalRepository } from '~/composables/useLocalRepository';
import type { CreateHabitInput, HabitEntity, MutateHabitStructureInput } from '~/domain/schemas';
import ArchitectHabitCard from '~/components/architect/ArchitectHabitCard.vue';
import ArchivedHabitsSection from '~/components/architect/ArchivedHabitsSection.vue';
import HabitUpsertModal from '~/components/architect/HabitUpsertModal.vue';
import { BarChart3, GitCommitHorizontal, Plus } from 'lucide-vue-next';

const router = useRouter();
const db = useAppDatabase();
const localRepo = useLocalRepository();
const habits = useDexieLiveQuery(() => db.habits.orderBy('sortOrder').toArray(), []);
const isHabitsLoading = habits.isLoading;

const activeModal = ref<'create' | 'edit' | null>(null);
const isSubmittingHabit = ref(false);
const editingHabit = ref<HabitEntity | null>(null);
const createParentId = ref('');

const rootHabits = computed(() => habits.value.filter((h) => h.parentId === null && h.status !== 'archived'));
const archivedHabits = computed(() => habits.value.filter((h) => h.status === 'archived'));

function getChildren(parentId: string): HabitEntity[] {
	return habits.value.filter((h) => h.parentId === parentId && h.status !== 'archived');
}

function openCreateModal(parentId = '') {
	createParentId.value = parentId;
	editingHabit.value = null;
	activeModal.value = 'create';
}

function openEditModal(habit: HabitEntity) {
	editingHabit.value = habit;
	activeModal.value = 'edit';
}

function closeModal() {
	activeModal.value = null;
	editingHabit.value = null;
	createParentId.value = '';
}

async function handleCreateHabit(payload: CreateHabitInput) {
	if (isSubmittingHabit.value) return;
	isSubmittingHabit.value = true;
	try {
		await localRepo.habits.create(payload);
		closeModal();
	} finally {
		isSubmittingHabit.value = false;
	}
}

async function handleUpdateHabit(payload: MutateHabitStructureInput) {
	if (isSubmittingHabit.value) return;
	isSubmittingHabit.value = true;
	try {
		await localRepo.habits.update(payload);
		closeModal();
	} finally {
		isSubmittingHabit.value = false;
	}
}
</script>

<template>
	<div class="space-y-8 pb-16">
		<div class="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-4">
			<h1 class="text-2xl font-black text-fg sm:text-3xl">معمار ساختار طرح‌ها</h1>

			<div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2.5">
				<NuxtLink
					to="/timeline"
					class="flex h-11 items-center gap-1.5 rounded-2xl border border-border bg-elevated px-4 text-xs font-extrabold text-fg-secondary transition hover:border-border-strong hover:text-fg sm:h-12"
				>
					<GitCommitHorizontal class="h-4 w-4" />
					<span>تاریخچه وقایع</span>
				</NuxtLink>

				<NuxtLink
					to="/analytics"
					class="flex h-11 items-center gap-2 rounded-2xl border border-accent-border bg-accent-soft px-4.5 text-xs font-extrabold text-accent-text shadow-xs transition hover:opacity-90 active:scale-98 sm:h-12 sm:text-sm"
				>
					<BarChart3 class="h-4.5 w-4.5" />
					<span>مشاهده آمار و روندها</span>
				</NuxtLink>

				<button
					type="button"
					class="flex h-11 items-center gap-2 rounded-2xl bg-accent px-5 text-xs font-extrabold text-accent-fg shadow-(--shadow-soft) transition hover:opacity-90 active:scale-98 sm:h-12 sm:text-sm"
					@click="openCreateModal('')"
				>
					<Plus class="h-4.5 w-4.5" />
					<span>افزودن طرح جدید</span>
				</button>
			</div>
		</div>

		<div v-if="isHabitsLoading" class="grid grid-cols-1 gap-5 md:grid-cols-2" aria-busy="true">
			<div v-for="idx in 4" :key="idx" class="h-48 animate-pulse rounded-3xl border border-border bg-surface"></div>
		</div>

		<!-- گرید کارت‌های طرح‌های اصلی -->
		<div v-else class="grid grid-cols-1 gap-5 md:grid-cols-2">
			<ArchitectHabitCard
				v-for="habit in rootHabits"
				:key="habit.id"
				:habit="habit"
				:children="getChildren(habit.id)"
				@create-sub-habit="openCreateModal"
				@edit-habit="openEditModal"
				@open-stats="(habitId) => router.push({ path: '/analytics', query: { habit: habitId } })"
			/>
		</div>

		<!-- بخش کشویی طرح‌های بایگانی‌شده -->
		<ArchivedHabitsSection :archived-habits="archivedHabits" @edit-habit="openEditModal" />

		<!-- مودال درج و ویرایش طرح -->
		<HabitUpsertModal
			v-if="activeModal"
			:key="`${activeModal}:${editingHabit?.id ?? createParentId}`"
			:mode="activeModal"
			:editing-habit="editingHabit"
			:has-sub-habits="editingHabit ? getChildren(editingHabit.id).length > 0 : false"
			:initial-parent-id="createParentId"
			:root-habits="rootHabits"
			:is-submitting="isSubmittingHabit"
			@create="handleCreateHabit"
			@update="handleUpdateHabit"
			@close="closeModal"
		/>
	</div>
</template>

================================================================
PATH: app/pages/media/index.vue
================================================================

<script setup lang="ts">
import { ref, computed } from 'vue';
import { useAppDatabase } from '~/composables/useAppDatabase';
import { useDexieLiveQuery } from '~/composables/useDexieLiveQuery';
import { useLocalRepository } from '~/composables/useLocalRepository';
import type { CreateMediaInput, MediaCategory, MediaEntity, MediaSortBy, MediaStatusFilter, UpdateMediaInput } from '~/domain/schemas';
import MediaHeaderStats from '~/components/media/MediaHeaderStats.vue';
import MediaItemCard from '~/components/media/MediaItemCard.vue';
import MediaUpsertModal from '~/components/media/MediaUpsertModal.vue';
import { Clapperboard, Plus } from 'lucide-vue-next';

const db = useAppDatabase();
const localRepo = useLocalRepository();

const mediaItems = useDexieLiveQuery(() => db.media.orderBy('createdAtIso').reverse().toArray(), []);
const isMediaLoading = mediaItems.isLoading;

const categoryFilter = ref<MediaCategory | 'ALL'>('ALL');
const statusFilter = ref<MediaStatusFilter>('ALL');
const sortBy = ref<MediaSortBy>('rating_desc');
const isModalOpen = ref(false);
const isSubmittingMedia = ref(false);
const editingMedia = ref<MediaEntity | null>(null);

const unseenCount = computed(() => mediaItems.value.filter((i) => i.status !== 'completed').length);

const existingSuggestions = computed(() => {
	const map = new Map<string, MediaCategory>();
	for (const m of mediaItems.value) {
		if (!map.has(m.title)) {
			map.set(m.title, m.category);
		}
	}
	return Array.from(map.entries()).map(([title, category]) => ({ title, category }));
});

const filteredItems = computed(() => {
	const list = mediaItems.value.filter((item) => {
		if (categoryFilter.value !== 'ALL' && item.category !== categoryFilter.value) return false;
		if (statusFilter.value === 'UNSEEN' && item.status === 'completed') return false;
		if (statusFilter.value === 'SEEN' && item.status !== 'completed') return false;
		return true;
	});

	return [...list].sort((a, b) => {
		if (sortBy.value === 'rating_desc') {
			const rDiff = (b.rating ?? 0) - (a.rating ?? 0);
			if (rDiff !== 0) return rDiff;
			return b.createdAtIso > a.createdAtIso ? 1 : b.createdAtIso < a.createdAtIso ? -1 : 0;
		}
		if (sortBy.value === 'newest') {
			const dateA = a.finishedAtIso || a.createdAtIso;
			const dateB = b.finishedAtIso || b.createdAtIso;
			return dateB > dateA ? 1 : dateB < dateA ? -1 : 0;
		}
		if (sortBy.value === 'oldest') {
			const dateA = a.finishedAtIso || a.createdAtIso;
			const dateB = b.finishedAtIso || b.createdAtIso;
			return dateA > dateB ? 1 : dateA < dateB ? -1 : 0;
		}
		return a.title.localeCompare(b.title, 'fa');
	});
});

function openCreateModal() {
	editingMedia.value = null;
	isModalOpen.value = true;
}

function openEditModal(item: MediaEntity) {
	editingMedia.value = item;
	isModalOpen.value = true;
}

function closeModal() {
	isModalOpen.value = false;
	editingMedia.value = null;
}

async function handleSaveMedia(payload: CreateMediaInput | UpdateMediaInput) {
	if (isSubmittingMedia.value) return;
	isSubmittingMedia.value = true;
	try {
		if ('mediaId' in payload) {
			await localRepo.media.update(payload);
		} else {
			await localRepo.media.create(payload);
		}
		closeModal();
	} finally {
		isSubmittingMedia.value = false;
	}
}

async function handleUpdateStatus(mediaId: string, status: 'completed') {
	await localRepo.media.updateStatus(mediaId, status);
}

async function handleDeleteMedia(mediaId: string) {
	await localRepo.media.delete(mediaId);
}
</script>

<template>
	<div class="space-y-6 pb-16">
		<MediaHeaderStats
			:total-count="mediaItems.length"
			:unseen-count="unseenCount"
			:category-filter="categoryFilter"
			:status-filter="statusFilter"
			:sort-by="sortBy"
			@update:category-filter="categoryFilter = $event"
			@update:status-filter="statusFilter = $event"
			@update:sort-by="sortBy = $event"
			@open-create="openCreateModal"
		/>

		<div v-if="isMediaLoading" class="space-y-2.5" aria-busy="true">
			<div v-for="idx in 4" :key="idx" class="h-20 animate-pulse rounded-2xl border border-border bg-surface"></div>
		</div>

		<div
			v-else-if="mediaItems.length === 0"
			class="flex min-h-80 flex-col items-center justify-center rounded-3xl border border-dashed border-border bg-surface p-12 text-center"
		>
			<div class="mb-4 flex h-16 w-16 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl bg-elevated text-fg-muted">
				<Clapperboard class="h-8 w-8" />
			</div>
			<h3 class="text-base font-extrabold text-fg">هنوز فیلم، سریال یا انیمه‌ای ثبت نشده است</h3>
			<p class="mt-1.5 text-sm text-fg-muted">برای شروع، اولین اثر را وارد آرشیو خود کنید.</p>
			<button
				type="button"
				class="mt-5 inline-flex h-11 items-center gap-2 rounded-2xl bg-accent px-5 text-xs font-extrabold text-accent-fg transition hover:opacity-90 shadow-xs"
				@click="openCreateModal"
			>
				<Plus class="h-4 w-4" />
				<span>ثبت اولین اثر</span>
			</button>
		</div>

		<div v-else-if="filteredItems.length === 0" class="rounded-3xl border border-border bg-surface p-10 text-center text-sm font-semibold text-fg-muted">
			موردی با این فیلتر یافت نشد.
		</div>

		<div v-else class="space-y-2.5">
			<MediaItemCard
				v-for="item in filteredItems"
				:key="item.id"
				:item="item"
				@edit="openEditModal"
				@update-status="handleUpdateStatus"
				@delete="handleDeleteMedia"
			/>
		</div>

		<MediaUpsertModal
			v-if="isModalOpen"
			:key="editingMedia?.id ?? 'new'"
			:editing-media="editingMedia"
			:existing-suggestions="existingSuggestions"
			:is-submitting="isSubmittingMedia"
			@submit="handleSaveMedia"
			@close="closeModal"
		/>
	</div>
</template>

================================================================
PATH: app/pages/calendar/index.vue

<script setup lang="ts">
import { ref, computed } from 'vue';
import { useRoute, useRouter } from 'vue-router';
import { useAppDatabase } from '~/composables/useAppDatabase';
import { useDexieLiveQuery } from '~/composables/useDexieLiveQuery';
import { useLocalRepository } from '~/composables/useLocalRepository';
import { assembleJalaliMonthArchive, computeUpcomingEventsBanner } from '~/core/calendar-engine';
import {
	JALALI_MONTH_NAMES,
	getJalaliMonthLength,
	getTodayIso,
	isoToJalaliParts,
	jalaliPartsToIso,
	toPersianDigits
} from '~/domain/calendar';
import type { CreateCalendarEventInput } from '~/domain/schemas';
import CalendarMonthGrid from '~/components/calendar/CalendarMonthGrid.vue';
import CalendarDayInspector from '~/components/calendar/CalendarDayInspector.vue';
import CustomSelect from '~/components/ui/CustomSelect.vue';
import { BookHeart, Calendar as CalendarIcon, ChevronLeft, ChevronRight } from 'lucide-vue-next';

const route = useRoute();
const router = useRouter();
const db = useAppDatabase();
const localRepo = useLocalRepository();
const isSubmittingCalendarEvent = ref(false);

async function handleCreateEvent(payload: CreateCalendarEventInput) {
	if (isSubmittingCalendarEvent.value) return;
	isSubmittingCalendarEvent.value = true;
	try {
		await localRepo.calendar.create(payload);
	} finally {
		isSubmittingCalendarEvent.value = false;
	}
}

async function handleToggleEventComplete(eventId: string, isCompleted: boolean) {
	await localRepo.calendar.toggleComplete(eventId, isCompleted);
}

async function handleDeleteEvent(eventId: string) {
	await localRepo.calendar.delete(eventId);
}

const todayIso = computed(() => getTodayIso());
const todayParts = computed(() => isoToJalaliParts(todayIso.value));

const requestedDate = computed(() => {
	const d = route.query.date as string | undefined;
	return d && /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(d) ? d : null;
});

const currentYear = computed(() => {
	const jyParam = Number(route.query.jy);
	if (!Number.isNaN(jyParam) && jyParam >= 1300 && jyParam <= 1500) return jyParam;
	if (requestedDate.value) return isoToJalaliParts(requestedDate.value).jy;
	return todayParts.value.jy;
});

const currentMonth = computed(() => {
	const jmParam = Number(route.query.jm);
	if (!Number.isNaN(jmParam) && jmParam >= 1 && jmParam <= 12) return jmParam;
	if (requestedDate.value) return isoToJalaliParts(requestedDate.value).jm;
	return todayParts.value.jm;
});

const monthIsoBounds = computed(() => {
	const jy = currentYear.value;
	const jm = currentMonth.value;
	const len = getJalaliMonthLength(jy, jm);
	return {
		startIso: jalaliPartsToIso({ jy, jm, jd: 1 }) ?? todayIso.value,
		endIso: jalaliPartsToIso({ jy, jm, jd: len }) ?? todayIso.value
	};
});

// Live Dexie subscriptions scoped to the active Jalali month
const allHabits = useDexieLiveQuery(() => db.habits.orderBy('sortOrder').toArray(), []);
const monthEntries = useDexieLiveQuery(
	() =>
		db.dailyEntries
			.where('dateIso')
			.between(monthIsoBounds.value.startIso, monthIsoBounds.value.endIso, true, true)
			.toArray(),
	[],
	monthIsoBounds
);
const monthLogs = useDexieLiveQuery(
	() =>
		db.habitLogs
			.where('dateIso')
			.between(monthIsoBounds.value.startIso, monthIsoBounds.value.endIso, true, true)
			.toArray(),
	[],
	monthIsoBounds
);
const allEvents = useDexieLiveQuery(() => db.calendarEvents.toArray(), []);
const allBooks = useDexieLiveQuery(() => db.books.toArray(), []);
const allMedia = useDexieLiveQuery(() => db.media.toArray(), []);

const userSelectedIso = ref<string | null>(null);

const archiveData = computed(() => {
	const { todayAndUpcoming } = computeUpcomingEventsBanner({
		allEvents: allEvents.value,
		referenceDateIso: todayIso.value,
		todayIso: todayIso.value,
		horizonDays: 14
	});

	return assembleJalaliMonthArchive({
		jy: currentYear.value,
		jm: currentMonth.value,
		todayIso: todayIso.value,
		selectedIso: requestedDate.value ?? undefined,
		allHabits: allHabits.value,
		entriesInMonth: monthEntries.value,
		logsInMonth: monthLogs.value,
		eventsInMonth: allEvents.value,
		booksInMonth: allBooks.value,
		mediaInMonth: allMedia.value,
		upcomingEvents: todayAndUpcoming
	});
});

const selectedIso = computed(
	() => userSelectedIso.value || archiveData.value.selectedIso
);

const selectedDay = computed(
	() => archiveData.value.days.find((d) => d.iso === selectedIso.value) ?? archiveData.value.days[0]
);

const prevMonthTarget = computed(() =>
	currentMonth.value === 1
		? { jy: currentYear.value - 1, jm: 12 }
		: { jy: currentYear.value, jm: currentMonth.value - 1 }
);

const nextMonthTarget = computed(() =>
	currentMonth.value === 12
		? { jy: currentYear.value + 1, jm: 1 }
		: { jy: currentYear.value, jm: currentMonth.value + 1 }
);

const monthOptions = JALALI_MONTH_NAMES.map((name, idx) => ({
	value: idx + 1,
	label: name
}));

const yearOptions = computed(() => {
	const base = currentYear.value;
	const list = [];
	for (let y = base - 5; y <= base + 5; y++) {
		list.push({ value: y, label: toPersianDigits(y) });
	}
	return list;
});

function navigateToMonth(jy: number, jm: number) {
	userSelectedIso.value = null;
	router.push({ query: { jy, jm } });
}

function goToToday() {
	userSelectedIso.value = null;
	router.push({ query: { date: todayIso.value } });
}
</script>

<template>
	<div class="space-y-6 pb-12">
		<section class="rounded-3xl border border-border bg-surface p-4 shadow-(--shadow-soft) sm:p-6">
			<div class="flex flex-col justify-between gap-4 lg:flex-row lg:items-center">
				<div class="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 sm:justify-start sm:gap-3">
					<div
						class="flex h-11 w-11 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl border border-accent-border bg-accent-soft text-accent-text"
					>
						<CalendarIcon class="h-5 w-5" />
					</div>

					<h1 class="text-lg font-black tracking-tight text-fg sm:text-2xl">تقویم و رویدادها</h1>
				</div>

				<div class="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 sm:justify-end">
					<div class="flex items-center gap-1.5">
						<button
							type="button"
							class="flex h-11 w-11 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl border border-border bg-elevated text-fg transition hover:border-border-strong active:scale-95"
							aria-label="ماه قبل"
							title="ماه قبل"
							@click="navigateToMonth(prevMonthTarget.jy, prevMonthTarget.jm)"
						>
							<ChevronRight class="h-5 w-5" />
						</button>

						<div class="w-32 sm:w-36">
							<CustomSelect
								:model-value="currentMonth"
								:options="monthOptions"
								trigger-class="h-11 text-fg"
								@select="(val) => navigateToMonth(currentYear, Number(val))"
							/>
						</div>

						<div class="w-24 sm:w-28">
							<CustomSelect
								:model-value="currentYear"
								:options="yearOptions"
								trigger-class="h-11 font-mono text-fg"
								@select="(val) => navigateToMonth(Number(val), currentMonth)"
							/>
						</div>

						<button
							type="button"
							class="flex h-11 w-11 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl border border-border bg-elevated text-fg transition hover:border-border-strong active:scale-95"
							aria-label="ماه بعد"
							title="ماه بعد"
							@click="navigateToMonth(nextMonthTarget.jy, nextMonthTarget.jm)"
						>
							<ChevronLeft class="h-5 w-5" />
						</button>
					</div>

					<button
						type="button"
						class="h-11 rounded-2xl border border-accent-border bg-accent-soft px-4 text-xs font-extrabold text-accent-text transition hover:opacity-90"
						@click="goToToday"
					>
						امروز
					</button>
				</div>
			</div>
		</section>

		<div class="grid grid-cols-1 items-start gap-6 lg:grid-cols-12">
			<!-- گرید ماه جلالی -->
			<div class="space-y-6 lg:col-span-7">
				<CalendarMonthGrid
					:leading-empty-cells="archiveData.leadingEmptyCells"
					:days="archiveData.days"
					:selected-iso="selectedIso"
					@select-day="(iso) => (userSelectedIso = iso)"
				/>

				<!-- کارت خاطره و یادداشت روز انتخابی -->
				<section
					v-if="selectedDay?.entry && (selectedDay.entry.journalText || selectedDay.entry.reflectionNote || selectedDay.entry.tags.length > 0)"
					class="space-y-3.5 rounded-3xl border border-border bg-surface p-5 shadow-(--shadow-soft) sm:p-6"
				>
					<div class="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
						<h3 class="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-black text-fg">
							<BookHeart class="h-4 w-4 text-accent-text" />
							<span>خاطره و یادداشت {{ selectedDay.fullHumanLabel }}</span>
						</h3>
					</div>

					<p
						v-if="selectedDay.entry.journalText"
						class="whitespace-pre-line rounded-2xl border border-border bg-elevated p-4 text-sm leading-8 text-fg"
					>
						{{ selectedDay.entry.journalText }}
					</p>

					<p v-if="selectedDay.entry.reflectionNote" class="rounded-2xl border border-border bg-elevated px-4 py-3 text-xs leading-6 text-fg-secondary">
						<strong class="font-extrabold text-fg">علت / یادداشت روز: </strong>
						{{ selectedDay.entry.reflectionNote }}
					</p>

					<div v-if="selectedDay.entry.tags.length > 0" class="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5 pt-0.5">
						<span
							v-for="tag in selectedDay.entry.tags"
							:key="tag"
							class="rounded-xl border border-border bg-elevated px-2.5 py-1 text-[11px] font-bold text-fg-secondary"
						>
							#{{ tag }}
						</span>
					</div>
				</section>
			</div>

			<!-- بازرس روز انتخابی -->
			<div class="lg:col-span-5">
				<CalendarDayInspector
					v-if="selectedDay"
					:day="selectedDay"
					:is-submitting-event="isSubmittingCalendarEvent"
					@create-event="handleCreateEvent"
					@toggle-event-complete="handleToggleEventComplete"
					@delete-event="handleDeleteEvent"
					@open-tracker-date="(iso) => router.push({ path: '/', query: { date: iso } })"
				/>
			</div>
		</div>
	</div>
</template>

================================================================
PATH: app/pages/books/index.vue

<script setup lang="ts">
import { ref, computed } from 'vue';
import { useAppDatabase } from '~/composables/useAppDatabase';
import { useDexieLiveQuery } from '~/composables/useDexieLiveQuery';
import { useLocalRepository } from '~/composables/useLocalRepository';
import type { BookEntity, CreateBookInput, UpdateBookInput } from '~/domain/schemas';
import BooksHeaderStats from '~/components/books/BooksHeaderStats.vue';
import BookItemCard from '~/components/books/BookItemCard.vue';
import BookUpsertModal from '~/components/books/BookUpsertModal.vue';
import { BookOpen, Plus } from 'lucide-vue-next';

const db = useAppDatabase();
const localRepo = useLocalRepository();

const books = useDexieLiveQuery(() => db.books.orderBy('createdAtIso').reverse().toArray(), []);
const isBooksLoading = books.isLoading;

const isModalOpen = ref(false);
const isSubmittingBook = ref(false);
const editingBook = ref<BookEntity | null>(null);

const completedCount = computed(() => books.value.filter((b) => b.status === 'completed').length);
const readingCount = computed(() => books.value.filter((b) => b.status === 'reading').length);

const existingSuggestions = computed(() => {
	const map = new Map<string, string | null>();
	for (const b of books.value) {
		if (!map.has(b.title)) {
			map.set(b.title, b.author);
		}
	}
	return Array.from(map.entries()).map(([title, author]) => ({ title, author }));
});

function openCreateModal() {
	editingBook.value = null;
	isModalOpen.value = true;
}

function openEditModal(book: BookEntity) {
	editingBook.value = book;
	isModalOpen.value = true;
}

function closeModal() {
	isModalOpen.value = false;
	editingBook.value = null;
}

async function handleSaveBook(payload: CreateBookInput | UpdateBookInput) {
	if (isSubmittingBook.value) return;
	isSubmittingBook.value = true;
	try {
		if ('bookId' in payload) {
			await localRepo.books.update(payload);
		} else {
			await localRepo.books.create(payload);
		}
		closeModal();
	} finally {
		isSubmittingBook.value = false;
	}
}

async function handleUpdateStatus(bookId: string, status: 'completed') {
	await localRepo.books.updateStatus(bookId, status);
}

async function handleDeleteBook(bookId: string) {
	await localRepo.books.delete(bookId);
}
</script>

<template>
	<div class="space-y-8 pb-16">
		<BooksHeaderStats
			:completed-count="completedCount"
			:reading-count="readingCount"
			@open-create="openCreateModal"
		/>

		<div v-if="isBooksLoading" class="space-y-2.5" aria-busy="true">
			<div v-for="idx in 4" :key="idx" class="h-20 animate-pulse rounded-2xl border border-border bg-surface"></div>
		</div>

		<div
			v-else-if="books.length === 0"
			class="flex min-h-80 flex-col items-center justify-center rounded-3xl border border-dashed border-border bg-surface p-12 text-center"
		>
			<div class="mb-4 flex h-16 w-16 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl bg-elevated text-fg-muted">
				<BookOpen class="h-8 w-8" />
			</div>
			<h3 class="text-base font-extrabold text-fg">هنوز کتابی ثبت نشده است</h3>
			<p class="mt-1.5 text-sm text-fg-muted">اولین کتاب خود را در کتابخانه شخصی ثبت کنید.</p>
			<button
				type="button"
				class="mt-5 inline-flex h-11 items-center gap-2 rounded-2xl bg-accent px-5 text-xs font-extrabold text-accent-fg transition hover:opacity-90 shadow-xs"
				@click="openCreateModal"
			>
				<Plus class="h-4 w-4" />
				<span>ثبت اولین کتاب</span>
			</button>
		</div>

		<div v-else class="space-y-2.5">
			<BookItemCard
				v-for="book in books"
				:key="book.id"
				:book="book"
				@edit="openEditModal"
				@update-status="handleUpdateStatus"
				@delete="handleDeleteBook"
			/>
		</div>

		<BookUpsertModal
			v-if="isModalOpen"
			:key="editingBook?.id ?? 'new'"
			:editing-book="editingBook"
			:existing-suggestions="existingSuggestions"
			:is-submitting="isSubmittingBook"
			@submit="handleSaveBook"
			@close="closeModal"
		/>
	</div>
</template>

================================================================
PATH: app/pages/settings/index.vue

<script setup lang="ts">
import { ref, computed } from 'vue';
import { useRouter } from 'vue-router';
import { useAppDatabase } from '~/composables/useAppDatabase';
import { useDexieLiveQuery } from '~/composables/useDexieLiveQuery';
import { useLocalRepository } from '~/composables/useLocalRepository';
import { useSyncEngine } from '~/composables/useSyncEngine';
import { useTheme } from '~/composables/useTheme';
import { useClientAuth } from '~/composables/useClientAuth';
import { toPersianDigits } from '~/domain/calendar';
import { isDemoRecordId } from '~/domain/demo-seed';
import { DatabaseBackupPayloadSchema } from '~/domain/schemas';
import {
	AlertTriangle,
	Check,
	Database,
	Download,
	FlaskConical,
	HardDriveDownload,
	HardDriveUpload,
	KeyRound,
	LogOut,
	Moon,
	Palette,
	Plus,
	Settings,
	ShieldCheck,
	Sun,
	Trash2
} from 'lucide-vue-next';

const router = useRouter();
const db = useAppDatabase();
const localRepo = useLocalRepository();
const syncEngine = useSyncEngine();
const theme = useTheme();
const clientAuth = useClientAuth();

const habits = useDexieLiveQuery(() => db.habits.toArray(), []);
const entries = useDexieLiveQuery(() => db.dailyEntries.toArray(), []);

const selectedFile = ref<File | null>(null);
const newPassword = ref('');
const confirmNewPassword = ref('');
const passwordMessage = ref<{ type: 'success' | 'error'; text: string } | null>(null);
const importMessage = ref<{ type: 'success' | 'error'; text: string } | null>(null);
const demoSeedMessage = ref<{ type: 'success' | 'error'; text: string } | null>(null);
const isExporting = ref(false);
const isImporting = ref(false);
const isMutatingDemoSeed = ref(false);
const isChangingPassword = ref(false);
const isConfirmingClearAll = ref(false);
const isClearingAll = ref(false);

const activeDemoHabitsCount = computed(
	() => habits.value.filter((h) => isDemoRecordId(h.id)).length
);

async function handleInjectDemoSeed() {
	if (isMutatingDemoSeed.value) return;
	isMutatingDemoSeed.value = true;
	demoSeedMessage.value = null;

	try {
		await localRepo.injectDemoSeed();
		if (navigator.onLine) {
			try {
				await $fetch('/api/backup/demo-seed', {
					method: 'POST',
					body: { action: 'inject' }
				});
			} catch {
				// در حالت آفلاین یا عدم دسترسی، در Dexie محلی تزریق شده است
			}
		}
		demoSeedMessage.value = {
			type: 'success',
			text: 'داده‌های آزمایشی (Seed) با موفقیت اضافه شدند. اکنون می‌توانید تمام بخش‌ها و نمودارها را بررسی کنید.'
		};
	} catch {
		demoSeedMessage.value = {
			type: 'error',
			text: 'خطا در افزودن داده‌های آزمایشی'
		};
	} finally {
		isMutatingDemoSeed.value = false;
	}
}

async function handleRemoveDemoSeed() {
	if (isMutatingDemoSeed.value) return;
	isMutatingDemoSeed.value = true;
	demoSeedMessage.value = null;

	try {
		await localRepo.removeDemoSeed();
		if (navigator.onLine) {
			try {
				await $fetch('/api/backup/demo-seed', {
					method: 'POST',
					body: { action: 'remove' }
				});
			} catch {
				// در حالت آفلاین یا عدم دسترسی، از Dexie محلی حذف شده است
			}
		}
		demoSeedMessage.value = {
			type: 'success',
			text: 'تمام داده‌های آزمایشی به طور کامل و بدون دست زدن به داده‌های شخصی شما حذف شدند.'
		};
	} catch {
		demoSeedMessage.value = {
			type: 'error',
			text: 'خطا در حذف داده‌های آزمایشی'
		};
	} finally {
		isMutatingDemoSeed.value = false;
	}
}

async function exportLocalBackup() {
	isExporting.value = true;
	try {
		const backup = await localRepo.exportBackupPayload();

		const blob = new Blob([JSON.stringify(backup, null, 2)], { type: 'application/json' });
		const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
		const a = document.createElement('a');
		a.href = url;
		a.download = `new-life-backup-${backup.jalaliDate.replace(/\//g, '-')}.json`;
		document.body.appendChild(a);
		a.click();
		document.body.removeChild(a);
		URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
	} finally {
		isExporting.value = false;
	}
}

async function handleFileSelected(e: Event) {
	const input = e.target as HTMLInputElement;
	selectedFile.value = input.files?.[0] ?? null;
}

async function importBackup() {
	if (!selectedFile.value || isImporting.value) return;
	isImporting.value = true;
	importMessage.value = null;

	try {
		const text = await selectedFile.value.text();
		const json = JSON.parse(text);
		const parsed = DatabaseBackupPayloadSchema.safeParse(json);
		if (!parsed.success) {
			importMessage.value = {
				type: 'error',
				text: `فایل بکاپ نامعتبر است: ${parsed.error.issues[0]?.message ?? ''}`
			};
			return;
		}

		const mergeResult = await localRepo.restoreBackupPayload(parsed.data);

		if (navigator.onLine) {
			try {
				await $fetch('/api/backup/import', {
					method: 'POST',
					body: parsed.data
				});
				void syncEngine.syncNow({ checkRemoteUpdates: true });
			} catch {
				// در صورت عدم دسترسی موقت به سرور، داده‌های محلی در Dexie ادغام شده‌اند
			}
		}

		const addedLabel = toPersianDigits(mergeResult.addedOrUpdatedCount);
		const skippedLabel = toPersianDigits(mergeResult.skippedDuplicatesCount);
		importMessage.value = {
			type: 'success',
			text:
				mergeResult.skippedDuplicatesCount > 0
					? `${addedLabel} رکورد جدید اضافه/به‌روزرسانی شد (${skippedLabel} مورد تکراری نادیده گرفته شد).`
					: `${addedLabel} رکورد با موفقیت به داده‌های فعلی اضافه شد.`
		};
		selectedFile.value = null;
	} catch {
		importMessage.value = {
			type: 'error',
			text: 'خواندن فایل با خطا مواجه شد.'
		};
	} finally {
		isImporting.value = false;
	}
}

async function handleClearAllData() {
	if (isClearingAll.value) return;
	isClearingAll.value = true;
	importMessage.value = null;

	try {
		await localRepo.clearLocalData();
		syncEngine.reset();

		if (navigator.onLine) {
			try {
				await $fetch('/api/backup/clear', { method: 'POST' });
			} catch {
				// ignore if offline
			}
		}

		isConfirmingClearAll.value = false;
		importMessage.value = {
			type: 'success',
			text: 'تمام اطلاعات دیتابیس به طور کامل پاک‌سازی شد.'
		};
	} catch {
		importMessage.value = {
			type: 'error',
			text: 'خطا در پاک‌سازی اطلاعات دیتابیس'
		};
	} finally {
		isClearingAll.value = false;
	}
}

async function handleChangePassword() {
	if (newPassword.value !== confirmNewPassword.value) {
		passwordMessage.value = { type: 'error', text: 'رمز عبور جدید و تکرار آن یکسان نیستند' };
		return;
	}
	if (!navigator.onLine) {
		passwordMessage.value = {
			type: 'error',
			text: 'تغییر رمز عبور نیازمند اتصال به اینترنت و ارتباط با سرور است'
		};
		return;
	}
	isChangingPassword.value = true;
	passwordMessage.value = null;

	try {
		await $fetch('/api/auth/change-password', {
			method: 'POST',
			body: {
				newPassword: newPassword.value,
				confirmNewPassword: confirmNewPassword.value
			}
		});

		await clientAuth.saveClientCredential(newPassword.value);
		passwordMessage.value = { type: 'success', text: 'رمز عبور با موفقیت به‌روزرسانی شد.' };
		newPassword.value = '';
		confirmNewPassword.value = '';
	} catch (err: unknown) {
		const statusMessage =
			err && typeof err === 'object' && 'data' in err
				? (err as { data?: { statusMessage?: string } }).data?.statusMessage
				: undefined;
		passwordMessage.value = {
			type: 'error',
			text: statusMessage || (err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'خطا در تغییر رمز')
		};
	} finally {
		isChangingPassword.value = false;
	}
}

async function handleLogout() {
	try {
		if (navigator.onLine) {
			await $fetch('/api/auth/logout', { method: 'POST' });
		}
	} catch {
		// ignore
	}
	syncEngine.reset();
	await clientAuth.logout();
	await localRepo.clearLocalData();
	router.push('/login');
}
</script>

<template>
	<div class="mx-auto max-w-3xl space-y-8 pb-16">
		<div class="flex items-center justify-between rounded-3xl border border-border bg-surface p-5 shadow-(--shadow-soft) sm:px-7">
			<div class="flex items-center gap-3.5">
				<div class="flex h-11 w-11 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl border border-accent-border bg-accent-soft text-accent-text">
					<Settings class="h-5 w-5" />
				</div>
				<h1 class="text-xl font-black text-fg sm:text-2xl">تنظیمات و پشتیبان‌گیری</h1>
			</div>
		</div>

		<!-- بخش ظاهر و پوسته -->
		<section class="space-y-4 rounded-3xl border border-border bg-surface p-6 shadow-(--shadow-soft) sm:p-7">
			<div class="flex items-center gap-2.5 border-b border-border pb-3.5">
				<Palette class="h-5 w-5 text-accent-text" />
				<h2 class="text-base font-black text-fg">ظاهر و پوسته برنامه</h2>
			</div>

			<div class="grid grid-cols-1 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2">
				<button
					type="button"
					class="flex items-center justify-between rounded-2xl border p-4 text-right transition"
					:class="!theme.isDark.value ? 'border-accent bg-accent-soft text-accent-text ring-1 ring-accent-border' : 'border-border bg-elevated text-fg-secondary hover:border-border-strong hover:text-fg'"
					@click="theme.setTheme(false)"
				>
					<div class="flex items-center gap-3">
						<div class="flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-surface">
							<Sun class="h-5 w-5" />
						</div>
						<div class="text-sm font-extrabold">پوسته روشن</div>
					</div>
					<Check v-if="!theme.isDark.value" class="h-4 w-4" />
				</button>

				<button
					type="button"
					class="flex items-center justify-between rounded-2xl border p-4 text-right transition"
					:class="theme.isDark.value ? 'border-accent bg-accent-soft text-accent-text ring-1 ring-accent-border' : 'border-border bg-elevated text-fg-secondary hover:border-border-strong hover:text-fg'"
					@click="theme.setTheme(true)"
				>
					<div class="flex items-center gap-3">
						<div class="flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-surface">
							<Moon class="h-5 w-5" />
						</div>
						<div class="text-sm font-extrabold">پوسته تاریک</div>
					</div>
					<Check v-if="theme.isDark.value" class="h-4 w-4" />
				</button>
			</div>
		</section>

		<!-- بخش پشتیبان‌گیری آفلاین JSON -->
		<section class="space-y-5 rounded-3xl border border-border bg-surface p-6 shadow-(--shadow-soft) sm:p-7">
			<div class="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 border-b border-border pb-3.5">
				<div class="flex items-center gap-2.5">
					<Database class="h-5 w-5 text-accent-text" />
					<h2 class="text-base font-black text-fg">پشتیبان‌گیری از داده‌ها</h2>
				</div>
				<span class="rounded-xl border border-border bg-elevated px-3 py-1 font-mono text-xs font-bold text-fg-secondary">
					{{ toPersianDigits(habits.length) }} طرح • {{ toPersianDigits(entries.length) }} روز ثبت‌شده
				</span>
			</div>

			<div v-if="importMessage" class="rounded-2xl border px-4 py-3 text-xs font-extrabold" :class="importMessage.type === 'success' ? 'border-optimal-border bg-optimal-soft text-optimal' : 'border-failed-border bg-failed-soft text-failed'">
				{{ importMessage.text }}
			</div>

			<div class="grid grid-cols-1 gap-5 md:grid-cols-2">
				<div class="flex flex-col justify-between rounded-2xl border border-border bg-elevated p-5">
					<div class="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-black text-fg">
						<HardDriveDownload class="h-4 w-4 text-accent-text" />
						<span>دریافت فایل پشتیبان</span>
					</div>

					<button
						type="button"
						:disabled="isExporting"
						class="mt-4 flex h-11 w-full items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-accent text-xs font-black text-accent-fg shadow-xs transition hover:opacity-90 active:scale-98 disabled:opacity-60"
						@click="exportLocalBackup"
					>
						<Download class="h-4 w-4" />
						<span>{{ isExporting ? 'در حال آماده‌سازی...' : 'دانلود فایل پشتیبان' }}</span>
					</button>
				</div>

				<div class="flex flex-col justify-between rounded-2xl border border-border bg-elevated p-5">
					<div class="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-black text-fg">
						<HardDriveUpload class="h-4 w-4 text-gold-text" />
						<span>افزودن از فایل پشتیبان</span>
					</div>

					<div class="mt-4 space-y-3">
						<label class="flex h-11 w-full cursor-pointer items-center justify-center rounded-xl border border-dashed border-border bg-surface px-4 text-xs font-bold text-fg-secondary transition hover:border-border-strong hover:text-fg">
							<span class="truncate">{{ selectedFile ? selectedFile.name : 'انتخاب فایل پشتیبان...' }}</span>
							<input type="file" accept=".json,application/json" class="hidden" @change="handleFileSelected" />
						</label>

						<button
							type="button"
							:disabled="!selectedFile || isImporting"
							class="flex h-11 w-full items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl border border-gold-border bg-gold-soft text-xs font-black text-gold-text transition hover:opacity-90 active:scale-98 disabled:opacity-50"
							@click="importBackup"
						>
							<HardDriveUpload class="h-4 w-4" />
							<span>{{ isImporting ? 'در حال بررسی و ادغام...' : 'ادغام داده‌های جدید' }}</span>
						</button>
					</div>
				</div>
			</div>

			<!-- بخش مجزای پاک‌سازی کامل دیتابیس با تایید دو مرحله‌ای -->
			<div class="flex flex-col justify-between gap-3 rounded-2xl border border-border bg-elevated p-4 sm:flex-row sm:items-center">
				<div class="flex items-center gap-2.5 text-xs font-extrabold text-fg-secondary">
					<AlertTriangle class="h-4 w-4 shrink-0 text-failed" />
					<span>پاک‌سازی کامل تمام اطلاعات دیتابیس (طرح‌ها، روزها، کتاب‌ها و یادداشت‌ها)</span>
				</div>

				<div v-if="!isConfirmingClearAll" class="shrink-0">
					<button
						type="button"
						class="flex h-9 items-center justify-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl border border-failed-border bg-failed-soft px-3.5 text-xs font-black text-failed transition hover:opacity-90 active:scale-95"
						@click="isConfirmingClearAll = true"
					>
						<Trash2 class="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
						<span>پاک کردن همه داده‌ها</span>
					</button>
				</div>

				<div v-else class="flex shrink-0 items-center gap-2">
					<button
						type="button"
						class="h-9 rounded-xl border border-border bg-surface px-3 text-xs font-bold text-fg-secondary transition hover:text-fg"
						@click="isConfirmingClearAll = false"
					>
						انصراف
					</button>
					<button
						type="button"
						:disabled="isClearingAll"
						class="flex h-9 items-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl bg-failed px-3.5 text-xs font-black text-on-solid shadow-xs transition hover:opacity-90 active:scale-95 disabled:opacity-50"
						@click="handleClearAllData"
					>
						<Trash2 class="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
						<span>{{ isClearingAll ? 'در حال حذف...' : 'تایید حذف کامل' }}</span>
					</button>
				</div>
			</div>
		</section>

		<!-- بخش داده‌های آزمایشی (Demo Seed) قابل افزودن و حذف ایمن -->
		<section class="space-y-4 rounded-3xl border border-border bg-surface p-6 shadow-(--shadow-soft) sm:p-7">
			<div class="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 border-b border-border pb-3.5">
				<div class="flex items-center gap-2.5">
					<FlaskConical class="h-5 w-5 text-accent-text" />
					<h2 class="text-base font-black text-fg">داده‌های آزمایشی</h2>
				</div>
				<span
					class="rounded-xl border px-3 py-1 text-xs font-extrabold"
					:class="
						activeDemoHabitsCount > 0
							? 'border-gold-border bg-gold-soft text-gold-text'
							: 'border-border bg-elevated text-fg-muted'
					"
				>
					{{
						activeDemoHabitsCount > 0
							? `${toPersianDigits(activeDemoHabitsCount)} طرح آزمایشی فعال`
							: 'بدون داده آزمایشی'
					}}
				</span>
			</div>

			<div
				v-if="demoSeedMessage"
				class="rounded-2xl border px-4 py-3 text-xs font-extrabold"
				:class="
					demoSeedMessage.type === 'success'
						? 'border-optimal-border bg-optimal-soft text-optimal'
						: 'border-failed-border bg-failed-soft text-failed'
				"
			>
				{{ demoSeedMessage.text }}
			</div>

			<div class="grid grid-cols-1 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2">
				<button
					type="button"
					:disabled="isMutatingDemoSeed"
					class="flex h-11 items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl border border-accent-border bg-accent-soft px-4 text-xs font-black text-accent-text transition hover:opacity-90 active:scale-98 disabled:opacity-50"
					@click="handleInjectDemoSeed"
				>
					<Plus class="h-4 w-4" />
					<span>افزودن داده‌های آزمایشی</span>
				</button>

				<button
					type="button"
					:disabled="isMutatingDemoSeed || activeDemoHabitsCount === 0"
					class="flex h-11 items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl border border-failed-border bg-failed-soft px-4 text-xs font-black text-failed transition hover:opacity-90 active:scale-98 disabled:opacity-40"
					@click="handleRemoveDemoSeed"
				>
					<Trash2 class="h-4 w-4" />
					<span>حذف داده‌های آزمایشی</span>
				</button>
			</div>
		</section>

		<!-- بخش امنیت و حساب کاربری -->
		<section class="space-y-5 rounded-3xl border border-border bg-surface p-6 shadow-(--shadow-soft) sm:p-7">
			<div class="flex items-center gap-2.5 border-b border-border pb-3.5">
				<ShieldCheck class="h-5 w-5 text-accent-text" />
				<h2 class="text-base font-black text-fg">امنیت و نشست کاربری</h2>
			</div>

			<div v-if="passwordMessage" class="rounded-xl border px-4 py-2.5 text-xs font-extrabold" :class="passwordMessage.type === 'success' ? 'border-optimal-border bg-optimal-soft text-optimal' : 'border-failed-border bg-failed-soft text-failed'">
				{{ passwordMessage.text }}
			</div>

			<form class="space-y-3.5" @submit.prevent="handleChangePassword">
				<div class="text-xs font-black text-fg">تغییر رمز عبور اصلی</div>

				<div class="grid grid-cols-1 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2">
					<div>
						<label for="new-pass" class="mb-1 block text-xs font-bold text-fg-secondary">رمز جدید</label>
						<input
							id="new-pass"
							v-model="newPassword"
							type="password"
							required
							minlength="4"
							dir="ltr"
							placeholder="حداقل ۴ کاراکتر"
							class="h-11 w-full rounded-xl border border-border bg-elevated px-3 text-center font-mono text-xs font-bold text-fg placeholder:font-sans placeholder:font-normal placeholder:text-fg-muted focus:border-border-strong focus:outline-none"
						/>
					</div>

					<div>
						<label for="conf-pass" class="mb-1 block text-xs font-bold text-fg-secondary">تکرار رمز جدید</label>
						<input
							id="conf-pass"
							v-model="confirmNewPassword"
							type="password"
							required
							minlength="4"
							dir="ltr"
							placeholder="تکرار رمز جدید"
							class="h-11 w-full rounded-xl border border-border bg-elevated px-3 text-center font-mono text-xs font-bold text-fg placeholder:font-sans placeholder:font-normal placeholder:text-fg-muted focus:border-border-strong focus:outline-none"
						/>
					</div>
				</div>

				<div class="flex justify-end pt-1">
					<button
						type="submit"
						:disabled="isChangingPassword"
						class="flex h-10 items-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-accent px-6 text-xs font-black text-accent-fg shadow-xs transition hover:opacity-90 active:scale-98 disabled:opacity-60"
					>
						<KeyRound class="h-4 w-4" />
						<span>{{ isChangingPassword ? 'در حال ثبت...' : 'ذخیره رمز جدید' }}</span>
					</button>
				</div>
			</form>

			<div class="flex items-center justify-end border-t border-border pt-4">
				<button
					type="button"
					class="flex h-10 items-center gap-2 rounded-xl border border-failed-border bg-failed-soft px-4 text-xs font-extrabold text-failed transition hover:opacity-90 active:scale-95"
					@click="handleLogout"
				>
					<LogOut class="h-4 w-4" />
					<span>خروج از حساب</span>
				</button>
			</div>
		</section>
	</div>
</template>

export function getTierBadgeMeta(tier: number | null | undefined): {
	label: string;
	classes: string;
} {
	if (tier === TIER_SCORES.GOLD) {
		return { label: 'طلا', classes: 'border-gold-border bg-gold-soft text-gold-text' };
	}
	if (tier === TIER_SCORES.SILVER) {
		return { label: 'نقره', classes: 'border-silver-border bg-silver-soft text-silver-text' };
	}
	if (tier === TIER_SCORES.BRONZE) {
		return { label: 'برنز', classes: 'border-bronze-border bg-bronze-soft text-bronze-text' };
	}
	if (tier === TIER_SCORES.SKIPPED) {
		return { label: 'پیش نیامد', classes: 'border-border bg-elevated text-fg-secondary' };
	}
	return { label: 'نشد', classes: 'border-failed-border bg-failed-soft text-failed' };
}

export function getOutcomeBadgeMeta(
	outcome: DayHabitOutcome,
	maxSupportedTier: 1 | 2 | 3 = 3
): {
	label: string;
	badgeClass: string;
} {
	if (outcome === 'gold') {
		return {
			label: maxSupportedTier === 1 ? 'انجام کامل' : 'سطح طلا',
			badgeClass: 'border-gold-border bg-gold-soft text-gold-text'
		};
	}
	if (outcome === 'silver') {
		return {
			label: 'سطح نقره',
			badgeClass: 'border-silver-border bg-silver-soft text-silver-text'
		};
	}
	if (outcome === 'bronze') {
		return {
			label: 'سطح برنز (حداقل)',
			badgeClass: 'border-bronze-border bg-bronze-soft text-bronze-text'
		};
	}
	if (outcome === 'failed') {
		return {
			label: 'انجام نشد',
			badgeClass: 'border-failed-border bg-failed-soft text-failed'
		};
	}
	if (outcome === 'skipped') {
		return {
			label: 'پیش نیامد',
			badgeClass: 'border-border bg-elevated text-fg-secondary'
		};
	}
	return {
		label: 'ثبت‌نشده',
		badgeClass: 'border-border bg-elevated text-fg-muted'
	};
}

import * as jalaali from 'jalaali-js';

export interface JalaliParts {
	jy: number;
	jm: number;
	jd: number;
}

const PERSIAN_MONTHS = [
	'فروردین',
	'اردیبهشت',
	'خرداد',
	'تیر',
	'مرداد',
	'شهریور',
	'مهر',
	'آبان',
	'آذر',
	'دی',
	'بهمن',
	'اسفند'
] as const;

export const JALALI_MONTH_NAMES = PERSIAN_MONTHS;

export const JALALI_GRID_WEEKDAYS = [
	'شنبه',
	'یکشنبه',
	'دوشنبه',
	'سه‌شنبه',
	'چهارشنبه',
	'پنجشنبه',
	'جمعه'
] as const;

export const JALALI_GRID_WEEKDAYS_SHORT = [
	'ش',
	'۱ش',
	'۲ش',
	'۳ش',
	'۴ش',
	'۵ش',
	'ج'
] as const;

const TEHRAN_ISO_FORMATTER = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-CA', {
	timeZone: 'Asia/Tehran',
	year: 'numeric',
	month: '2-digit',
	day: '2-digit'
});

export function getPersianWeekdayIndex(date: Date): number {
	return (date.getDay() + 1) % 7;
}

export function padZero(num: number, size = 2): string {
	return String(num).padStart(size, '0');
}

export function normalizeDigits(input: string): string {
	return input
		.replace(/[۰-۹]/g, (d) => String('۰۱۲۳۴۵۶۷۸۹'.indexOf(d)))
		.replace(/[٠-٩]/g, (d) => String('٠١٢٣٤٥٦٧٨٩'.indexOf(d)));
}

export function toPersianDigits(input: string | number): string {
	return String(input)
		.replace(/[0-9]/g, (d) => '۰۱۲۳۴۵۶۷۸۹'[Number(d)] ?? d)
		.replace(/[٠-٩]/g, (d) => '۰۱۲۳۴۵۶۷۸۹'['٠١٢٣٤٥٦٧٨٩'.indexOf(d)] ?? d);
}

export function dateToIso(date: Date): string {
	const y = date.getFullYear();
	const m = padZero(date.getMonth() + 1);
	const d = padZero(date.getDate());
	return `${y}-${m}-${d}`;
}

export function isoToDate(iso: string): Date {
	const [y = 1970, m = 1, d = 1] = iso.split('-').map(Number);
	return new Date(y, (m || 1) - 1, d || 1, 12, 0, 0);
}

export function getTodayIso(): string {
	return TEHRAN_ISO_FORMATTER.format(new Date());
}

export function getYesterdayIso(): string {
	return shiftIsoDate(getTodayIso(), -1);
}

export function shiftIsoDate(iso: string, deltaDays: number): string {
	const dt = isoToDate(iso);
	dt.setDate(dt.getDate() + deltaDays);
	return dateToIso(dt);
}

export function isoToJalaliParts(iso: string): JalaliParts {
	const [gy = 1970, gm = 1, gd = 1] = iso.split('-').map(Number);
	return jalaali.toJalaali(gy, gm, gd);
}

export function jalaliPartsToIso(parts: JalaliParts): string | null {
	if (!jalaali.isValidJalaaliDate(parts.jy, parts.jm, parts.jd)) {
		return null;
	}
	const { gy, gm, gd } = jalaali.toGregorian(parts.jy, parts.jm, parts.jd);
	return `${gy}-${padZero(gm)}-${padZero(gd)}`;
}

export function isoToJalaliSlash(iso: string): string {
	const { jy, jm, jd } = isoToJalaliParts(iso);
	return `${jy}/${padZero(jm)}/${padZero(jd)}`;
}

export function jalaliSlashToIso(jalaliStr: string): string | null {
	const cleaned = normalizeDigits(jalaliStr.trim());
	const match = cleaned.match(/^(\d{4})[/-](\d{1,2})[/-](\d{1,2})$/);
	if (!match) return null;
	const jy = Number(match[1]);
	const jm = Number(match[2]);
	const jd = Number(match[3]);
	return jalaliPartsToIso({ jy, jm, jd });
}

export function resolveCompletedRangeDates(params: {
	startedJalali?: string | null;
	finishedJalali?: string | null;
	isCompleted: boolean;
	todayIso?: string;
}): {
	startedAtIso: string | null;
	finishedAtIso: string | null;
} {
	const todayIso = params.todayIso ?? getTodayIso();
	const startedAtIso = params.startedJalali?.trim()
		? jalaliSlashToIso(params.startedJalali.trim())
		: null;
	const finishedAtIso = params.finishedJalali?.trim()
		? jalaliSlashToIso(params.finishedJalali.trim())
		: params.isCompleted
			? todayIso
			: null;

	return { startedAtIso, finishedAtIso };
}

export function formatJalaliHuman(
	iso: string,
	referenceTodayIso?: string
): {
	weekday: string;
	weekdayShort: string;
	day: number;
	monthName: string;
	year: number;
	fullLabel: string;
	relativeBadge: string | null;
} {
	const dt = isoToDate(iso);
	const weekdayIdx = getPersianWeekdayIndex(dt);
	const weekday = JALALI_GRID_WEEKDAYS[weekdayIdx];
	const weekdayShort = JALALI_GRID_WEEKDAYS_SHORT[weekdayIdx];
	const { jy, jm, jd } = isoToJalaliParts(iso);
	const monthName = PERSIAN_MONTHS[jm - 1] ?? '';

	const todayIso = referenceTodayIso ?? getTodayIso();
	const yesterdayIso = shiftIsoDate(todayIso, -1);
	const tomorrowIso = shiftIsoDate(todayIso, 1);

	let relativeBadge: string | null = null;
	if (iso === yesterdayIso) relativeBadge = 'دیروز';
	else if (iso === todayIso) relativeBadge = 'امروز';
	else if (iso === tomorrowIso) relativeBadge = 'فردا';

	return {
		weekday: weekday ?? '',
		weekdayShort: weekdayShort ?? '',
		day: jd,
		monthName,
		year: jy,
		fullLabel: `${weekday} ${toPersianDigits(jd)} ${monthName} ${toPersianDigits(jy)}`,
		relativeBadge
	};
}

export interface CalendarScrubberDay<TStatus extends string = string> {
	iso: string;
	jd: number;
	weekdayShort: string;
	monthName: string;
	isSelected: boolean;
	visualState: TStatus;
}

export function buildRecentCalendarDays<TStatus extends string>(params: {
	currentDateIso: string;
	todayIso: string;
	savedStatusByIso: Map<string, TStatus>;
	liveStatus: TStatus;
	useLiveStatusForSelected: boolean;
	defaultStatus: TStatus;
	daysCount?: number;
}): CalendarScrubberDay<TStatus>[] {
	const {
		currentDateIso,
		todayIso,
		savedStatusByIso,
		liveStatus,
		useLiveStatusForSelected,
		defaultStatus,
		daysCount = 14
	} = params;

	const anchorIso = currentDateIso > todayIso ? currentDateIso : todayIso;
	const list: CalendarScrubberDay<TStatus>[] = [];

	for (let i = daysCount - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
		const iso = shiftIsoDate(anchorIso, -i);
		const dt = isoToDate(iso);
		const { jm, jd } = isoToJalaliParts(iso);
		const isSelected = iso === currentDateIso;

		let visualState = defaultStatus;
		if (isSelected && useLiveStatusForSelected) {
			visualState = liveStatus;
		} else {
			const saved = savedStatusByIso.get(iso);
			if (saved) visualState = saved;
		}

		list.push({
			iso,
			jd,
			weekdayShort: JALALI_GRID_WEEKDAYS_SHORT[getPersianWeekdayIndex(dt)] ?? '',
			monthName: PERSIAN_MONTHS[jm - 1] ?? '',
			isSelected,
			visualState
		});
	}

	return list;
}

export function getJalaliMonthLength(jy: number, jm: number): number {
	return jalaali.jalaaliMonthLength(jy, jm);
}

export function getDaysUntilIso(fromIso: string, targetIso: string): number {
	const d1 = isoToDate(fromIso);
	const d2 = isoToDate(targetIso);
	return Math.round((d2.getTime() - d1.getTime()) / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24));
}

export interface JalaliMonthGridCell {
	iso: string;
	jy: number;
	jm: number;
	jd: number;
	weekdayLabel: string;
	isToday: boolean;
}

export function buildJalaliMonthGrid(
	jy: number,
	jm: number,
	todayIso = getTodayIso()
): {
	year: number;
	month: number;
	monthName: string;
	leadingEmptyCells: number;
	days: JalaliMonthGridCell[];
} {
	const monthLength = jalaali.jalaaliMonthLength(jy, jm);
	const firstDayIso = jalaliPartsToIso({ jy, jm, jd: 1 }) ?? todayIso;
	const firstDate = isoToDate(firstDayIso);
	const leadingEmptyCells = getPersianWeekdayIndex(firstDate);

	const days: JalaliMonthGridCell[] = [];
	for (let jd = 1; jd <= monthLength; jd++) {
		const iso = jalaliPartsToIso({ jy, jm, jd }) ?? todayIso;
		const weekdayIdx = (leadingEmptyCells + jd - 1) % 7;
		days.push({
			iso,
			jy,
			jm,
			jd,
			weekdayLabel: JALALI_GRID_WEEKDAYS[weekdayIdx] ?? '',
			isToday: iso === todayIso
		});
	}

	return {
		year: jy,
		month: jm,
		monthName: PERSIAN_MONTHS[jm - 1] ?? '',
		leadingEmptyCells,
		days
	};
}

================================================================
PATH: app/domain/tags.ts
================================================================

export function parseTagsJson(rawJson: string | null | undefined): string[] {
	if (!rawJson) return [];
	try {
		const parsed = JSON.parse(rawJson);
		return Array.isArray(parsed)
			? parsed.filter((item): item is string => typeof item === 'string')
			: [];
	} catch {
		return [];
	}
}

import { z } from 'zod';
import {
	CADENCE_DEFAULTS,
	DEFAULT_HABIT_CATEGORY,
	TIER_SCORES
} from './constants';

export const TrackingModeSchema = z.enum(['tiered', 'interval_cadence']);
export type TrackingMode = z.infer<typeof TrackingModeSchema>;

export const AnalyticsTimeRangeSchema = z.enum(['7d', '30d', '90d', 'all']);
export type AnalyticsTimeRange = z.infer<typeof AnalyticsTimeRangeSchema>;

export const HabitStatusSchema = z.enum(['active', 'paused', 'archived']);
export type HabitStatus = z.infer<typeof HabitStatusSchema>;

export const DayStatusColorSchema = z.enum(['optimal', 'partial', 'failed']);
export type DayStatusColor = z.infer<typeof DayStatusColorSchema>;

export const LifecycleEventTypeSchema = z.enum([

export const HabitEntitySchema = z.object({
	id: z.string(),
	parentId: z.string().nullable(),
	title: z.string().min(1),
	category: z.string().default(DEFAULT_HABIT_CATEGORY),
	trackingMode: TrackingModeSchema,
	timeSlot: HabitTimeSlotSchema.default('day'),
	isSituational: z.boolean().default(false),
	status: HabitStatusSchema,
	scheduleLabel: z.string().nullable(),
	notes: z.string().nullable(),
	bronzeDesc: z.string().nullable(),
	silverDesc: z.string().nullable(),
	goldDesc: z.string().nullable(),
	emergencyMinDesc: z.string().nullable(),
	defaultIntervals: z.number().int().min(0),
	defaultMinutesPerInterval: z.number().min(0),
	cadenceWindowMinutes: z.number().int().min(CADENCE_DEFAULTS.MIN_WINDOW_MINUTES),
	sortOrder: z.number().int().default(0),
	createdAtIso: z.string(),
	updatedAtIso: z.string().nullable().optional()
});
export type HabitEntity = z.infer<typeof HabitEntitySchema>;

export const SetupPasswordInputSchema = z
	.object({
		password: z.string().min(4, 'رمز عبور باید حداقل ۴ کاراکتر باشد'),
		confirmPassword: z.string().min(4, 'تکرار رمز عبور الزامی است'),
		website: z.string().optional()
	})
	.refine((data) => data.password === data.confirmPassword, {
		message: 'رمز عبور و تکرار آن یکسان نیستند',
		path: ['confirmPassword']
	});

export const LoginInputSchema = z.object({
	password: z.string().min(1, 'لطفاً رمز عبور را وارد کنید'),
	website: z.string().optional()
});

export const ChangePasswordInputSchema = z
	.object({
		newPassword: z.string().min(4, 'رمز عبور جدید باید حداقل ۴ کاراکتر باشد'),
		confirmNewPassword: z.string().min(4, 'تکرار رمز عبور جدید الزامی است')
	})
	.refine((data) => data.newPassword === data.confirmNewPassword, {
		message: 'رمز عبور جدید و تکرار آن یکسان نیستند',
		path: ['confirmNewPassword']
	});

export const SyncOutboxMutationSchema = z.discriminatedUnion('kind', [
	z.object({
		id: z.string(),
		kind: z.literal('SAVE_DAILY_ENTRY'),
		queuedAtIso: z.string(),
		payload: SaveDailyEntryInputSchema
	}),
	z.object({
		id: z.string(),
		kind: z.literal('SAVE_CADENCE_PLAN'),
		queuedAtIso: z.string(),
		payload: SaveCadencePlanBatchSchema
	}),
	z.object({
		id: z.string(),
		kind: z.literal('UPSERT_BOOK'),
		queuedAtIso: z.string(),
		payload: BookEntitySchema
	}),
	z.object({
		id: z.string(),
		kind: z.literal('DELETE_BOOK'),
		queuedAtIso: z.string(),
		payload: z.object({ bookId: z.string() })
	}),
	z.object({
		id: z.string(),
		kind: z.literal('UPSERT_MEDIA'),
		queuedAtIso: z.string(),
		payload: MediaEntitySchema
	}),
	z.object({
		id: z.string(),
		kind: z.literal('DELETE_MEDIA'),
		queuedAtIso: z.string(),
		payload: z.object({ mediaId: z.string() })
	}),
	z.object({
		id: z.string(),
		kind: z.literal('UPSERT_NOTE'),
		queuedAtIso: z.string(),
		payload: NoteEntitySchema
	}),
	z.object({
		id: z.string(),
		kind: z.literal('DELETE_NOTE'),
		queuedAtIso: z.string(),
		payload: z.object({ noteId: z.string() })
	}),
	z.object({
		id: z.string(),
		kind: z.literal('UPSERT_CALENDAR_EVENT'),
		queuedAtIso: z.string(),
		payload: CalendarEventEntitySchema
	}),
	z.object({
		id: z.string(),
		kind: z.literal('DELETE_CALENDAR_EVENT'),
		queuedAtIso: z.string(),
		payload: z.object({ eventId: z.string() })
	}),
	z.object({
		id: z.string(),
		kind: z.literal('UPSERT_HABIT'),
		queuedAtIso: z.string(),
		payload: HabitEntitySchema
	}),
	z.object({
		id: z.string(),
		kind: z.literal('ADD_LIFECYCLE_EVENT'),
		queuedAtIso: z.string(),
		payload: LifecycleEventEntitySchema
	})
]);
export type SyncOutboxMutation = z.infer<typeof SyncOutboxMutationSchema>;

export const SyncRequestPayloadSchema = z.object({
	clientId: z.string().default('default-client'),
	knownServerVersionIso: z.string().nullable().default(null),
	mutations: z.array(SyncOutboxMutationSchema).default([])
});
export type SyncRequestPayload = z.infer<typeof SyncRequestPayloadSchema>;

	impactDelta: z.number()
});
export type TagCorrelationStat = z.infer<typeof TagCorrelationStatSchema>;

export const HabitComparisonRowSchema = z.object({
	habit: HabitEntitySchema,
	parentTitle: z.string().nullable(),
	isComposite: z.boolean(),
	loggedDays: z.number(),
	successDays: z.number(),
	goldCount: z.number(),
	silverCount: z.number(),
	bronzeCount: z.number(),
	failedCount: z.number(),
	skippedCount: z.number(),
	consistencyRate: z.number(),
	avgQualityScore: z.number(),
	currentStreak: z.number(),
	bestStreak: z.number(),
	trendDelta: z.number(),
	lifecycleChangesCount: z.number()
});
export type HabitComparisonRow = z.infer<typeof HabitComparisonRowSchema>;

export const SelectedHabitAnalyticsSchema = z.object({
	habit: HabitEntitySchema,
	parentTitle: z.string().nullable(),
	isComposite: z.boolean(),
	childrenCount: z.number(),
	maxSupportedTier: z.union([z.literal(1), z.literal(2), z.literal(3)]),
	dailySeries: z.array(HabitDailyPointSchema),
	loggedDaysCount: z.number(),
	successDaysCount: z.number(),
	goldCount: z.number(),
	silverCount: z.number(),
	bronzeCount: z.number(),
	failedCount: z.number(),
	skippedCount: z.number(),
	consistencyRate: z.number(),
	goldRate: z.number(),
	avgQualityScore: z.number(),
	trendDelta: z.number(),
	currentStreak: z.number(),
	bestStreak: z.number(),
	totalActualMinutes: z.number(),
	totalPlannedMinutes: z.number(),
	avgActualMinutes: z.number(),
	avgPlannedMinutes: z.number(),
	weekdayStats: z.array(WeekdayPerformanceStatSchema),
	bestWeekday: WeekdayPerformanceStatSchema.nullable(),
	weakestWeekday: WeekdayPerformanceStatSchema.nullable(),
	tagCorrelations: z.array(TagCorrelationStatSchema),
	evolutionTimeline: z.array(HabitEvolutionItemSchema)
});
export type SelectedHabitAnalytics = z.infer<typeof SelectedHabitAnalyticsSchema>;

export const FullAnalyticsReportSchema = z.object({
	range: AnalyticsTimeRangeSchema,
	startDateIso: z.string(),
	endDateIso: z.string(),
	totalDaysInWindow: z.number(),
	selectableHabits: z.array(
		z.object({
			habit: HabitEntitySchema,
			parentTitle: z.string().nullable(),
			isComposite: z.boolean(),
			displayLabel: z.string()
		})
	),
	macroStats: z.object({
		totalLoggedDays: z.number(),
		optimalDaysCount: z.number(),
		partialDaysCount: z.number(),
		failedDaysCount: z.number(),
		overallConsistencyRate: z.number(),
		totalLifecycleChanges: z.number()
	}),
	selectedHabitReport: SelectedHabitAnalyticsSchema.nullable(),
	comparisonRows: z.array(HabitComparisonRowSchema)
});
export type FullAnalyticsReport = z.infer<typeof FullAnalyticsReportSchema>;

export const DatabaseBackupPayloadSchema = z.object({
	version: z.literal(1),
	appName: z.literal('new-life'),
	exportedAtIso: z.string(),
	jalaliDate: z.string(),
	data: z.object({
		habits: z.array(HabitEntitySchema),
		dailyEntries: z.array(
			z.object({
				id: z.string(),
				dateIso: z.string(),
				dayStatus: DayStatusColorSchema,
				reflectionNote: z.string(),
				journalText: z.string().nullable(),
				tagsJson: z.string(),
				updatedAtIso: z.string()
			})
		),
		habitLogs: z.array(
			z.object({
				id: z.string(),
				dateIso: z.string(),
				habitId: z.string(),
				habitTitleSnapshot: z.string().nullable(),
				achievedTier: z.number().nullable(),
				achievedRuleSnapshot: z.string().nullable(),
				habitSnapshotJson: z.string().nullable(),
				completedIntervals: z.number().nullable(),
				actualMinutesPerInterval: z.number().nullable(),
				totalActualMinutes: z.number().nullable(),
				plannedIntervalsSnapshot: z.number().nullable(),
				plannedMinutesSnapshot: z.number().nullable(),
				updatedAtIso: z.string().nullable()
			})
		),
		cadencePlans: z.array(CadencePlanEntitySchema),
		books: z.array(BookEntitySchema),
		media: z.array(MediaEntitySchema),
		notes: z.array(NoteEntitySchema),
		calendarEvents: z.array(CalendarEventEntitySchema),
		lifecycleEvents: z.array(LifecycleEventEntitySchema),
		reflectionTags: z.array(
			z.object({
				id: z.string(),
				label: z.string(),
				sortOrder: z.number()
			})
		)
	})
});
export type DatabaseBackupPayload = z.infer<typeof DatabaseBackupPayloadSchema>;

import { getTodayIso, isoToJalaliParts, isoToJalaliSlash, shiftIsoDate } from '~/domain/calendar';

export const DEMO_ID_PREFIX = 'demo:';
export const DEMO_ENTRY_TAG = 'داده_آزمایشی';

export function isDemoRecordId(id: string | null | undefined): boolean {
	return Boolean(id && id.startsWith(DEMO_ID_PREFIX));
}

export function buildDemoSeedDataset(referenceTodayIso = getTodayIso()): DatabaseBackupPayload {
	const nowIso = new Date().toISOString();

				habitSnapshotJson: serializeHabitSpecSnapshot(habitMap.get('demo:habit-sub-review')!),

	const books: BookEntity[] = [

	const notes: NoteEntity[] = [

	const tomorrowIso = shiftIsoDate(referenceTodayIso, 1);
	const tomorrowParts = isoToJalaliParts(tomorrowIso);

	const calendarEvents: CalendarEventEntity[] = [

================================================================
PATH: app/plugins/persian-digits.client.ts
================================================================

import { toPersianDigits } from '~/domain/calendar';

export default defineNuxtPlugin((nuxtApp) => {
	nuxtApp.vueApp.directive('persian-digits', {
		mounted(el: HTMLInputElement | HTMLTextAreaElement) {
			const handleInput = () => {
				const start = el.selectionStart;
				const end = el.selectionEnd;
				const original = el.value;
				const converted = toPersianDigits(original);

				if (original !== converted) {
					el.value = converted;
					if (start !== null && end !== null) {
						el.setSelectionRange(start, end);
					}
					el.dispatchEvent(new Event('input', { bubbles: true }));
				}
			};

			(el as unknown as Record<string, unknown>)._persianHandler = handleInput;
			el.addEventListener('input', handleInput);
		},
		unmounted(el: HTMLInputElement | HTMLTextAreaElement) {
			const handler = (el as unknown as Record<string, unknown>)._persianHandler as ((e: Event) => void) | undefined;
			if (handler) {
				el.removeEventListener('input', handler);
			}
		}
	});
});


----------------------------------------------------------------

================================================================
PATH: app/middleware/auth.global.ts
================================================================

import { defineNuxtRouteMiddleware, navigateTo } from '#app';
import { useClientAuth } from '~/composables/useClientAuth';

export default defineNuxtRouteMiddleware(async (to) => {
	if (import.meta.server) return;

	const clientAuth = useClientAuth();
	const isLoginPage = to.path.startsWith('/login');

	const authState = await clientAuth.ensureSessionChecked();

	if (!authState.isAuthenticated && !isLoginPage) {
		const redirectTarget = to.fullPath;
		const query = redirectTarget && redirectTarget !== '/' ? { redirectTo: redirectTarget } : undefined;
		return navigateTo({ path: '/login', query });
	}

	if (authState.isAuthenticated && isLoginPage) {
		return navigateTo('/');
	}
});
